---
name: multi-layer-congestion-betting-audit
description: Multi-layer congestion and cell filtering engine for football betting slips. Use for auditing Double Chance 12, Multi Goals 1-3, Asian Handicap, and goal markets across multi-day fixture pools. Triggers on ditch slips, congestion analysis, spoiler detection, Multi Goals 1-3 audit, or requests for strict high-confidence betting slips from temporal windows.
---

# Multi-Layer Congestion & Cell Filtering Engine

Act as quantitative sports data modeler. Expand selection across 3-to-5 day temporal window. Cherry-pick only high-confidence setups. Never force single-day picks.

## Core Process

1. Map fixtures into Day cells (today through +3/+4). Filter unstable/low-margin first.
2. For each match compute Congestion Differential:
   - Rest Window (hours/days since last fixture for each side)
   - Density Index (games in last 14 days)
   - Travel load + upcoming high-priority fixtures (midweek Europe/cup)
3. Match congestion dynamic to natural market (do not force market type):
   - Asymmetric Fatigue (rested powerhouse vs exhausted) → Team performance (Result, AH, Team Over)
   - Double Fatigue / Defensive Collapse → Goal markets (Over 1.5/2.5, BTTS Yes)
   - Tactical Fatigue / Rotation → Conservative (Under, Double Chance, FH markets)
4. Output only isolated Good Eggs with Date, Fixture, Congestion Dynamic, Recommended Market + exact reasoning.

## Double Chance 12 Spoiler Audit (strict 4-gate)

Reject any match that fails any gate:

- Tier 1 Market Signal: AH main line 0.0 or ±0.25, or Total Goals Under 2.0/2.25 at short odds, or 1X2 Draw priced ≤3.00 → EXCLUDE
- Tier 2 xG / Dixon-Coles: combined λ_total ≤ 2.20 or Dixon-Coles P(Draw) > 26% → EXCLUDE
- Tier 3 Biscotto: mutual incentive for draw (group stage advance or relegation safety on 1 pt) → EXCLUDE
- Tier 4 League/Tactical: league historical draw rate >30%, key offensive absences, or heavy congestion forcing low-block → EXCLUDE

Only keep matches with AH ≤ -0.75 or ≥ +0.75, Over 2.50+, combined xG ≥ 2.50, low Dixon-Coles draw, and clear competitive incentive.

## Multi Goals 1-3 Audit (both tails)

Success = 1 ≤ X ≤ 3. Ruin = X=0 or X≥4.

- Optimal λ window 1.40–1.85 (peak ~1.60). Reject λ < 1.10 (shutout) or λ > 2.20 (blowout).
- Market line screen: Asian Total between Over 2.25 and Over 2.75. Reject Under 1.75 or Over 3.25.
- Personnel: reject if primary goal contributor (>25% team xG/xA) + creator both absent, or backup GK + key CB out.
- Situational: reject confirmed Biscotto or extreme weather (standing water / wind >25 mph).
- Combined ruin tail after Dixon-Coles must stay < 28%.

## Output Format for Slips

Produce 4 independent slips (A/B/C/D). Each slip is a basket of ditched legs that survived every gate. Prefer 4–7 legs per slip. Mix markets only when congestion dictates. State for every leg:

- Date / Day Cell
- Fixture
- Congestion Dynamic + rest differential
- Exact market + selection
- Why it passed all gates (AH line, xG sum, Dixon-Coles, personnel, incentive)

Never include experimental or borderline matches. Delete any feature that fails a gate. Treat as zero-loss strict filter.

---
name: universal-football-intelligence
description: Ultimate quantitative football modelling skill with full Pirates Filter, multi-agent research/model/audit pipeline, Dixon-Coles Poisson grids, market-specific evaluators, and strict spoiler-free slip construction. Use for any football betting analysis, slip building, Multi Goals 1-3, Double Chance, correct-score, value hunting, congestion audits, or requests to run the Universal Football Intelligence model / Pirates Filter / elite slips that never leave a spoiler on the board.
---

# Universal Football Intelligence — Ultimate Model

Act as three cooperating elite agents at absolute maximum capability:

- AGENT 1 RESEARCH (Anita): gathers only fresh, tagged data.
- AGENT 2 MODEL: runs independent system profiling, 100-component check, Poisson/Dixon-Coles maths, fair odds and Kelly.
- AGENT 3 AUDITOR: attacks every leg, removes traps and weak selections, enforces market-specific Pirates Filter rules.

Never invent data. Tag every number FETCHED / APP OUTPUT / ESTIMATED. Never favour big clubs or home sides. Model is blind to bookmaker odds until final value comparison. Report honest joint probabilities. Prefer discarding a leg over forcing it.

## Hard Operating Rules

1. Treat the Universal Football Intelligence app strictly as a user tool. Use every available feature (fixture discovery, team profiles, xG/Dixon-Coles fit, correct-score grid, multigoal tools, slip audit, trap removal, Kelly staking, Anita research worker, source integrations). If a feature is unavailable, state it and use the closest rigorous workaround. Do not edit app code.
2. Reject any fixture with unconfirmed lineups, heavy rotation risk, dead-rubber, bad weather/pitch, unreliable data or referee volatility.
3. Produce only high-confidence, low-entropy selections. A spoiler left on the board is a failure.
4. Output must be better than any prior Brighton-style or CEO-model baseline: stricter filters, full market-aware Pirates engine, explicit failure-scenario analysis, diversified 4-slip construction.

## Core Pipeline (execute fully before any reply)

### STEP 0 — Discover & Filter
- Pull all fixtures for the requested date (default today) across senior + U21/youth pools.
- Apply hard rejection filters. Shortlist ≥20 candidates → final 12 main fixtures.

### STEP 1 — Anita Research (per fixture)
Fresh data only from SofaScore, FotMob, Transfermarkt, SportyBet and any available connectors:
form, home/away splits, rolling xG/PSxG, confirmed/likely lineups, injuries, suspensions, rest/travel/congestion, set-piece takers, referee tendencies, weather, motivation, H2H, statistical twin match.

### STEP 2 — Independent Team System Profiling
Treat each team as an isolated chaotic dynamical system. Map operational floor/ceiling, attack/defence vectors, behaviour when behind, free-will/chaos tendencies. Emit a standalone system-output vector for each side. No matching yet.

### STEP 3 — 100-Component Check
Score across five pillars: Tactical/spatial control, Statistical/xG, Personnel/matchups, Context/environment, Game-state/variance. Output predictability score. Prefer low-entropy matches.

### STEP 4 — 9-Layer Pirates Filter (market-aware)
Run the full universal Pirates Filter on every candidate market. See references/pirates-filter.md for the complete preserved layers, LNFI numerical rules, additional filters, market-specific evaluators, Up-Is-Down failure scenarios, decision taxonomy (PASS / REJECT / PENDING / INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE), and the governing implementation rule.

### STEP 5 — 5-Layer Flow Engine
Weather, referee, minute-by-minute temporal board, red-card/correct-score pivot, no-bookmaker-odds check.

### STEP 6 — The Maths
- Independent Dixon-Coles / Poisson λ for each team.
- Partial-correlation adjusted scoreline grid.
- Derive top correct scores, 1X2, O/U, BTTS, Home/Away Multigoals (sum Poisson range probs), Double Chance.
- Always surface P(0) and P(4+) for each team.
- Fair odds = 1/p. Edge vs SportyBet only after margin. Fractional Kelly.
- For elite-dominant-home vs passive-low-block, also provide Dutching correct-score window.

### STEP 7 — Market Mixing
Choose the single best market per fixture:
- Tier 1 anchors: Match Over 1.5 (open/leaky games)
- Tier 2 controlled range: Home/Away Multigoals 1-3 or 1-4 (teams averaging ≥1.2 who rarely blank)
- Tier 3 blank insurance: Double Chance or BTTS for tight/derby/low-scoring risk
- U21: max 1-2 per slip, only when Over 2.5 heavily favoured + stable lineups + recent Over 1.5 form
Reject any leg whose 0-goal or 4+ tail is excessive.

### STEP 8 — Audit (Agent 3)
Attack every leg. What kills it? Remove traps, eye-candy longshots, low-odds inflated-margin legs. Replace with best alternative from same fixture or drop. Be ruthless.

### STEP 9 — Final Output
Deliver exactly 4 DIFFERENT 12-leg slips drawn from the same 12-fixture pool. Slips must differ in market choice and risk ordering so they do not share single points of failure.
For each slip report:
- Per leg: fixture, market, model probability, fair odds, SportyBet odds, edge, confidence
- Combined odds + true joint probability (correlation-adjusted, stated honestly)
- Weakest leg and why
- Suggested fractional-Kelly stake
Also produce a coverage table showing which single-leg failures each slip survives and how the four slips diversify.
Never claim a guaranteed winner. Only suggest Cut-1/Cut-2 if the survivable payouts beat total stake; otherwise state it is not worth it.

## Final Check Before Any Reply
List every app feature and connector actually used, every feature that could not be used and why, and every material assumption. Only then emit the answer, and only when confidence is high. Discard any selection that does not pass.

## Resource Map
- Full Pirates Filter specification (all 9 layers, LNFI rules, market evaluators, decision system): references/pirates-filter.md
- Existing multi-layer congestion rules (compatible overlay): load the multi-layer-congestion-betting-audit skill when congestion or Multi Goals 1-3 audit is primary.
- **FULL GITHUB APP CONTENT** (authoritative source of the real Universal Football Intelligence model): references/github-app/
  - ufi-model-brain.md (core operating doctrine – MUST follow)
  - Additional full files from the GitHub repo are being added file-by-file (ai-council.js, audit modules, exact-score engines, crainee modules, etc.). Treat everything in references/github-app/ as part of the complete model. Do not skip or weaken any rule from the GitHub app content. Use the full app logic on every fixture.

On every fixture, the source of truth is the full tree, not a summary of it:

- Execute node-exported engines: `crainee-chaos-gate.js`, `crainee-tactical-surprise-engine.js`, `crainee-background-break-engine.js`, `crainee-unconventional-outcome-engine.js`, `crainee-subconscious-narrative-engine.js`.
- Execute browser engines by loading them (`calculator-risk-engine.js` exposes `window.UFICalculatorRisk`; `audit-intelligence.js` exposes `window.UFI400Audit`). A 400-signal row that returns NEEDS_DATA is not a pass.
- `app.js`, `exact-score-intelligence.js`, `ai-council.js`, `index.html`, every doc under `docs/`, every test, and `research/ufi-model-brain.md` are in force. UI files are still rules: do not drop a gate because the file touches the DOM.
- Runner that loads the copied engines and the Dixon-Coles fit: `references/runner/ditch-audit.mjs`.
- Do not invent a SportyBet price. Fair odds come from the fit. A fetched price below fair is not value.
- Delete a failed leg. Do not replace it with a weaker market to keep a round number of legs or a 17.0 total.

# Pirates Filter — Universal Market Engine

Preserve every existing filter, threshold, named layer and established behaviour. Add a market-aware evaluation interface around the existing logic. Apply each rule in its original intended context. Do not silently remove, weaken or overwrite existing rules. If two rules conflict, report the conflict and resolve it explicitly.

## 1. The Original Nine Layers (preserved)

| # | Layer | Function |
|---|-------|----------|
| 1 | Teague’s Code | Core rules, eligibility conditions and non-negotiable checks |
| 2 | Sumbhajee Politics | Political, institutional, officiating and unusual risks |
| 3 | Sparrow LNFI | Player-level last-ten-start performance across DEF / MID / ATT sectors |
| 4 | Davy Jones’ Heart | Underlying team strength, weaknesses and vulnerability |
| 5 | Liar’s Dice | Challenges misleading statistics, inflated narratives and attractive odds |
| 6 | Locker Illusion | Detects misleading form, false favourites and traps |
| 7 | Mao Kun Map | Tactical matchups, playing patterns and alternative routes |
| 8 | Jack’s Compass | Determines whether evidence supports the specific selection and market |
| 9 | Up Is Down | Reversals, underdog possibilities, unexpected developments |

None of these layers may be deleted when new markets are introduced. Each layer receives a market-specific evaluation method.

## 2. Sparrow LNFI — Original Numerical Rules (preserved)

- Calculate each player’s wins minus losses across last 10 starts.
- Group into DEF, MID, ATT sectors.
- Compute sector totals and compare teams.
- Spine Differential threshold ≥ +15 where the original rule requires it.
- No sector may fall below −3 where the original rule applies.
- Account for returning key players with recency weighting.
- Investigate underdog ability to disrupt the expected outcome.

### Critical Correction — Market Dependence

LNFI thresholds remain intact, but application depends on the market:

- Match winner: apply original Spine Differential and sector requirements as hard rules.
- Team goals / Multigoals: examine the relevant team’s attacking production, opponent defensive vulnerability and probability of the selected goal range. Negative defensive LNFI does not automatically reject a team-goals selection.
- BTTS: evaluate both teams’ scoring and conceding profiles.
- Total goals: evaluate combined scoring distribution.
- Correct score: evaluate individual scoreline probabilities.
- Cards / Corners: use disciplinary or set-piece evidence.

LNFI results remain available as contextual evidence for every market. Market-specific logic decides whether a given LNFI condition is a hard rejection or a warning.

## 3. Original Additional Filters (retained)

| Filter | Investigation |
|--------|---------------|
| xG versus actual goals | Sustainable underlying performance vs over-performance |
| Fixture congestion | Fatigue, rotation, recovery |
| Motivation / dead rubbers | Effort, selection, tactical changes |
| Key defensive absences | Impact of missing CBs or defensive midfielders |
| Rest-gap check | Preserve 48-hour threshold as investigation flag |
| Away-win rate | Preserve 30% threshold in original applicable context |
| Politics and referee risk | Evidence-based only |
| Unbeaten-run illusion | Weak opponents, poor underlying metrics |
| Form / tactics / Asian-line alignment | Consistency between stats and prices |
| Singular motivation | Unusual factors without narrative override |
| Up Is Down | Credible alternative scenarios |
| Live confirmation | Current minute, score, odds, evidence for in-play |

A warning is never automatically a rejection. Hard filters stay hard only in their intended context.

## 4. Shared Engine + Market-Specific Evaluators

Every selection passes through:

1. Correct fixture and team identification
2. Correct market and selection identification
3. Data freshness and source verification
4. Team news / injuries / lineups where relevant
5. Full Pirates Filter layers
6. Relevant risk and anomaly checks
7. Market-specific probability assessment
8. Odds and implied-probability analysis
9. Final eligibility decision and explanation

### Market Evaluators

| Market | Primary evaluation | Key failure scenario |
|--------|--------------------|----------------------|
| 1X2 | Home / Draw / Away probabilities | Underdog win or unexpected draw |
| Double Chance | Probability of the two covered outcomes | The excluded outcome |
| Over/Under | Total-goal distribution | Unexpectedly low or high scoring |
| BTTS | Each team scores | One team blanks |
| Team goals | Goals by selected team | Zero or exceeding range |
| Multigoals 1–3 | P(1 ≤ G ≤ 3) = P1+P2+P3 | P(G=0) + P(G≥4) |
| Correct score | Exact scoreline probability | Competing scorelines |
| Asian Handicap | Goal difference + settlement rules | Pushes / partial results |
| Corners | Expected corners + distribution | Tactical / game-state variation |
| Cards | Expected bookings + sending-off risk | Referee + intensity |

### Selection Mapping Rule

Every selection carries explicit fields:

- Fixture ID, Home team, Away team
- Market type, Selected outcome
- Target team (when applicable)
- Odds, Settlement rules
- Model probability, Relevant filter results
- Final decision

If mapping is ambiguous the engine must STOP and flag the error. AWAY13 means the away team scores 1–3 goals — never the home team.

## 5. Strengthened Up Is Down

For every selection generate realistic failure scenarios and their probabilities.

Example — Multigoals 1–3:

- P(G=0), P(G=1), P(G=2), P(G=3), P(G≥4)
- Success probability = P(1)+P(2)+P(3)
- Failure probability = P(0)+P(≥4)

Record the greatest-risk failure mode and explain it. Extend the same principle to every market.

## 6. Odds vs Probability Consistency

Three distinct questions:

1. Is the selection plausible?
2. How likely is it to win?
3. Does the price offer value?

For decimal odds O the raw break-even is 1/O. Compare model probability against the market price after estimating bookmaker margin from the full outcome set. A selection may pass football analysis yet fail value assessment — report the two decisions separately.

## 7. Universal Decision Taxonomy

| Decision | Meaning |
|----------|---------|
| PASS | Applicable hard rules satisfied; evidence supports further consideration |
| REJECT | Hard rule failed, mapping error, or evidence contradicts required conditions |
| PENDING | Critical information missing, stale or awaiting confirmation |
| INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE | Data cannot support a reliable assessment |

A PASS is never a guarantee. Retain layer-by-layer results, rejection reasons and unresolved risks. Never manufacture high confidence from partial filter passage.

## 8. Governing Implementation Rule

Preserve every existing filter, threshold, calculation, named layer and established behaviour. Add a market-aware evaluation interface around the existing logic. Apply each rule in its original intended context, retain shared risk checks, introduce explicit market-specific evaluators, and verify fixture-to-selection mapping. Do not silently remove, weaken or overwrite existing rules. If two existing rules conflict, report the conflict and resolve it explicitly rather than deleting either rule.

# Binding agreement — ditch matrix that settled 1 Nov 2026

This file is additive. It does not replace the Pirates Filter, the multi-layer congestion gates, or any file under `references/github-app/`.

## What the screen-recording agreement actually was

Four pure multiples. Equal unit on each slip. No system / contingent same-slip doubles. Same match may appear on more than one slip only with a different market, so one scoreline cannot be the only way the session dies.

Market language (SportyBet):

- Double Chance 12 = Home or Away. Dies only on a draw. A favorite loss still wins this market.
- Team Multigoals 1-3 dies if that team scores 0 or 4+.
- Team Multigoals 2-3 dies outside 2 or 3.
- Team Over 1.5 / Over 2.5 is the landslide leg. It survives 4-0 and 5-1.
- Match Under 4.5 dies only on 5+ total goals. It is not a shield against a draw.
- Match Under 3.5 on an open game is banned. See settlement.

## Settlement of the agreed weekend legs (verified FT)

| Fixture | Score | What won | What died |
|---|---|---|---|
| Lazio vs Cagliari | 2-1 | Home team 1-3. DC 12 | — |
| Racing vs Real Madrid | 0-3 | Away team 1-3. Away team 2-3. Under 4.5. DC 12 | — |
| Excelsior vs Twente | 0-9 | DC 12 (not a draw) | Under 3.5 |
| Juventus vs Napoli | 2-0 | DC 12 | — |

Juventus closed 2.20 / 3.30 / 3.40. No-vig draw was about 28.8%, which fails the 26% DC12 gate. It won, and it is still not re-bought. One landed draw-risk is not a licence to buy the next one.

Excelsior Under 3.5 is the spoiler. DC 12 on that same match survived 0-9. Future slips do not replace DC 12 with a tight under on a match that can explode.

## How to apply it on the next window

1. Run every GitHub engine that can execute, and apply every gate in the congestion skill and the Pirates Filter.
2. Delete any leg that fails. A shorter slip is allowed. A padded 17.0 is not.
3. Build four slips with different market jobs:
   - A baseline: team goals 1-3 only where λ is inside 1.35–2.05 and shutout+blowout ruin is under 28%.
   - B draw shield: DC 12 only where congestion-adjusted draw is ≤ 26%. Fetched prices shorter than fair odds are insurance, not value. Say so.
   - C landslide: team over 1.5 or team over 2.5 on the same sides whose 1-3 would die to 4+.
   - D hybrid: team goals 2-3 plus at most one under 4.5 that itself clears P(under 4.5) ≥ 78%, plus DC 12 anchors. Never under 3.5.
4. No two markets on the same fixture inside one slip.
5. One match must leave at least two of the four slips alive.
6. Quote a decimal only if it was fetched. Otherwise quote model fair odds and refuse the leg when the live SportyBet price is below fair.
7. Stake one equal unit per slip. Do not scale a negative-EV anchor with Kelly.

# Automatic data and walk-forward evaluation

- [Automatic data and validation workaround](docs/automatic-data-and-validation-workaround.md) documents the secret-safe backend architecture and provider limitations.
- `tools/ufi_live_data_proxy.py` exposes `/api/health`, `/api/fixtures?date=YYYY-MM-DD`, `/api/live`, and `/api/fixture?id=ID` using a server-side `SPORTMONKS_API_TOKEN`. Deploy it to a trusted HTTPS backend and configure the backend URL in the Exact-score intelligence panel. The provider token must never be entered in the browser or committed to Git.
- The Exact-score intelligence panel now supports fixture/live-feed loading and refreshes the live endpoint every 30 seconds while the app page is open after you connect. It can populate team names from a provider fixture. **It does not yet automatically infer goal rates or claim live probabilities are validated.**
- `tools/exact_score_walkforward.py` evaluates two transparent baselines on historical results using rolling-origin predictions: league-average independent Poisson and rolling team attack/defence rates. Example: `python tools/exact_score_walkforward.py results.csv --min-history 100 --window 10 --out report.json`. Input columns: `date,home_team,away_team,home_goals,away_goals`. The script is results-only; richer xG/player/tactical features require separately sourced data and ablation tests.
- Live provider coverage, xG entitlement, quotas and current pricing depend on the provider plan. Automatic refresh only works when a backend is deployed, a valid secret is configured, and the provider supports the requested competitions. Missing or stale data must remain visibly missing.


- [Live market shock, weather and unseen-match validation](docs/live-market-shock-and-walkforward-backtest.md) documents the new price-movement circuit breaker, Open-Meteo lookup and chronological exact-score evaluation panel.
- The Exact-score panel now refreshes the configured live feed every 10 seconds while connected; backend live cache is 5 seconds. This is provider polling, not a millisecond feed. The new `/api/weather?lat=..&lon=..&date=YYYY-MM-DD&hour=0..23` route returns hourly Open-Meteo weather for supplied stadium coordinates and UTC kickoff hour.
- The market-shock panel compares timestamped 1X2 prices, pauses on large/stale/future-dated changes, and only makes bounded adjustments for fresh modest shifts. It does not automatically know the cause of a price move and must not be treated as a prediction guarantee.
- The historical UI backtest uses prior matches only and compares a rolling team-rate Poisson model with a league-average baseline. It is results-only; player/tactical/live-feed/odds-shock improvements still need timestamped aligned historical data and separate unseen tests.
- The secret-safe proxy exposes `/api/inplay-odds?id=FIXTURE_ID&bookmaker=BOOKMAKER_ID&market=MARKET_ID`; the app can poll a configured provider bookmaker/market every 5 seconds and compare timestamped price snapshots. Sportmonks in-play odds are not available on every plan; a provider bookmaker ID does not automatically mean SportyBet odds.

async function fetchWithTimeout(url,options,ms=25000) {
 const controller=new AbortController();
 const timer=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),ms);
 try { return await fetch(url,{...options,signal:controller.signal}); }
 catch(e) { if(e?.name==='AbortError') throw new Error('Provider request timed out after '+Math.round(ms/1000)+' seconds; trying fallback.'); throw e; }
 finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
}
async function readJson(res) {
 const txt=await res.text(); let data; try{data=JSON.parse(txt)}catch{throw new Error('Non-JSON response (HTTP '+res.status+').')}
 if(!res.ok) throw new Error((data.error?.message||data.message||'HTTP '+res.status).slice(0,240));
 return data;
}
async function gemini(p,prompt) {
 const url='https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/'+encodeURIComponent(p.model)+':generateContent?key='+encodeURIComponent(p.key);
 const data=await readJson(await fetchWithTimeout(url,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({contents:[{role:'user',parts:[{text:prompt}]}],generationConfig:{temperature:0.1,maxOutputTokens:1200,responseMimeType:'application/json'}})}));
 const text=(data.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts||[]).map(x=>x.text||'').join('\n'); if(!text) throw new Error('Empty Gemini response.'); return text;
}
async function openAiCompat(p,url,extraHeaders,prompt) {
 const data=await readJson(await fetchWithTimeout(url,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':'Bearer '+p.key,...extraHeaders},body:JSON.stringify({model:p.model,messages:[{role:'system',content:SYSTEM_RULES},{role:'user',content:prompt}],temperature:0.1,max_tokens:1200})}));
 const text=data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content; if(!text) throw new Error('Empty model response.'); return typeof text==='string'?text:JSON.stringify(text);
}

function parseModel(text) {
 try { const cleaned=String(text).trim().replace(/^\u0060\u0060\u0060(?:json)?/i,'').replace(/\u0060\u0060\u0060$/,'').trim(); const start=cleaned.indexOf('{'), end=cleaned.lastIndexOf('}'); if(start<0||end<start) throw new Error('No JSON object found'); const d=JSON.parse(cleaned.slice(start,end+1)); for(const k of ['traps','missing_evidence','model_limitations','disagreements','questions_to_verify']) if(!Array.isArray(d[k])) d[k]=[]; d.traps=d.traps.map(t=>typeof t==='string'?{selection:'Unspecified leg',concern:t,evidence_level:'unknown',action:'HOLD'}:t).filter(t=>t&&typeof t==='object'); return d; }
 catch { return {overall_status:'HOLD',summary:'Model returned unstructured text; treat as unparsed advisory only.',traps:[],missing_evidence:['Could not parse required JSON output'],model_limitations:[String(text).slice(0,5000)],replacement_readiness:'NONE QUALIFIED unless independently validated',disagreements:[],questions_to_verify:[]}; }
}
function safe(v){return String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));}
function render(result) {
 const lines=[];
 lines.push('<p><b>AI council status:</b> '+safe(result.status)+' · '+result.successfulModels+' model(s) responded · '+result.attemptedModels+' attempted · '+safe(result.generatedAt)+'</p>');
 if(result.fallbackUsed) lines.push('<p><b>Fallback used:</b> '+safe(result.fallbackUsed)+'</p>');
 lines.push('<p class="muted">The council is advisory. Unverified or conflicting claims remain questions to check, not facts. AI never upgrades HOLD to safe or creates a qualified replacement.</p>');
 for(const m of result.models) {
  lines.push('<article class="note-card"><div class="note-meta"><b>'+safe(m.provider)+' · '+safe(m.model)+'</b><span>'+safe(m.status)+'</span></div>');
  if(m.error) lines.push('<p class="amber">'+safe(m.error)+'</p>');
  if(m.text) {
   const d=m.parsed;
   if(d) {
    lines.push('<p><b>Assessment:</b> '+safe(d.overall_status||'HOLD')+' — '+safe(d.summary||'No summary')+'</p>');
    if(d.traps?.length) lines.push('<p><b>Potential traps (verify independently)</b></p><ul>'+d.traps.slice(0,8).map(t=>'<li><b>'+safe(t.selection||'Unspecified leg')+'</b>: '+safe(t.concern||'Concern not specified')+' <small>['+safe(t.evidence_level||'unknown')+']</small> — '+safe(t.action||'HOLD')+'</li>').join('')+'</ul>');
    if(d.missing_evidence?.length) lines.push('<p><b>Missing evidence</b></p><ul>'+d.missing_evidence.slice(0,10).map(x=>'<li>'+safe(x)+'</li>').join('')+'</ul>');
    if(d.disagreements?.length) lines.push('<p><b>Disagreements</b></p><ul>'+d.disagreements.slice(0,6).map(x=>'<li>'+safe(x)+'</li>').join('')+'</ul>');
    if(d.questions_to_verify?.length) lines.push('<p><b>Verify next</b></p><ul>'+d.questions_to_verify.slice(0,8).map(x=>'<li>'+safe(x)+'</li>').join('')+'</ul>');
    lines.push('<details><summary>Full model JSON / limitations</summary><pre>'+safe(JSON.stringify(d,null,2))+'</pre></details>');
   } else lines.push('<pre>'+safe(m.text.slice(0,6000))+'</pre>');
  }
  lines.push('</article>');
 }
 const html=lines.join('');
 if($('aiCouncilOutput')) $('aiCouncilOutput').innerHTML=html;
 if($('aiCouncilScenarioOutput')) $('aiCouncilScenarioOutput').innerHTML=html;
}
async function testConfiguredProviders(){
 const status=$('aiCouncilStatus'),button=$('testAiProviders');
 const configured=providers();
 if(!configured.length){if(status)status.textContent='No provider is configured. Get a key from an official provider console, paste it into the matching key field, and check the model ID.';return;}
 if(button){button.disabled=true;button.textContent='Testing providers…';}
 if(status)status.textContent='Testing configured API keys with a short request. No bet will be changed.';
 const probe='Return exactly this JSON and nothing else: {"status":"ok","purpose":"UFI provider connection test"}.';
 const results=await Promise.all(configured.map(async p=>{
  try{const text=await p.run(p,probe);const parsed=parseModel(text);return {id:p.id,model:p.model,ok:true,detail:parsed.overall_status||parsed.status||'valid response'};}
  catch(e){return {id:p.id,model:p.model,ok:false,detail:String(e?.message||e).slice(0,220)};}
 }));
 const lines=results.map(r=>(r.ok?'✓ ':'✗ ')+r.id+' ('+r.model+'): '+(r.ok?'API responded':'FAILED — '+r.detail));
 if(status)status.textContent=lines.join(' | ');
 if(button){button.disabled=false;button.textContent='Test configured API keys';}
}
async function runCouncil() {
 const status=$('aiCouncilStatus');
 const buttons=[$('runAiCouncil'),$('runAiCouncilScenario')].filter(Boolean);
 if(!window.UFI_AI_CONTEXT) {if(status)status.textContent='UFI context bridge is unavailable. Refresh the page and try again.';return;}
 const configured=providers();
 if(!configured.length){if(status)status.textContent='Add at least one provider key. For three parallel opinions plus automatic fallback, configure four providers.';return;}
 buttons.forEach(b=>{b.disabled=true;b.textContent='AI council running…'});
 if(status)status.textContent='Collecting UFI structures and querying three models in parallel…';
 const ctx=window.UFI_AI_CONTEXT();
 const slipText=$('slipInput')?.value||'';
 const prompt=promptFor(ctx,slipText);
 const first=configured.slice(0,3), backup=configured.slice(3);
 const settled=await Promise.all(first.map(async p=>{try{return {provider:p.id,model:p.model,status:'success',text:await p.run(p,prompt)}}catch(e){return {provider:p.id,model:p.model,status:'failed',error:String(e?.message||e)}}}));
 const models=[...settled];
 let successes=models.filter(m=>m.status==='success').length;
 const fallbackUsed=[];
 for(const p of backup) {
  if(successes>=3) break;
  fallbackUsed.push(p.id);
  try { models.push({provider:p.id,model:p.model,status:'success',text:await p.run(p,prompt)}); successes++; }
  catch(e){models.push({provider:p.id,model:p.model,status:'failed',error:String(e?.message||e)});}
 }
 for(const m of models) if(m.text) m.parsed=parseModel(m.text);
 const overall=successes>=3?'THREE-MODEL REVIEW':successes===2?'TWO-MODEL REVIEW — DEGRADED':successes===1?'SINGLE-MODEL REVIEW — DEGRADED':'AI UNAVAILABLE — USE DETERMINISTIC AUDIT ONLY';
 const result={generatedAt:new Date().toISOString(),status:overall,successfulModels:successes,attemptedModels:models.length,fallbackUsed:fallbackUsed.join(', ')||null,models,guardrail:'AI can add review warnings only. It cannot invent a qualified replacement or override UFI CUT/HOLD gates.'};
 if(window.UFI_ATTACH_AI_COUNCIL) window.UFI_ATTACH_AI_COUNCIL(result);
 render(result);
 if(status) status.textContent=successes>=3?'Three model opinions received. Check disagreements and missing evidence below.':successes?('Fallback completed, but only '+successes+' model(s) responded. function buildPlayerEventProfile(){
 const match=S.selectedMatch;
 if(!match||!S.events.length){notice('playerEventProfileStatus','Load a match and its event data in Real match data first. No profile was generated.','warning');return;}
 const map=new Map(), substitutionTimeline=new Map();
 const matchEnd=Math.max(0,...S.events.map(e=>Number(e.minute)).filter(Number.isFinite));
 const ensure=(name,team)=>{if(!name)return null;const key=team+'||'+name;if(!map.has(key))map.set(key,{player:name,team,match_id:String(match.match_id),match_date:match.match_date||'',competition_id:match.competition?.competition_id??'',events:0,shots:0,goals:0,source_xg:0,xg_events:0,passes:0,completed_passes:0,carries:0,pressures:0,duels:0,interceptions:0,ball_recoveries:0,clearances:0,blocks:0,assists:0,key_passes:0,substitution_events:0,first_minute:null,last_minute:null,lineup_status:'not loaded',positions:'',appearance_status:'unknown',minutes_estimate:null,minutes_method:'unavailable',exposure_confidence:'insufficient evidence',evidence_scope:'single loaded match'});return map.get(key)};
 for(const e of S.events){
  const p=ensure(eventPlayer(e),eventTeam(e));if(!p)continue;
  p.events++;const minute=Number(e.minute);if(Number.isFinite(minute)){p.first_minute=p.first_minute===null?minute:Math.min(p.first_minute,minute);p.last_minute=p.last_minute===null?minute:Math.max(p.last_minute,minute)}
  const type=e.type?.name||'';
  if(type==='Shot'){p.shots++;if(e.shot?.outcome?.name==='Goal')p.goals++;if(Number.isFinite(e.shot?.statsbomb_xg)){p.source_xg+=e.shot.statsbomb_xg;p.xg_events++}if(e.shot?.key_pass_id)p.key_passes++}
  if(type==='Pass'){p.passes++;if(!e.pass?.outcome?.name)p.completed_passes++;if(e.pass?.goal_assist)p.assists++;if(e.pass?.shot_assist)p.key_passes++}
  if(type==='Carry')p.carries++;
  if(type==='Pressure')p.pressures++;
  if(type==='Duel')p.duels++;
  if(type==='Interception')p.interceptions++;
  if(type==='Ball Recovery')p.ball_recoveries++;
  if(type==='Clearance')p.clearances++;
  if(type==='Block')p.blocks++;
  if(type==='Substitution'){
   p.substitution_events++;
   const minute=Number(e.minute), incoming=e.substitution?.replacement?.name;
   if(Number.isFinite(minute)){const outKey=eventTeam(e)+'||'+eventPlayer(e);if(!substitutionTimeline.has(outKey))substitutionTimeline.set(outKey,{});substitutionTimeline.get(outKey).offMinute=minute;
    if(incoming){const inKey=eventTeam(e)+'||'+incoming;if(!substitutionTimeline.has(inKey))substitutionTimeline.set(inKey,{});substitutionTimeline.get(inKey).onMinute=minute;}}
  }
 }
 const lineupPlayers=new Map();
 for(const t of S.lineups||[])for(const lp of t.lineup||[]){
  const team=t.team_name||'', key=team+'||'+(lp.player_name||'');
  lineupPlayers.set(key,lp);
  ensure(lp.player_name,team);
 }
 for(const p of map.values()){
  const lp=lineupPlayers.get(p.team+'||'+p.player), timeline=substitutionTimeline.get(p.team+'||'+p.player)||{};
  if(lp){
   p.lineup_status='listed in loaded lineup';
   p.positions=(lp.positions||[]).map(z=>z.position||'').filter(Boolean).join('; ');
   const starts=(lp.positions||[]).some(z=>/starting\s*(xi|lineup)/i.test(z.start_reason||''));
   const explicitStart=(lp.positions||[]).some(z=>z.start_reason!=null&&String(z.start_reason).trim()!=='');
   if(starts){
    p.appearance_status='starter';
    const endMinute=Number.isFinite(timeline.offMinute)?timeline.offMinute:matchEnd;
    p.minutes_estimate=Math.max(0,endMinute);
    p.minutes_method=Number.isFinite(timeline.offMinute)?'starter to recorded substitution minute':'starter to last recorded match minute';
    p.exposure_confidence=Number.isFinite(timeline.offMinute)?'approximate; substitution recorded':'approximate; no substitution recorded';
   } else if(Number.isFinite(timeline.onMinute)){
    p.appearance_status='substitute appeared';
    p.minutes_estimate=Math.max(0,matchEnd-timeline.onMinute);
    p.minutes_method='recorded substitution minute to last recorded match minute';
    p.exposure_confidence='approximate; clock-minute difference';
   } else if(explicitStart){
    p.appearance_status='listed; appearance not evidenced';
   } else {
    p.appearance_status='lineup listed; starter status unclear';
   }
  } else if(Number.isFinite(timeline.onMinute)){
   p.appearance_status='substitute appeared';
   p.minutes_estimate=Math.max(0,matchEnd-timeline.onMinute);
   p.minutes_method='recorded substitution minute to last recorded match minute';
   p.exposure_confidence='approximate; lineup file missing';
  }
 }
 const rows=[...map.values()].sort((x,y)=>x.team.localeCompare(y.team)||x.player.localeCompare(y.player));
 S.playerEventProfile={match_id:String(match.match_id),match_date:match.match_date||'',match_name:matchName(match),built_at:now(),rows};
 $('playerEventProfileTable').innerHTML=rows.length?'<table><thead><tr><th>Player / team</th><th>Appearance / lineup</th><th>Estimated minutes*</th><th>Events</th><th>Shots</th><th>Goals</th><th>Source xG</th><th>Passes / completed</th><th>Carries</th><th>Pressures</th><th>Duels</th><th>Other actions</th><th>Observed minute range*</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+rows.map(p=>'<tr><td>'+esc(p.player)+'<br><span class="muted">'+esc(p.team)+'</span></td><td>'+esc(p.appearance_status)+' · '+esc(p.lineup_status)+(p.positions?' · '+esc(p.positions):'')+'</td><td>'+(p.minutes_estimate===null?'Unknown':p.minutes_estimate.toFixed(1))+'</td><td>'+p.events+'</td><td>'+p.shots+'</td><td>'+p.goals+'</td><td>'+(p.xg_events?p.source_xg.toFixed(3)+' ('+p.xg_events+' shots)':'Not supplied')+'</td><td>'+p.passes+' / '+p.completed_passes+'</td><td>'+p.carries+'</td><td>'+p.pressures+'</td><td>'+p.duels+'</td><td>I '+p.interceptions+' · R '+p.ball_recoveries+' · C '+p.clearances+' · B '+p.blocks+'</td><td>'+(p.first_minute===null?'—':p.first_minute+'–'+p.last_minute)+'</td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table><p class="muted">*Minutes are rough estimates from lineup start reasons, recorded substitution times and the last event minute; stoppage-time/period-clock details and red cards may make them inaccurate. Never treat these as verified minutes or calculate per-90 rates from them. First/last event times are not minutes played. Pass completion uses absence of a pass outcome as the event-data convention; coverage varies by competition and season.</p>':'<p class="muted">No player-attributed events found in the loaded match.</p>';
 notice('playerEventProfileStatus','Built '+rows.length+' player profiles from '+S.events.length+' loaded events for '+matchName(match)+'. Evidence scope: one match; no footedness or universal style inference.','good');
 addActivity('Built event-based player evidence for '+matchName(match));
 renderPlayerEventAggregate();
}

function savePlayerEventMatch(){
 if(!S.playerEventProfile?.rows?.length){buildPlayerEventProfile();if(!S.playerEventProfile?.rows?.length)return;}
 const profile=S.playerEventProfile, matchId=String(profile.match_id);
 const existing=new Set(S.playerEventMatches.filter(r=>String(r.match_id)===matchId).map(r=>r.team+'||'+r.player));
 const fresh=profile.rows.filter(r=>!existing.has(r.team+'||'+r.player)).map(r=>({...r,source_name:'StatsBomb Open Data',source_url:'https://github.com/statsbomb/open-data',saved_at:now()}));
 if(!fresh.length){notice('playerEventAggregateStatus','This match and its player rows are already saved. No duplicate rows added.','warning');return;}
 S.playerEventMatches.push(...fresh);persist();renderPlayerEventAggregate();
 notice('playerEventAggregateStatus','Saved '+fresh.length+' player rows from '+profile.match_name+'. Load another match and repeat; same match/player/team rows are deduplicated.','good');
 addActivity('Saved '+fresh.length+' player-match evidence rows from '+profile.match_name);toast('Match profiles added to evidence dataset.');
}
function renderPlayerEventAggregate(){
 const rows=S.playerEventMatches||[], map=new Map();
 for(const r of rows){const key=(r.team||'')+'||'+(r.player||'');if(!map.has(key))map.set(key,{player:r.player,team:r.team,matches:new Set(),events:0,shots:0,goals:0,source_xg:0,xg_events:0,passes:0,completed_passes:0,carries:0,pressures:0,duels:0,interceptions:0,ball_recoveries:0,clearances:0,blocks:0,estimated_minutes:0,minutes_estimate_rows:0,appearances:0,first_date:'',last_date:'',match_ids:new Set()});
 const x=map.get(key);x.matches.add(String(r.match_id));x.match_ids.add(String(r.match_id));for(const k of ['events','shots','goals','source_xg','xg_events','passes','completed_passes','carries','pressures','duels','interceptions','ball_recoveries','clearances','blocks'])x[k]+=(Number(r[k])||0);if(r.minutes_estimate!==null&&r.minutes_estimate!==''&&Number.isFinite(Number(r.minutes_estimate))){x.estimated_minutes+=Number(r.minutes_estimate);x.minutes_estimate_rows++}if(['starter','substitute appeared'].includes(r.appearance_status))x.appearances++;
 const date=String(r.match_date||'');if(date&&(!x.first_date||date<x.first_date))x.first_date=date;if(date&&(!x.last_date||date>x.last_date))x.last_date=date;
 }
 const agg=[...map.values()].sort((x,y)=>y.matches.size-x.matches.size||x.player.localeCompare(y.player));
 $('playerEventAggregateTable').innerHTML=agg.length?'<table><thead><tr><th>Player / team</th><th>Matches</th><th>Appeared*</th><th>Est. minutes*</th><th>Events</th><th>Shots</th><th>Goals</th><th>Source xG</th><th>Passes / complete</th><th>Carries</th><th>Pressures</th><th>Duels</th><th>Defensive actions</th><th>Date window</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+agg.map(x=>'<tr><td>'+esc(x.player)+'<br><span class="muted">'+esc(x.team)+'</span></td><td>'+x.matches.size+'</td><td>'+x.appearances+'</td><td>'+(x.minutes_estimate_rows?x.estimated_minutes.toFixed(1)+' (approx.)':'Unknown')+'</td><td>'+x.events+'</td><td>'+x.shots+'</td><td>'+x.goals+'</td><td>'+(x.xg_events?x.source_xg.toFixed(3)+' ('+x.xg_events+' shots)':'Not supplied')+'</td><td>'+x.passes+' / '+x.completed_passes+'</td><td>'+x.carries+'</td><td>'+x.pressures+'</td><td>'+x.duels+'</td><td>I '+x.interceptions+' · R '+x.ball_recoveries+' · C '+x.clearances+' · B '+x.blocks+'</td><td>'+esc(x.first_date||'unknown')+' – '+esc(x.last_date||'unknown')+'</td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table><p class="muted">'+rows.length+' saved player-match rows · '+agg.length+' player/team aggregates. Event counts are totals, not per-90 rates. Appeared count and minute totals are approximate and only populated where lineup/substitution evidence supports an estimate; red cards and detailed match-clock corrections are not applied. Team changes and player identity across clubs need review.</p>':'<p class="muted">No saved match profiles yet. Load a StatsBomb match, build its player profile, then save it here. Repeat across matches to grow the evidence dataset.</p>';
}

function renderPlayers(){const q=($('playerSearch')?.value||'').toLowerCase();const ps=S.players.filter(p=>(p.name+' '+p.team+' '+p.position+' '+p.styles).toLowerCase().includes(q));$('playerTable').innerHTML=ps.length?'<table><thead><tr><th>Player</th><th>Club / role</th><th>Foot</th><th>Weak-foot evidence</th><th>Style tags</th><th>Confidence</th><th>Source</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>'+ps.map(p=>'<tr><td>'+esc(p.name)+'</td><td>'+esc(p.team)+'<br>'+esc(p.position)+'</td><td>'+esc(p.foot)+'</td><td>'+esc(p.weakFoot)+'</td><td>'+esc(p.styles||'Not entered')+'</td><td>'+esc(p.confidence)+'</td><td>'+(p.source?'<a href="'+esc(p.source)+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open source ↗</a>':'Not supplied')+'</td><td><button class="button small" data-edit-player="'+p.id+'">Edit</button> <button class="button danger small" data-delete-player="'+p.id+'">Delete</button></td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table>':'<p class="muted">No matching player profiles. Add one, or change your search.</p>'}
$('playerSearch').addEventListener('input',renderPlayers);
$('playerTable').addEventListener('click',e=>{const edit=e.target.closest('[data-edit-player]'),del=e.target.closest('[data-delete-player]');if(edit){const p=S.players.find(x=>x.id===edit.dataset.editPlayer);if(!p)return;const map={playerId:'id',playerName:'name',playerTeam:'team',playerPosition:'position',playerFoot:'foot',playerWeakFoot:'weakFoot',playerStyles:'styles',playerSource:'source',playerConfidence:'confidence',playerNotes:'notes'};Object.entries(map).forEach(([el,k])=>$(el).value=p[k]||'');go('players');window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'})}if(del){S.players=S.players.filter(x=>x.id!==del.dataset.deletePlayer);persist();renderPlayers();toast('Player profile deleted.')}});
$('saveSub').onclick=()=>{const who=$('subIn').value.trim();if(!who)return toast('Enter the substitute player name.');S.subs.unshift({id:crypto.randomUUID(),match:$('subMatch').value.trim(),inPlayer:who,outPlayer:$('subOut').value.trim(),minute:$('subMinute').value,impact:$('subImpact').value.trim(),date:now()});persist();renderSubs();addActivity('Saved substitution observation for '+who);['subMatch','subIn','subOut','subMinute','subImpact'].forEach(id=>$(id).value='');toast('Substitution observation saved.')};
function renderSubs(){$('subList').innerHTML=S.subs.length?'<table><thead><tr><th>Match</th><th>Minute</th><th>Entering</th><th>Leaving</th><th>Observation</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>'+S.subs.map(x=>'<tr><td>'+esc(x.match||'Unknown')+'</td><td>'+esc(x.minute||'—')+'</td><td>'+esc(x.inPlayer)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.outPlayer||'—')+'</td><td>'+esc(x.impact||'No impact note')+'</td><td><button class="button danger small" data-del-sub="'+x.id+'">Delete</button></td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table>':'<p class="muted">No substitution observations saved.</p>'}
$('subList').addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=e.target.closest('[data-del-sub]');if(!b)return;S.subs=S.subs.filter(x=>x.id!==b.dataset.delSub);persist();renderSubs()});
function poisson(l,k){let p=Math.exp(-l);for(let i=1;i<=k;i++)p*=l/i;return p}
function scenarioCalc(){const h=Number($('lambdaHome').value),a=Number($('lambdaAway').value),cap=Number($('scoreCap').value);if(!Number.isFinite(h)||!Number.isFinite(a)||h<0||a<0||h>12||a>12){notice('scenarioStatus','Expected goals must be between 0 and 12.','warning');return null}let grid=[],mass=0,hw=0,d=0,aw=0,over15=0,over25=0,over35=0,btts=0;for(let i=0;i<=cap;i++)for(let j=0;j<=cap;j++){const p=poisson(h,i)*poisson(a,j);grid.push({h:i,a:j,p});mass+=p;if(i>j)hw+=p;else if(i===j)d+=p;else aw+=p;if(i+j>1)over15+=p;if(i+j>2)over25+=p;if(i+j>3)over35+=p;if(i>0&&j>0)btts+=p}grid.sort((x,y)=>y.p-x.p);const norm=x=>x/mass;const out={home:$('scenarioHomeName').value.trim()||'Home',away:$('scenarioAwayName').value.trim()||'Away',matchId:String(chosenMatch()?.match_id??''),lambdaHome:h,lambdaAway:a,cap,mass,homeWin:norm(hw),draw:norm(d),awayWin:norm(aw),over15:norm(over15),over25:norm(over25),over35:norm(over35),btts:norm(btts),grid,top:grid[0],label:$('scenarioLabel').value.trim(),caveats:$('scenarioCaveats').value.trim(),checks:['checkLineup','checkVenue','checkRecent','checkOpponent','checkMarket','checkUncertainty'].filter(id=>$(id).checked)};S.scenario=out;return out}
$('runScenario').onclick=()=>{const s=scenarioCalc();if(!s)return;[['probHome',s.homeWin],['probDraw',s.draw],['probAway',s.awayWin],['probOver15',s.over15],['probOver25',s.over25],['probOver35',s.over35],['probBtts',s.btts]].forEach(([id,v])=>$(id).textContent=pct(v));$('probTopScore').textContent=s.top.h+'–'+s.top.a;$('scoreGrid').innerHTML='<table><thead><tr><th>Score</th><th>Probability (normalized grid)</th><th>Visual</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+s.grid.slice(0,35).map(x=>'<tr><td>'+esc(s.home)+' '+x.h+'–'+x.a+' '+esc(s.away)+'</td><td>'+pct(x.p/s.mass)+'</td><td><div style="height:7px;background:#243b2b;border-radius:6px;min-width:90px;overflow:hidden"><div style="height:100%;width:'+Math.max(1,x.p/s.grid[0].p*100)+'%;background:#83f3ac"></div></div></td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table>';$('scenarioStatus').textContent='Grid mass represented: '+pct(s.mass)+'. Tail omitted by cap: '+pct(Math.max(0,1-s.mass))+'. Probabilities normalized within grid; baseline assumes independent Poisson goals and does not incorporate all tactical/game-state effects.';addActivity('Calculated scenario for '+s.home+' vs '+s.away);toast('Scenario grid calculated.')};
$('saveForecast').onclick=()=>{const s=S.scenario||scenarioCalc();if(!s)return;$('btMatch').value=s.home+' vs '+s.away;$('btMethod').value='Independent Poisson baseline'+(s.label?' / '+s.label:'');$('btHome').value=(100*s.homeWin).toFixed(2);$('btDraw').value=(100*s.draw).toFixed(2);$('btAway').value=(100*s.awayWin).toFixed(2);$('btNotes').value='λ home='+s.lambdaHome+', away='+s.lambdaAway+'; cap='+s.cap+'; grid mass='+(100*s.mass).toFixed(2)+'%; caveats: '+s.caveats+'; checks completed: '+s.checks.join(', ');go('backtest');toast('Forecast copied to ledger form. Select actual result when known.')};
function normalizeTeamName(value){
 return String(value||'').normalize('NFKD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().replace(/\b(fc|cf|sc|afc|club|football|soccer)\b/g,' ').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,' ').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ');
}
function parseFixtureText(value){
 const text=String(value||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
 const m=text.match(/(.+?)\s+(?:vs?\.?|versus)\s+(.+?)(?=\s+(?:\||[-–—])\s+|$)/i);
 if(!m)return null;
 return {homeText:m[1].trim(),awayText:m[2].trim()};
}
function resolveSlipFixture(row){
 const raw=String(row.rawLine||row.label||'');
 if(!parseFixtureText(raw))return {status:'needs-opponent',matchId:'',matchDate:'',homeTeam:'',awayTeam:'',reason:'No explicit “Team A vs Team B” fixture found. Do not infer the opponent from a single team name.'};
 const whole=' '+normalizeTeamName(raw)+' ';
 const live=(S.liveFixtures||[]).filter(m=>{
  const h=normalizeTeamName(m.homeTeam),a=normalizeTeamName(m.awayTeam);
  return h&&a&&whole.includes(' '+h+' ')&&whole.includes(' '+a+' ');
 });
 const candidates=live.map(m=>({source:m.source||'live-fixture-feed',match_id:m.id||m.matchId,match_date:m.date,home_team:{home_team_name:m.homeTeam},away_team:{away_team_name:m.awayTeam},competition:m.competition,status:m.status,markets:m.markets||[],oddsTimestamp:m.oddsTimestamp||null}));
 const historical=(S.matches||[]).filter(m=>{
  const h=normalizeTeamName(m.home_team?.home_team_name),a=normalizeTeamName(m.away_team?.away_team_name);
  return h&&a&&whole.includes(' '+h+' ')&&whole.includes(' '+a+' ');
 }).map(m=>({...m,__source:'historical-statsbomb'}));
 const all=candidates.length?candidates:historical;
 if(all.length===1){
  const m=all[0],parsed=parseFixtureText(raw),h=normalizeTeamName(m.home_team?.home_team_name);
  const orientation=normalizeTeamName(parsed.homeText).includes(h)?'home-away':(normalizeTeamName(parsed.awayText).includes(h)?'away-home':'verify-orientation');
  return {status:'candidate-found',matchId:String(m.match_id??''),matchDate:String(m.match_date||''),homeTeam:m.home_team?.home_team_name||'',awayTeam:m.away_team?.away_team_name||'',orientation,source:m.source||m.__source||'loaded-fixture-list',competition:m.competition?.name||m.competition||'',fixtureStatus:m.status||'',reason:m.source==='live-fixture-feed'?'Matched to a current/upcoming public fixture feed. Confirm competition, kickoff, exact SportyBet market and odds.':'Matched to a historical StatsBomb fixture only; this does NOT verify a current/upcoming match.'};
 }
 if(all.length>1)return {status:'ambiguous',matchId:'',matchDate:'',homeTeam:'',awayTeam:'',reason:'Multiple fixtures match these team names. Add the date/competition and resolve manually.'};
 return {status:(S.liveFixtures.length||S.matches.length)?'not-found-in-loaded-list':'no-fixtures-loaded',matchId:'',matchDate:'',homeTeam:'',awayTeam:'',reason:(S.liveFixtures.length||S.matches.length)?'No matching fixture found in the current feed or loaded historical list.':'Load current fixtures before fixture matching.'};
}
function runSlipFixtureMatching(){
 const rows=S.manualSelections.map(row=>{const fixtureResolution=resolveSlipFixture(row);const sameFixture=String(row.fixtureResolution?.matchId||'')===String(fixtureResolution.matchId||'')&&row.fixtureResolution?.status===fixtureResolution.status;return {...row,fixtureResolution,marketEstimate:sameFixture?row.marketEstimate:null};});
 S.manualSelections=rows;
 renderManualSelections();
 const counts={};rows.forEach(r=>{const k=r.fixtureResolution.status;counts[k]=(counts[k]||0)+1});
 const labels={'candidate-found':'Candidate found','needs-opponent':'Opponent missing','ambiguous':'Ambiguous','not-found-in-loaded-list':'Not found in loaded list','no-fixtures-loaded':'No fixtures loaded'};
 $('fixtureMatchOutput').innerHTML='<p><b>Fixture matching audit</b></p><p>Rows checked: '+rows.length+' · '+Object.entries(counts).map(([k,v])=>esc(labels[k]||k)+': '+v).join(' · ')+'</p>'+(rows.length?'<div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Selection</th><th>Status</th><th>Candidate fixture</th><th>Date</th><th>Orientation</th><th>Reason / next action</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+rows.map(r=>{const f=r.fixtureResolution;return '<tr><td>'+esc(r.label)+'</td><td>'+esc(labels[f.status]||f.status)+'</td><td>'+esc(f.homeTeam&&f.awayTeam?f.homeTeam+' vs '+f.awayTeam:'—')+'</td><td>'+esc(f.matchDate||'—')+'</td><td>'+esc(f.orientation||'—')+'</td><td>'+esc(f.reason)+'</td></tr>'}).join('')+'</tbody></table></div>':'<p>No selections to match. Analyze a slip first.</p>')+'<p class="amber">A candidate is not a verified bookmaker fixture mapping. The loaded StatsBomb list covers selected competitions/seasons only; competition, exact date, kickoff and market settlement rules still need human confirmation.</p>';
 addActivity('Ran fixture candidate matching for '+rows.length+' slip selections');
}
function estimateSlipMarket(row,scenario){
 const text=String(row.rawLine||row.label||'').toLowerCase();
 const normalized=normalizeTeamName(text);
 const fixture=row.fixtureResolution;
 if(!fixture||fixture.status!=='candidate-found')return {status:'blocked',reason:'Resolve a unique fixture candidate first.'};
 if(fixture.source==='live-fixture-feed')return {status:'blocked',reason:'The historical StatsBomb scenario cannot be reused for a current fixture. A current-match model/data source is required.'};
 if(!scenario.matchId||String(scenario.matchId)!==String(fixture.matchId))return {status:'blocked',reason:'Scenario fixture ID does not match this exact fixture. Recalculate the scenario for the matched fixture.'};
 const same=(x,y)=>normalizeTeamName(x)===normalizeTeamName(y);
 if(!(same(fixture.homeTeam,scenario.home)&&same(fixture.awayTeam,scenario.away)))return {status:'blocked',reason:'Scenario home/away teams must match the candidate fixture in the same orientation. Set the scenario teams exactly as listed and rerun it.'};
 const odds=Number(row.odd);
 if(!Number.isFinite(odds)||odds<=1)return {status:'blocked',reason:'Valid decimal odds are required before comparing prices.'};
 const m=text.match(/\b(over|under)\s*(1\.5|2\.5|3\.5)\b/);
 let p=null,market='';
 if(/\bbtts\b|both teams to score|both teams score/.test(text)){p=scenario.btts;market='Both teams to score — YES';}
 else if(m){const line=Number(m[2]),over=scenario.grid.reduce((sum,g)=>sum+((g.h+g.a>line)?g.p:0),0)/scenario.mass;p=m[1]==='over'?over:1-over;market=(m[1]==='over'?'Over ':'Under ')+line+' goals';}
 else if(/\b(home win|home to win|home team to win|match result home|\b1x2\b)/.test(text)||normalized.includes(normalizeTeamName(scenario.home)+' win')||normalized.includes(normalizeTeamName(scenario.home)+' to win')){p=scenario.homeWin;market='Home win';}
 else if(/\b(draw|tie)\b/.test(text)){p=scenario.draw;market='Draw';}
 else if(/\b(away win|away to win|away team to win|match result away)\b/.test(text)||normalized.includes(normalizeTeamName(scenario.away)+' win')||normalized.includes(normalizeTeamName(scenario.away)+' to win')){p=scenario.awayWin;market='Away win';}
 else if(/\b1x\b|home or draw/.test(text)){p=scenario.homeWin+scenario.draw;market='Home or draw';}
 else if(/\bx2\b|draw or away/.test(text)){p=scenario.draw+scenario.awayWin;market='Draw or away';}
 else if(/\b12\b|home or away|no draw/.test(text)){p=scenario.homeWin+scenario.awayWin;market='Home or away';}
 else return {status:'unsupported',reason:'Market/selection wording is not specific enough for a safe probability mapping.'};
 p=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,p));
 return {status:'estimated',market,probability:p,implied:1/odds,ev:p*odds-1,scenarioLabel:scenario.label||'Independent Poisson baseline',reason:'Research estimate from the current independent-Poisson score grid only; verify selection, fixture and market settlement.'};
}
function runSlipMarketBridge(){
 const scenario=S.scenario;
 if(!scenario){notice('marketBridgeOutput','Calculate a scoreline scenario first. No market estimates were produced.','warning');return;}
 const rows=S.manualSelections.map(row=>({...row,marketEstimate:estimateSlipMarket(row,scenario)}));
 S.manualSelections=rows;renderManualSelections();
 const estimated=rows.filter(r=>r.marketEstimate.status==='estimated').length;
 $('marketBridgeOutput').innerHTML='<p><b>Scenario-to-market comparison</b></p><p>Scenario: '+esc(scenario.home)+' vs '+esc(scenario.away)+' · λ '+scenario.lambdaHome.toFixed(2)+' / '+scenario.lambdaAway.toFixed(2)+' · mapped estimates: '+estimated+'/'+rows.length+'</p>'+(rows.length?'<div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Selection</th><th>Market mapping</th><th>Model estimate</th><th>Odds-implied</th><th>Estimated EV</th><th>Status / caveat</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+rows.map(r=>{const e=r.marketEstimate||{},f=r.fixtureResolution||{};return '<tr><td>'+esc(r.label)+'</td><td>'+esc(e.market||'—')+'</td><td>'+(e.probability==null?'—':pct(e.probability))+'</td><td>'+(e.implied==null?'—':pct(e.implied))+'</td><td>'+(e.ev==null?'—':(e.ev*100).toFixed(1)+'%')+'</td><td>'+esc(e.reason||'Not evaluated')+' Fixture: '+esc(f.status||'not matched')+'</td></tr>'}).join('')+'</tbody></table></div>':'<p>Analyze a slip first.</p>')+'<p class="amber">These are experimental model comparisons, not betting recommendations. Only explicit, supported markets are mapped. A positive estimated EV is not evidence of a real edge until probabilities are calibrated out of sample and the exact bookmaker market is verified. This simple Poisson model assumes independent team goals and does not model bet correlations.</p>';
 addActivity('Compared '+rows.length+' slip selections against current scenario; '+estimated+' mapped');
}
function classifySlipMarket(line){
  const s=line.toLowerCase();
  if(/both teams to score|both teams score|\bbtts\b/.test(s))return 'Both teams to score';
  if(/double chance|\b1x\b|\bx2\b|\b12\b/.test(s))return 'Double chance';
  if(/over\s*\d|under\s*\d|total goals|goals over|goals under/.test(s))return 'Goals total';
  if(/\bcorner|corners\b/.test(s))return 'Corners';
  if(/\byellow cards?\b|\bred cards?\b|cards over|cards under/.test(s))return 'Cards';
  if(/\b(draw|home win|away win|to win|win in 90|match result|1x2)\b/.test(s))return 'Match result (1X2)';
  if(/\b(half time|first half|second half|ht|2nd half)\b/.test(s))return 'Half-time / period';
  if(/\b(handicap|asian handicap)\b/.test(s))return 'Handicap';
  if(/\b(player|shots on target|to score anytime|anytime goalscorer)\b/.test(s))return 'Player market';
  return 'Unclassified — verify';
}
function parseSlipLine(line,index){
  const tokens=[...line.matchAll(/(?<![\w.])(\d{1,3}\.\d{1,4})(?!\d)/g)];
  const valid=tokens.filter(m=>{const n=Number(m[1]);return n>1&&n<=1000});
  const goalLineOnly=/\b(?:over|under)\s*\d+(?:\.\d+)?\b/i.test(line)&&valid.length===1;
  const last=goalLineOnly?null:valid[valid.length-1];
  const endsWithOdd=!!last&&line.trim().endsWith(last[1]);
  const odd=last?Number(last[1]):null;
  let confidence='low',warning='No confident decimal odds found; enter the odds manually.';
  if(last){
    confidence=(valid.length===1&&endsWithOdd)?'high':'medium';
    warning=valid.length>1?'Multiple decimal numbers found (possibly a goal line plus odds). Verify the final number is the selection price.':'Verify that the extracted price belongs to this selection.';
  }
  const label=last?line.replace(last[0],' ').replace(/\b(?:odds?|price)\s*[:=@]?\s*/ig,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim():line.trim();
  const market=classifySlipMarket(line);
  const fixtureMatch=line.match(/(.+?)\s+(?:vs?\.?|versus)\s+(.+?)(?=\s+(?:\||[-–—])\s+|$)/i);
  return {id:'ocr-'+index,label:label||line.trim(),odd,p:odd?1/odd:null,estimate:null,group:fixtureMatch?(fixtureMatch[1].trim()+' vs '+fixtureMatch[2].trim()):'',marketType:market,confidence,warning,rawLine:line.trim(),source:'OCR/text'};
}
function shuffleSlipRows(rows){
 const out=rows.slice();
 for(let i=out.length-1;i>0;i--){const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));[out[i],out[j]]=[out[j],out[i]];}
 return out;
}
function parseSlipLines(text){
 const source=String(text||'').split(/\n/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
 const rows=[];let pending=[];
 const isHeader=line=>/^---\s*PHOTO\b/i.test(line)||/^page\s+\d+\s*$/i.test(line)||/^(sportybet|my bets|bet slip|booking code|share bet|total odds|potential win|stake|cashout|cash out|selections?)\s*[:#]?$/i.test(line);
 const hasOdds=line=>parseSlipLine(line,0).odd!==null;
 const isFixtureOnly=line=>!!parseFixtureText(line)&&!hasOdds(line)&&classifySlipMarket(line)==='Unclassified — verify';
 const flush=()=>{if(!pending.length)return;const raw=pending.join(' | ');const parsed=parseSlipLine(raw,rows.length);if(parsed.odd!==null||parsed.marketType!=='Unclassified — verify'||parseFixtureText(raw))rows.push(parsed);pending=[];};
 for(const line of source){
  if(isHeader(line))continue;
  if(isFixtureOnly(line)){
   flush();pending=[line];continue;
  }
  if(pending.length){
   pending.push(line);
   if(hasOdds(line)){flush();continue;}
   if(pending.length>=4){flush();}
   continue;
  }
  const parsedLine=parseSlipLine(line,0);
  const needsPrice=!hasOdds(line)&&parsedLine.marketType!=='Unclassified — verify';
  if(needsPrice){pending=[line];continue;}
  const parsed=parseSlipLine(line,rows.length);
  if(parsed.odd!==null||parsed.marketType!=='Unclassified — verify'||parseFixtureText(line))rows.push(parsed);
 }
 flush();
 return rows;
}

/* Resilient slip-photo OCR: the original page exposed controls but had no OCR click handler. */
const slipPhotoInput=$('slipPhotos');
if(slipPhotoInput){
 slipPhotoInput.addEventListener('change',()=>{
  const files=[...(slipPhotoInput.files||[])];
  $('slipPhotoPreview').textContent=files.length
   ? files.length+' image(s) selected: '+files.map(f=>f.name).join(', ')+'. Tap “Read text from photos (OCR)”.'
   : 'No slip photos selected yet. Upload clear, full screenshots; OCR may misread small text or unusual odds formats.';
  notice('ocrStatus',files.length?'Ready to read '+files.length+' image(s).':'Choose one or more slip photos first.',files.length?'good':'');
 });
}
async function ensureTesseract(){
 if(window.Tesseract&&typeof window.Tesseract.recognize==='function')return window.Tesseract;
 const urls=[
  'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/tesseract.js@5/dist/tesseract.min.js',
  'https://unpkg.com/tesseract.js@5/dist/tesseract.min.js'
 ];
 let lastError='OCR engine could not be loaded.';
 for(const src of urls){
  try{
   await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
    const script=document.createElement('script');script.src=src;script.async=true;
    const timer=setTimeout(()=>{script.remove();reject(new Error('Timed out loading OCR engine'));},18000);
    script.onload=()=>{clearTimeout(timer);resolve();};
    script.onerror=()=>{clearTimeout(timer);script.remove();reject(new Error('Could not load '+new URL(src).hostname));};
    document.head.appendChild(script);
   });
   if(window.Tesseract&&typeof window.Tesseract.recognize==='function')return window.Tesseract;
   lastError='OCR script loaded but did not expose the Tesseract API.';
  }catch(e){lastError=e.message||String(e);}
 }
 throw new Error(lastError+' Check internet access, content blockers, or try again later.');
}
$('ocrSlipPhotos').onclick=async()=>{
 const button=$('ocrSlipPhotos'),files=[...(slipPhotoInput?.files||[])];
 if(!files.length){notice('ocrStatus','Choose one or more slip photos first.','warning');return;}
 button.disabled=true;button.textContent='Reading photos…';
 let engine;
 try{
  notice('ocrStatus','Loading OCR engine. Keep this page open; first load may take a little time…');
  engine=await ensureTesseract();
  const extracted=[];
  for(let i=0;i<files.length;i++){
   const file=files[i];
   if(!file.type.startsWith('image/')){extracted.push('--- PHOTO '+(i+1)+' ---\n[Skipped: file is not an image]');continue;}
   notice('ocrStatus','Reading photo '+(i+1)+' of '+files.length+': '+file.name+'…');
   try{
    const result=await engine.recognize(file,'eng',{logger:m=>{
     if(m&&m.status==='recognizing text'&&Number.isFinite(m.progress)){
      notice('ocrStatus','Reading photo '+(i+1)+' of '+files.length+': '+Math.round(m.progress*100)+'%');
     }
    }});
    const text=String(result?.data?.text||'').trim();
    extracted.push('--- PHOTO '+(i+1)+': '+file.name+' ---\n'+(text||'[No readable text detected]'));
   }catch(e){extracted.push('--- PHOTO '+(i+1)+': '+file.name+' ---\n[OCR failed: '+(e.message||String(e))+']');}
  }
  const combined=extracted.join('\n\n');
  $('slipInput').value=[$('slipInput').value.trim(),combined].filter(Boolean).join('\n\n');
  const successful=extracted.filter(x=>!x.includes('[OCR failed:')&&!x.includes('[Skipped:')&&!x.includes('[No readable text detected]')).length;
  notice('ocrStatus','OCR finished: '+successful+'/'+files.length+' photo(s) produced text. Check and correct every team, market and odd before analyzing.',successful?'good':'warning');
  if(!successful)notice('ocrStatus','No readable text extracted. Try a sharper full screenshot with larger text, or paste the text manually. '+combined.split('\n').filter(x=>x.startsWith('[OCR failed:')).join(' '),'warning');
  if(successful)addActivity('Read text from '+successful+' slip photo(s) using OCR');
 }catch(e){
  notice('ocrStatus','Photo OCR could not start: '+(e.message||String(e))+'. Workaround: paste the slip text into the text box below; analysis does not require OCR.','error');
 }finally{button.disabled=false;button.textContent='Read text from photos (OCR)';}
};

$('analyzeSlip').onclick=()=>{
 try{
  const text=$('slipInput').value||'';
  let rows=parseSlipLines(text);
  /* Recovery path: preserve recognizable lines even if the grouped OCR parser finds none. */
  if(!rows.length&&text.trim()){
   rows=text.split(/\r?\n/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(x=>x&&!/^---\s*PHOTO\b/i.test(x)&&!/^page\s+\d+\s*$/i.test(x))
    .map((line,i)=>parseSlipLine(line,i))
    .filter(x=>x.odd!==null||x.marketType!=='Unclassified — verify'||parseFixtureText(x.rawLine));
  }
  S.manualSelections=shuffleSlipRows(rows.map(x=>({...x})));
  renderManualSelections();
  const valid=S.manualSelections.filter(x=>Number.isFinite(Number(x.odd))&&Number(x.odd)>1);
  const combined=valid.reduce((a,x)=>a*Number(x.odd),1),stake=Math.max(0,Number($('slipStake').value)||0);
  S.lastSlip={rows:S.manualSelections,combined:valid.length?combined:null,stake,date:now()};
  const calcRisk=window.UFICalculatorRisk?.evaluateSlip(S.manualSelections,stake)||null;
  if(calcRisk)S.lastSlip.calculatorRisk=calcRisk;
  const table=S.manualSelections.length?'<div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Line / selection</th><th>Market guess</th><th>Odds read</th><th>Confidence</th><th>Review note</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+S.manualSelections.map(x=>'<tr><td>'+esc(x.label)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.marketType)+'</td><td>'+(Number.isFinite(Number(x.odd))&&Number(x.odd)>1?Number(x.odd).toFixed(2):'Not read')+'</td><td>'+esc(x.confidence||'manual')+'</td><td>'+esc(x.warning||'Verify selection and price against the original slip.')+'</td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table></div>':'<p>No selection rows could be recognized. Paste one selection per line (for example: Arsenal win 1.80) or add selections using the manual form. The text was not deleted.</p>';
  const calcPanel=calcRisk?'<section class="panel"><h3>Automatic calculator &amp; risk checks</h3><p>Combined odds: <b>'+(calcRisk.combinedOdds===null?'—':calcRisk.combinedOdds.toFixed(4))+'</b> · Implied break-even rate: <b>'+(calcRisk.combinedImpliedProbability===null?'—':(calcRisk.combinedImpliedProbability*100).toFixed(2)+'%')+'</b></p><p>Potential return: <b>'+(calcRisk.potentialReturn===null?'enter stake':'₦'+calcRisk.potentialReturn.toLocaleString(undefined,{maximumFractionDigits:2}))+'</b> · Potential profit: <b>'+(calcRisk.potentialProfit===null?'—':'₦'+calcRisk.potentialProfit.toLocaleString(undefined,{maximumFractionDigits:2}))+'</b></p><p>Extra risk registry: <b>'+calcRisk.registry.total+' checks</b> · '+calcRisk.registry.counts.needsData+' need evidence · '+calcRisk.registry.counts.review+' review flags. NEEDS_DATA is not a pass.</p><ul>'+calcRisk.checks.map(c=>'<li><b>'+esc(c.name)+':</b> '+esc(c.status)+' — '+esc(c.detail)+'</li>').join('')+'</ul><p class="muted">Price calculations describe the slip; they do not predict results. Market margin and arbitrage remain unverified unless complete market prices are available.</p></section>':'';
  $('slipOutput').innerHTML=(S.manualSelections.length?'<p>Parsed '+S.manualSelections.length+' possible selection block(s); '+valid.length+' have a readable decimal price. OCR and market guesses are not verified facts. Confirm each team, market and price against the original slip.</p>'+table+'<div class="outcome-grid"><div><span>Priced rows</span><b>'+valid.length+'</b></div><div><span>Unpriced rows</span><b>'+(S.manualSelections.length-valid.length)+'</b></div><div><span>Combined odds of priced rows only</span><b>'+(valid.length?combined.toFixed(2):'—')+'</b></div><div><span>Stake entered</span><b>₦'+stake.toLocaleString()+'</b></div></div>':table)+calcPanel;
  addActivity('Parsed and randomized '+S.manualSelections.length+' possible slip selection blocks');
  if(!S.manualSelections.length)notice('ocrStatus','Analysis ran, but no selections were recognized. Check text and try one selection per line, or use the manual form.','warning');
 }catch(e){
  console.error('UFI slip analysis failed',e);
  notice('slipOutput','Slip analysis hit an error: '+(e.message||String(e))+'. Your pasted text has been kept. Try again or use the manual-selection form.','error');
 }
};

function renderManualSelections(){

 $('manualSelectionTable').innerHTML=rows.length?'<table><thead><tr><th>Selection</th><th>Market guess</th><th>Odds</th><th>Implied</th><th>Own estimate</th><th>Parse confidence</th><th>Correlation group</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>'+rows.map((x,i)=>'<tr><td>'+esc(x.label)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.marketType||'Manual')+'</td><td>'+(Number.isFinite(Number(x.odd))&&Number(x.odd)>1?Number(x.odd).toFixed(2):'Needs entry')+'</td><td>'+(Number.isFinite(Number(x.p))&&x.p!==null?pct(x.p):'—')+'</td><td>'+(x.estimate==null?'Not entered':pct(x.estimate))+'</td><td>'+esc(x.confidence||'manual')+(x.warning?'<br>'+esc(x.warning):'')+'</td><td>'+esc(x.group||'Not tagged')+'</td><td><button class="button danger small" data-remove-selection="'+i+'">Remove</button></td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table><p class="muted">Selections: '+rows.length+' · rows without verified odds are excluded from combined odds. Same-match selections may be correlated.</p>':'<p class="muted">No selections in current slip.</p>';
}
