const LIVE_LEAGUES=[
 ['eng.1','Premier League'],['esp.1','La Liga'],['ger.1','Bundesliga'],['ita.1','Serie A'],['fra.1','Ligue 1'],
 ['ned.1','Eredivisie'],['por.1','Primeira Liga'],['uefa.champions','Champions League'],['uefa.europa','Europa League'],
 ['usa.1','MLS'],['ksa.1','Saudi Pro League'],['tur.1','Turkish Super Lig']
];
async function fetchSportyBetUpcoming(){
 const endpoint='https://www.sportybet.com/api/ng/factsCenter/pcUpcomingEvents?sportId=sr%3Asport%3A1&marketId=1%2C18%2C10%2C29%2C11%2C26%2C36%2C14%2C60100&pageSize=100&pageNum=1&todayGames=false&timeline=168&_t='+Date.now();
 const payload=await json(endpoint);
 if(payload?.bizCode!==10000||!payload?.data)throw Error('SportyBet returned an unexpected response; no verified prices loaded.');
 const rows=[];
 for(const tournament of (payload.data.tournaments||[])){
  for(const ev of (tournament.events||[])){
   const home=ev.homeTeamName||ev.homeTeam?.name,away=ev.awayTeamName||ev.awayTeam?.name;
   if(!home||!away)continue;
   const markets=(ev.markets||[]).map(m=>({id:String(m.id||''),name:String(m.desc||m.name||''),specifier:m.specifier||'',status:m.status||'',outcomes:(m.outcomes||[]).filter(o=>o.isActive!==false).map(o=>({id:String(o.id||''),name:String(o.desc||o.name||''),odds:Number(o.odds),active:o.isActive!==false})).filter(o=>Number.isFinite(o.odds)&&o.odds>1)}));
   rows.push({id:String(ev.eventId||''),matchId:String(ev.eventId||''),date:ev.estimateStartTime?new Date(Number(ev.estimateStartTime)).toISOString():'',homeTeam:home,awayTeam:away,competition:tournament.name||tournament.categoryName||'SportyBet fixture',status:ev.matchStatus||'Scheduled',source:'sportybet-live-api',markets,oddsTimestamp:now()});
  }
 }
 if(!rows.length)throw Error('SportyBet returned no usable football fixtures.');
 return rows;
}
async function loadLiveFixtures(){
 const button=$('loadLiveFixtures');if(button){button.disabled=true;button.textContent='Loading current fixtures…'}
 notice('liveFixtureOutput','Connecting to SportyBet fixture/market feed first, then using ESPN as a fallback. Browser access to SportyBet may be blocked by CORS.','');
 try{
  let sportyBetRows=[],sportyBetError='';
  try{sportyBetRows=await fetchSportyBetUpcoming();S.sportyBetFixtures=sportyBetRows}catch(e){sportyBetError=String(e?.message||e);S.sportyBetFixtures=[]}
  const nowDate=new Date(),endDate=new Date(nowDate.getTime()+7*86400000);
  const ymd=d=>d.getFullYear()+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0');
  const dates=ymd(nowDate)+'-'+ymd(endDate);
  const results=await Promise.allSettled(LIVE_LEAGUES.map(async ([league,name])=>{
   const url='https://site.api.espn.com/apis/site/v2/sports/soccer/'+league+'/scoreboard?dates='+dates+'&limit=100';
   const data=await json(url);
   return (data.events||[]).map(ev=>{
    const comp=(ev.competitions||[])[0],teams=comp?.competitors||[];
    const home=teams.find(t=>t.homeAway==='home'),away=teams.find(t=>t.homeAway==='away');
    if(!home||!away)return null;
    return {id:String(ev.id||league+'-'+ev.date),date:ev.date||'',homeTeam:home.team?.displayName||home.team?.name||'',awayTeam:away.team?.displayName||away.team?.name||'',competition:name,status:ev.status?.type?.detail||ev.status?.type?.name||'Scheduled',url:ev.links?.[0]?.href||''};
   }).filter(x=>x&&x.homeTeam&&x.awayTeam);
  }));
  const good=results.filter(x=>x.status==='fulfilled').flatMap(x=>x.value);
  const failed=results.filter(x=>x.status==='rejected').length;
  const uniq=new Map();good.forEach(f=>uniq.set(f.id,f));
  const espnRows=[...uniq.values()];
  const allRows=[...sportyBetRows,...espnRows.filter(e=>!sportyBetRows.some(s=>normalizeTeamName(s.homeTeam)===normalizeTeamName(e.homeTeam)&&normalizeTeamName(s.awayTeam)===normalizeTeamName(e.awayTeam)))];
  S.liveFixtures=allRows.sort((a,b)=>String(a.date).localeCompare(String(b.date)));
  const rows=S.liveFixtures;
  $('liveFixtureOutput').innerHTML='<p><b>Current fixture feed:</b> '+rows.length+' fixtures found. SportyBet source: '+sportyBetRows.length+' fixtures with market prices; ESPN fallback: '+espnRows.length+' fixtures across '+(LIVE_LEAGUES.length-failed)+'/'+LIVE_LEAGUES.length+' league feeds.</p>'+(sportyBetError?'<p class="amber">SportyBet direct feed unavailable in this browser: '+esc(sportyBetError)+'. This may be a CORS/network restriction; do not treat ESPN fixtures as SportyBet odds.</p>':'')+
   (rows.length?'<div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Kickoff (feed timezone)</th><th>Fixture</th><th>Competition</th><th>Status</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+rows.map(f=>'<tr><td>'+esc(f.date?new Date(f.date).toLocaleString():'Not supplied')+'</td><td>'+esc(f.homeTeam+' vs '+f.awayTeam)+'</td><td>'+esc(f.competition)+'</td><td>'+esc(f.status)+'</td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table></div>':'<p>No fixtures returned. This may be an API/network limitation; it does not mean no matches are scheduled.</p>')+
   '<p class="amber">SportyBet odds are timestamped source prices, not guaranteed to match the price on your open slip. Market settlement rules, lineups, injuries and calibrated probabilities remain separate checks. ESPN fallback fixtures have no SportyBet prices. Use “Match slip fixtures” again after loading this feed.</p>';
  addActivity('Loaded '+rows.length+' public current/upcoming fixtures; '+failed+' league feeds failed');
  toast('Fixture feed loaded: '+rows.length+' matches');
 }catch(err){notice('liveFixtureOutput','Could not load public fixture feed: '+String(err?.message||err)+'. Check internet access and try again.','error')}
 finally{if(button){button.disabled=false;button.textContent='Load current & upcoming fixtures'}}
}
$('loadLiveFixtures').onclick=loadLiveFixtures;
$('runFullSlipAudit').onclick=async()=>{
 const button=$('runFullSlipAudit');
 if(!S.manualSelections.length){notice('strictAuditOutput','First upload your slip images or paste the extracted selections, click Analyze slip, and correct the teams/markets/odds.','warning');return;}
 button.disabled=true;button.textContent='Running full pipeline…';
 try{
  await loadLiveFixtures();
  runSlipFixtureMatching();
  await runStrictSlipAudit();
  const total=S.manualSelections.length;
  const latest=S.lastRiskAudit;
  const cut=latest?.rows?.filter(x=>x.status==='CUT').length||0;
  const hold=latest?.rows?.filter(x=>x.status==='HOLD — verify').length||0;
  const survivors=latest?.rows?.filter(x=>x.status==='SURVIVOR CANDIDATE').length||0;
  notice('liveFixtureOutput','Full pipeline finished: '+total+' selections checked; '+cut+' hard-stop CUT; '+hold+' HOLD for missing/uncertain evidence; '+survivors+' survivor candidates. Replacement picks are not auto-filled unless a candidate has an independently supported, fixture-specific estimate and odds. Review the per-leg table in the Strict Slip Auditor below.','');
 }catch(err){notice('strictAuditOutput','Full audit stopped safely: '+String(err?.message||err)+'. No bets were automatically placed or removed.','error')}
 finally{button.disabled=false;button.textContent='Run FULL slip audit (all stages)'}
};
const NEWS_BASE='https://api.gdeltproject.org/api/v2/doc/doc';
function riskNewsKeywords(title){
 const s=String(title||'').toLowerCase();
 const rules=[
  ['injury / fitness',/\binjur(?:y|ed|ies)\b|\bhamstring\b|\bmuscle\b|\bknock\b|\bfitness\b|\bfitness test\b|\bmedical\b|\breturn from injury\b/],
  ['availability / suspension',/\bsuspend(?:ed|sion)?\b|\bmiss(?:es|ing)\b|\bruled out\b|\bunavailable\b|\bwill not play\b|\bset to miss\b|\bban\b/],
  ['rotation / rest',/\brotat(?:e|ion|ed)\b|\brest(?:ed|ing)?\b|\bchanges to the lineup\b|\bchanged side\b|\bsecond string\b|\bpriorit(?:y|ise|ize)\b/],
  ['late lineup / uncertainty',/\bdoubt(?:ful)?\b|\bquestionable\b|\blate decision\b|\bstarting xi\b|\blineup\b|\bteam news\b|\bstarting eleven\b/],
  ['fixture congestion / fatigue',/\bfatigue\b|\bcongestion\b|\bthree games in\b|\bshort turnaround\b|\bextra time\b|\btravel\b/],
  ['motivation / context',/\brelegation\b|\btitle race\b|\bmust win\b|\bdead rubber\b|\brotation expected\b|\bcup tie\b/]
 ];
 return rules.filter(x=>x[1].test(s)).map(x=>x[0]);
}
async function fetchLiveFixtureNews(fixture){
 if(!fixture||fixture.status!=='candidate-found'||!fixture.homeTeam||!fixture.awayTeam) return {status:'blocked',checkedAt:now(),articles:[],riskTerms:[],message:'Cannot search news until a unique fixture candidate is matched.'};
 const query='"'+fixture.homeTeam+'" "'+fixture.awayTeam+'" (football OR soccer) (injury OR injured OR suspension OR suspended OR lineup OR team news OR rotation OR fitness OR doubtful OR unavailable)';
 const params=new URLSearchParams({query,mode:'ArtList',format:'json',maxrecords:'12',sort:'DateDesc',timespan:'7d'});
 const controller=new AbortController(),timer=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),12000);
 try{
  const response=await fetch(NEWS_BASE+'?'+params.toString(),{signal:controller.signal,headers:{Accept:'application/json'}});
  if(!response.ok)throw Error('News service HTTP '+response.status);
  const data=await response.json();
  const articles=(Array.isArray(data.articles)?data.articles:[]).map(a=>({title:String(a.title||''),url:String(a.url||''),domain:String(a.domain||''),seenDate:String(a.seendate||''),language:String(a.language||''),terms:riskNewsKeywords(a.title)})).filter(a=>a.title&&/^https?:\/\//i.test(a.url));
  const riskTerms=[...new Set(articles.flatMap(a=>a.terms))];
  return {status:'live-results',checkedAt:now(),query,articles,riskTerms,message:articles.length?'Recent articles found. Headlines are signals only; open sources and confirm the facts.':'No matching articles found in this search window. This is not evidence of no team risk.'};
 }catch(err){
  return {status:'unavailable',checkedAt:now(),articles:[],riskTerms:[],message:'Live news could not be verified: '+(err?.name==='AbortError'?'request timed out':String(err?.message||err))};
 }finally{clearTimeout(timer);}
}
function moodTagsFromText(value){
 const s=String(value||'').toLowerCase(),tags=[];
 const rules=[
  ['availability / lineup uncertainty',/injur|fitness|suspend|unavailable|lineup|rotation|doubtful|missing player|selection uncertainty/],
  ['fatigue / fixture congestion',/fatigue|congestion|short turnaround|travel|rest|tired|fixture pile/],
  ['motivation / match context',/motivation|relegation|title race|must win|dead rubber|cup tie|priority/],
  ['price / market trap',/odds|price|expected value|\bev\b|market mismatch|price differs|overround|correlation/],
  ['low-probability / form concern',/probability|below|form|poor run|loss|goals|scoring|defence|defense/],
  ['high-variance market',/player market|cards|corners|handicap|half.time|unclassified|shots on target/],
  ['data / fixture uncertainty',/unmatched|not verified|needs data|unknown|missing evidence|not uniquely matched|unavailable|uncalibrated/]
 ];
 for(const [tag,re] of rules)if(re.test(s))tags.push(tag);
 return tags;
}
function collectStoredMoodMatches(row){
 const fixture=row.fixtureResolution||{},parsed=parseFixtureText(row.rawLine||row.label||''),market=String(row.marketType||classifySlipMarket(row.rawLine||row.label||''));
 const currentTeams=[fixture.homeTeam,fixture.awayTeam,parsed?.homeText,parsed?.awayText].filter(Boolean).map(normalizeTeamName).filter(Boolean);
 const hits=[],seen=new Set();
 const add=(item)=>{const key=[item.kind,item.match||'',item.label||'',item.date||'',item.mood||''].join('|');if(!seen.has(key)){seen.add(key);hits.push(item)}};
 for(const audit of (S.riskAudits||[])){
  for(const old of (audit.rows||[])){
   const oldFixture=old.fixture||{},oldTeams=[oldFixture.homeTeam,oldFixture.awayTeam].filter(Boolean).map(normalizeTeamName).filter(Boolean);
   const overlap=currentTeams.some(t=>oldTeams.includes(t));
   const oldMarket=String(old.marketType||classifySlipMarket(old.rawLine||old.label||''));
   const text=[...(old.reasons||[]),...(old.warnings||[]),old.status||'',old.label||''].join(' ');
   const tags=moodTagsFromText(text);
   const status=String(old.status||'');
   const concerning=/CUT|HOLD/.test(status);
   const sharedMarket=market===oldMarket&&market!=='Unclassified — verify';
   const sharedMood=tags.length>0&&moodTagsFromText([row.label,row.rawLine,market].join(' ')).some(t=>tags.includes(t));
   if(overlap&&concerning){
    add({kind:'same-team history',match:[oldFixture.homeTeam,oldFixture.awayTeam].filter(Boolean).join(' vs ')||old.label,label:old.label||'Saved audited selection',date:audit.date||'',status,mood:tags.join(', ')||'prior CUT/HOLD reasons',reason:[...(old.reasons||[]),...(old.warnings||[])].slice(0,4).join('; ')||'Previous audit marked this leg for review',weight:status==='CUT'?2:1});
   }else if(sharedMarket&&sharedMood&&concerning){
    add({kind:'similar historical pattern',match:[oldFixture.homeTeam,oldFixture.awayTeam].filter(Boolean).join(' vs ')||old.label,label:old.label||'Saved audited selection',date:audit.date||'',status,mood:tags.join(', '),reason:[...(old.reasons||[]),...(old.warnings||[])].slice(0,3).join('; ')||'Similar market and mood tags in a prior audit',weight:0});
   }
  }
 }
 for(const context of (S.contexts||[])){
  const team=normalizeTeamName(context.team||context.teamName||'');
  const evidence=String(context.evidence||context.notes||'');
  const tags=moodTagsFromText(evidence);
  if(team&&currentTeams.includes(team)&&(tags.length||/mood|spoiler|game.?killer|trap/i.test(evidence))){
   add({kind:'saved team context',match:context.team||context.teamName||'Saved team',label:'Saved team context',date:context.date||'',status:'USER-SAVED CONTEXT',mood:tags.join(', ')||'user-defined team mood',reason:evidence.slice(0,260),weight:1});
  }
 }
 for(const note of (S.notes||[])){
  const text=[note.title,note.type,note.body].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
  const noteTeams=currentTeams.filter(t=>normalizeTeamName(text).includes(t));
  const tags=moodTagsFromText(text);
  const moodNote=/mood|spoiler|game.?killer|trap|risk pattern|cut reason/i.test(text);
  if(moodNote&&(noteTeams.length||tags.length)){
   add({kind:noteTeams.length?'saved mood note for this team':'saved general mood note',match:noteTeams.length?noteTeams.join(' / '):'Research note',label:note.title||'Saved research note',date:note.date||'',status:'USER-SAVED NOTE',mood:tags.join(', ')||'user-defined mood',reason:String(note.body||'').slice(0,260),weight:noteTeams.length?1:0});
  }
 }
 const teamHits=hits.filter(x=>x.kind==='same-team history'||x.kind==='saved mood note for this team'||x.kind==='saved team context');
 const patterns=hits.filter(x=>x.kind==='similar historical pattern'||x.kind==='saved general mood note');
 return {matched:hits.length>0,teamMatchCount:teamHits.length,patternMatchCount:patterns.length,hits:hits.sort((a,b)=>b.weight-a.weight),moodLabels:[...new Set(hits.flatMap(x=>String(x.mood||'').split(', ').filter(Boolean)))]};
}
function scoreSlipLeg(row,news){
 const f=row.fixtureResolution||{},e=row.marketEstimate||null,reasons=[],warnings=[];
 let risk=0,hardStop=false;
 const moodAudit=collectStoredMoodMatches(row);
 const cut=(n,reason)=>{risk+=n;reasons.push(reason);hardStop=true};
 const warn=(n,reason)=>{risk+=n;warnings.push(reason)};
 if(moodAudit.teamMatchCount)warn(Math.min(25,15+5*(moodAudit.teamMatchCount-1)),'Stored mood/game-killer history matches this team: '+moodAudit.hits.filter(x=>x.weight>0).slice(0,3).map(x=>x.match+' ['+x.status+'] '+x.mood).join(' | '));
 else if(moodAudit.patternMatchCount)warn(6,'Stored mood pattern resembles this market. Pattern-only evidence is a weak signal: '+moodAudit.hits.slice(0,2).map(x=>x.match+' ['+x.status+'] '+x.mood).join(' | '));
 if(!(Number(row.odd)>1))cut(100,'Odds missing or invalid');
 if(row.confidence&&row.confidence!=='high')warn(22,'OCR confidence is not high; manually verify the selection and price');
 if(f.status!=='candidate-found')warn(35,'Fixture is not uniquely matched; cannot verify current fixture or kickoff');
 if(f.orientation==='verify-orientation')warn(35,'Home/away orientation needs manual verification');
 if(!e||e.status!=='estimated')warn(35,'No supported probability for this exact market. This is not proof the match is bad; hold it until a valid estimate exists.');
 if(e?.status==='estimated'){
  if(!Number.isFinite(e.probability)||e.probability<0||e.probability>1)cut(100,'Model probability is invalid');
  else if(e.probability<0.55)warn(28,'Experimental estimate is below the strict 55% threshold; reject unless independently validated');
  else if(e.probability<0.65)warn(10,'Estimated hit probability is below 65%');
  if(!Number.isFinite(e.ev)||e.ev<=0)warn(25,'Estimated expected value is not positive; price may be poor and the model is not calibrated');
  else if(e.ev<0.03)warn(8,'Estimated edge is under 3 percentage points and fragile');
 }
 const market=String(row.marketType||classifySlipMarket(row.rawLine||row.label||''));
 if(/Player market|Cards|Corners|Half-time|Handicap|Unclassified/.test(market))warn(15,'High-variance or unsupported-by-current-model market type: '+market);
 if(f.status==='candidate-found'){
  if(f.source==='historical-statsbomb')cut(100,'This is a historical StatsBomb match, not proof of a current/upcoming fixture.');
  const teams=[f.homeTeam,f.awayTeam].filter(Boolean).map(normalizeTeamName);
  const playerEvidence=(S.playerEventMatches||[]).some(p=>teams.includes(normalizeTeamName(p.team)))||(S.players||[]).some(p=>teams.includes(normalizeTeamName(p.team))&&p.source&&p.confidence);
  if(!playerEvidence)warn(8,'No saved player-event / sourced player-intelligence evidence for these teams');
  if(!S.lineups?.length||String(S.lineupsMatchId)!==String(f.matchId))warn(12,'No lineup file loaded for this exact fixture; availability and rotation remain unverified');
  const teamContext=(S.contexts||[]).some(c=>teams.includes(normalizeTeamName(c.team||c.teamName||'')));
  if(!teamContext)warn(5,'No saved team-context note found for this fixture');
 }
 if((S.backtests||[]).length<30)warn(10,'Fewer than 30 saved settled forecasts; predictive calibration is not established');
 if(news?.status==='unavailable')warn(18,'Live-news service unavailable; current availability risk remains unknown');
 else if(news?.status==='blocked')warn(20,'No fixture-specific live-news search possible');
 else if(news?.status==='live-results'){
  if(!news.articles.length)warn(12,'No matching reports found; absence of news is not clearance');
  if(news.riskTerms.includes('injury / fitness'))warn(25,'Headlines contain injury/fitness keywords. Verify named player, team, date and original report before treating this as a real risk.');
  if(news.riskTerms.includes('availability / suspension'))warn(28,'Headlines contain availability/suspension keywords; source text has not been verified.');
  if(news.riskTerms.includes('rotation / rest'))warn(18,'Headlines contain rotation/rest keywords; verify this exact fixture.');
  if(news.riskTerms.includes('late lineup / uncertainty'))warn(12,'Recent headlines mention lineup/selection uncertainty');
  if(news.riskTerms.includes('fixture congestion / fatigue'))warn(8,'Recent headlines mention congestion/fatigue');
  if(news.riskTerms.includes('motivation / context'))warn(5,'Recent headlines mention a context/motivation factor');
 }
 const status=hardStop?'CUT':risk>=12?'HOLD — verify':'SURVIVOR CANDIDATE';
 return {riskScore:Math.min(100,risk),status,hardStop,reasons,warnings,moodAudit,probability:e?.status==='estimated'?e.probability:null,ev:e?.status==='estimated'?e.ev:null,newsStatus:news?.status||'not checked'};
}
function matchSportyBetPrice(row,fixture){
 const markets=Array.isArray(fixture?.markets)?fixture.markets:[];
 if(fixture?.source!=='sportybet-live-api'||!markets.length)return {status:'unavailable',reason:'No SportyBet markets attached to this exact fixture.'};
 const raw=String(row.rawLine||row.label||'').toLowerCase();
 const compact=s=>String(s||'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9.]+/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
 const text=compact(raw),lineOdd=Number(row.odd),kind=String(row.marketType||classifySlipMarket(raw));
 const marketKindMatch=m=>{
  const n=compact(m.name);
  if(kind==='Goals total')return /total goals|over\/under|goal line|goals/.test(n);
  if(kind==='Both teams to score')return /both teams|btts/.test(n);
  if(kind==='Match result (1X2)')return /1x2|match result|full time result|winner/.test(n);
  if(kind==='Double chance')return /double chance/.test(n);
  if(kind==='Corners')return /corner/.test(n);
  if(kind==='Cards')return /card|booking/.test(n);
  if(kind==='Player market')return /player|shots|goalscorer|on target/.test(n);
  return n.length>0&&text.includes(n);
 };
 const matches=[];
 for(const m of markets){
  if(!marketKindMatch(m))continue;
  for(const o of (m.outcomes||[])){
   const outcome=compact(o.name);
   const outcomeMentioned=outcome.length>=2&&text.includes(outcome);
   const teamMentioned=[fixture.homeTeam,fixture.awayTeam].some(t=>compact(t).length>2&&text.includes(compact(t)));
   const priceDifference=Number.isFinite(lineOdd)?Math.abs(lineOdd-o.odds):null;
   if(outcomeMentioned&&teamMentioned)matches.push({marketId:m.id,marketName:m.name,outcomeId:o.id,outcomeName:o.name,sourceOdds:o.odds,slipOdds:Number.isFinite(lineOdd)?lineOdd:null,priceDifference,oddsTimestamp:fixture.oddsTimestamp});
  }
 }
 if(!matches.length)return {status:'unmatched',reason:'SportyBet fixture exists, but UFI could not confidently map both the precise market and outcome from the slip text.',candidateMarkets:markets.length};
 const best=matches.sort((a,b)=>(a.priceDifference??999)-(b.priceDifference??999))[0];
 if(best.priceDifference!==null&&best.priceDifference<=0.02)return {...best,status:'exact-market-and-price-match',reason:'Market, outcome and displayed decimal price match the source feed within 0.02.'};
 return {...best,status:'market-mapped-price-differs',reason:'Possible market/outcome match, but the source price differs from the slip price; do not treat the price as verified.'};
}
async function runStrictSlipAudit(){
 const button=$('runStrictAudit');button.disabled=true;button.textContent='Fetching live news & auditing…';
 try{
  if(!S.manualSelections.length){notice('strictAuditOutput','Analyze a slip or add selections first.','warning');return;}
  const rows=[];
  for(let i=0;i<S.manualSelections.length;i++){
   const row=S.manualSelections[i],fixture=row.fixtureResolution||{};
   notice('strictAuditOutput','Searching recent news and scoring leg '+(i+1)+' of '+S.manualSelections.length+'…');
   const news=await fetchLiveFixtureNews(fixture);
   row.liveNews=news;
   row.sportyBetMarketMatch=matchSportyBetPrice(row,fixture);
   const estimate=(row.marketEstimate&&String(row.marketEstimate.fixtureId||'')===String(fixture.matchId||''))?row.marketEstimate:((S.scenario&&fixture.source!=='live-fixture-feed'&&String(S.scenario.matchId||'')===String(fixture.matchId||''))?{...estimateSlipMarket(row,S.scenario),fixtureId:String(fixture.matchId||''),modelSource:'experimental-scoreline-scenario'}:null);
   row.marketEstimate=estimate;
   row.riskAudit=scoreSlipLeg(row,news);
   row.extendedAudit=window.UFI400Audit?.evaluate(row,news,S)||null;
   rows.push({...row,audit:{index:i,fixture,estimate,news,...row.riskAudit,extendedAudit:row.extendedAudit,sportyBetMarketMatch:row.sportyBetMarketMatch}});
  }
  const survivors=rows.filter(r=>r.audit.status==='SURVIVOR CANDIDATE').sort((a,b)=>(b.audit.probability??-1)-(a.audit.probability??-1));
  const cut=rows.filter(r=>r.audit.status==='CUT');
  const hold=rows.filter(r=>r.audit.status==='HOLD — verify');
  const replacements=survivors.filter(r=>r.source==='replacement');
  const original=survivors.find(r=>r.source!=='replacement');
  const picks=[{label:'Game 1 — highest-probability surviving original',row:original||null},{label:'Game 2 — replacement A (surviving candidate)',row:replacements[0]||null},{label:'Game 3 — replacement B (next surviving candidate)',row:replacements[1]||null}];
  S.lastRiskAudit={date:now(),calculatorRisk:window.UFICalculatorRisk?.evaluateSlip(S.manualSelections,Number($('slipStake').value)||0)||null,pipelineVersion:'UFI strict audit v3 + 400-signal registry',aiCouncil:S.aiCouncilLast?{status:S.aiCouncilLast.status,successfulModels:S.aiCouncilLast.successfulModels,generatedAt:S.aiCouncilLast.generatedAt,models:(S.aiCouncilLast.models||[]).map(m=>({provider:m.provider,model:m.model,status:m.status,error:m.error||null,assessment:m.parsed?.summary||null,traps:(m.parsed?.traps||[]).slice(0,8),missingEvidence:(m.parsed?.missing_evidence||[]).slice(0,10)}))}:null,newsProvider:'GDELT DOC 2.0 public article search',fixtureProvider:S.sportyBetFixtures?.length?'SportyBet public web API (unofficial) + ESPN fallback':'ESPN public scoreboards; SportyBet feed unavailable',sportyBetOddsFetched:(S.sportyBetFixtures||[]).length,sportyBetOddsTimestamp:S.sportyBetFixtures?.[0]?.oddsTimestamp||null,replacementSearch:{status:'not-qualified',reason:'Current fixture discovery is available, but UFI does not yet have an integrated, verified odds feed plus a calibrated per-fixture prediction model for unseen replacement matches. No replacement is fabricated.',candidateFixturesAvailable:(S.liveFixtures||[]).length},newsWindow:'last 7 days',workspaceEvidence:{playerRecords:S.players.length,playerMatchProfiles:S.playerEventMatches.length,substitutionNotes:S.subs.length,teamContextNotes:S.contexts.length,savedSettledForecasts:S.backtests.length,loadedLineupMatchId:S.lineupsMatchId||null},extendedSignalAudit:{version:'UFI 400-signal registry v1',totalChecks:400,perSelection:rows.map(r=>({label:r.label,counts:r.extendedAudit?.counts||{},coveragePercent:r.extendedAudit?.coveragePercent??0,failures:(r.extendedAudit?.failures||[]).map(x=>({id:x.id,name:x.name,detail:x.detail})),needsData:(r.extendedAudit?.needsData||[]).slice(0,30).map(x=>({id:x.id,name:x.name,detail:x.detail}))}))},rows:rows.map(r=>{const f=r.audit.fixture||{};const teams=[f.homeTeam,f.awayTeam].filter(Boolean).map(normalizeTeamName);return {id:r.id||('selection-'+r.audit.index),label:r.label,rawLine:r.rawLine||r.label,marketType:r.marketType||classifySlipMarket(r.rawLine||r.label||''),odds:r.odd,source:r.source||'manual/OCR',fixture:f,newsStatus:r.audit.newsStatus,articles:r.audit.news?.articles||[],riskScore:r.audit.riskScore,status:r.audit.status,reasons:r.audit.reasons,warnings:r.audit.warnings,moodAudit:r.audit.moodAudit||null,probability:r.audit.probability,probabilitySource:r.marketEstimate?.modelSource||(r.marketEstimate?.status==='estimated'?'scenario model':'none'),ev:r.audit.ev,evidence:{fixtureMatched:f.status==='candidate-found',currentFixtureFeedMatch:f.source==='live-fixture-feed',historicalFixtureOnly:f.source==='historical-statsbomb',validDecimalOdds:Number(r.odd)>1,liveNewsStatus:r.audit.newsStatus,newsArticleCount:r.audit.news?.articles?.length||0,playerEvidenceAvailable:(S.playerEventMatches||[]).some(p=>teams.includes(normalizeTeamName(p.team)))||(S.players||[]).some(p=>teams.includes(normalizeTeamName(p.team))&&p.source&&p.confidence),lineupEvidenceLoaded:!!S.lineups?.length&&String(S.lineupsMatchId)===String(f.matchId),teamContextAvailable:(S.contexts||[]).some(c=>teams.includes(normalizeTeamName(c.team||c.teamName||''))),settledForecastCount:(S.backtests||[]).length,probabilityAvailable:r.audit.probability!=null,probabilityCalibrated:false,liveSportyBetOddsVerified:r.sportyBetMarketMatch?.status==='exact-market-and-price-match',sportyBetMarketMatch:r.sportyBetMarketMatch?.status||'unmatched',sportyBetMarketMatchReason:r.sportyBetMarketMatch?.reason||'No source market match',sportyBetMarketCount:(f.markets||[]).length,sportyBetOddsTimestamp:f.oddsTimestamp||null,liveSportyBetPricesAvailable:!!(f.source==='sportybet-live-api'&&(f.markets||[]).length)}}}),cutCount:cut.length,holdCount:hold.length,survivorCount:survivors.length,shortlist:picks.map(p=>({slot:p.label,selection:p.row?.label||null,probability:p.row?.audit.probability??null,odds:p.row?.odd??null,ev:p.row?.audit.ev??null}))};
  S.riskAudits.unshift(S.lastRiskAudit);S.riskAudits=S.riskAudits.slice(0,100);persist();
  const listNews=r=>r.audit.news?.articles?.length?'<ul>'+r.audit.news.articles.slice(0,4).map(a=>'<li><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="'+esc(a.url)+'">'+esc(a.title)+'</a> <small>'+esc(a.domain)+' · '+esc(a.seenDate||'date unavailable')+'</small>'+(a.terms.length?' <b>Signals: '+esc(a.terms.join(', '))+'</b>':'')+'</li>').join('')+'</ul>':'<small>'+esc(r.audit.news?.message||'No news status')+'</small>';
  const table=rows.length?'<div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Selection</th><th>Risk / 100</th><th>Decision</th><th>Model P / EV</th><th>SportyBet market match</th><th>Reasons, stored moods & live source headlines</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+rows.map(r=>'<tr><td>'+esc(r.label)+'<br><small>'+esc(r.audit.fixture.homeTeam&&r.audit.fixture.awayTeam?r.audit.fixture.homeTeam+' vs '+r.audit.fixture.awayTeam:'Unmatched fixture')+'</small></td><td>'+r.audit.riskScore+'</td><td><b>'+esc(r.audit.status)+'</b></td><td>'+(r.audit.probability==null?'—':pct(r.audit.probability))+'<br>'+(r.audit.ev==null?'EV —':(r.audit.ev*100).toFixed(1)+'% EV')+'</td><td>'+(r.audit.reasons.length?'<b>Cut reasons</b><ul>'+r.audit.reasons.map(x=>'<li>'+esc(x)+'</li>').join('')+'</ul>':'')+(r.audit.warnings.length?'<b>Warnings</b><ul>'+r.audit.warnings.map(x=>'<li>'+esc(x)+'</li>').join('')+'</ul>':'')+(r.audit.moodAudit?.matched?'<b>Stored mood / game-killer matches</b><p>'+esc((r.audit.moodAudit.moodLabels||[]).join(' · '))+'</p><ul>'+r.audit.moodAudit.hits.map(x=>'<li><b>'+esc(x.kind)+': '+esc(x.match)+'</b> · '+esc(x.status)+' · '+esc(x.date||'date unknown')+' · '+esc(x.mood)+'<br>'+esc(x.reason)+'</li>').join('')+'</ul>':'<small>No saved mood/game-killer record matched this leg. This does not mean the leg is safe.</small>')+listNews(r)+'</td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table></div>':'<p>No selections to audit.</p>';
  const extendedSummary=rows.map(r=>{const x=r.extendedAudit;return '<li><b>'+esc(r.label)+'</b>: '+(x?x.total+' signals; '+(x.counts.ASSESSED||0)+' assessed; '+(x.counts.NEEDS_DATA||0)+' need data; '+(x.counts.FAIL||0)+' failed':'400-signal registry unavailable')+'. Coverage '+(x?x.coveragePercent:0)+'%.</li>'}).join('');
  const traps='<h3>🧠 400-SIGNAL AUDIT COVERAGE</h3><p>Each selection is checked against 400 named signals across 20 domains.    addActivity('Removed '+cut.length+' CUT legs from slip after risk audit');
  });
  if(S.aiCouncilLast) renderAICouncilRiskBridge(S.aiCouncilLast);

 }catch(err){notice('strictAuditOutput','Risk audit stopped: '+String(err?.message||err)+'. No selections were automatically removed.','error');}

Save forecasts with actual results after the match.';$('backtestTable').innerHTML='<p class="muted">No forecasts logged yet.</p>';return}const acc=rows.filter(x=>['H','D','A'][x.probs.indexOf(Math.max(...x.probs))]===x.result).length/rows.length;const brier=rows.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.probs.reduce((s,p,i)=>s+(p-(['H','D','A'][i]===x.result?1:0))**2,0),0)/rows.length;const logloss=rows.reduce((a,x)=>a-Math.log(Math.max(1e-15,x.probs[['H','D','A'].indexOf(x.result)])),0)/rows.length;$('btAccuracy').textContent=pct(acc);$('btBrier').textContent=brier.toFixed(4);$('btLogloss').textContent=logloss.toFixed(4);$('calibrationSummary').textContent='Mean scores over '+rows.length+' settled records. Lower Brier and log loss are better. Accuracy alone ignores confidence quality; evaluate chronologically and compare against a baseline.';$('backtestTable').innerHTML='<table><thead><tr><th>Match</th><th>Method</th><th>Forecast H/D/A</th><th>Actual</th><th>Brier</th><th>Logged</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>'+rows.slice().reverse().map(x=>{const y=['H','D','A'].indexOf(x.result),br=x.probs.reduce((s,p,i)=>s+(p-(i===y?1:0))**2,0);return '<tr><td>'+esc(x.match)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.method||'Not specified')+'</td><td>'+x.probs.map(pct).join(' / ')+'</td><td>'+esc(x.result)+'</td><td>'+br.toFixed(4)+'</td><td>'+fmtDate(x.date)+'</td><td><button class="button danger small" data-delete-bt="'+x.id+'">Delete</button></td></tr>'}).join('')+'</tbody></table>'}

function renderAICouncilRiskBridge(result){
 if(!result||!S.lastRiskAudit)return;
 const flags=(result.models||[]).flatMap(m=>(m.parsed?.traps||[]).map(t=>({provider:m.provider,selection:t.selection||'Unspecified leg',concern:t.concern||'Concern not specified',evidenceLevel:t.evidence_level||'unknown',action:t.action||'HOLD'}))).slice(0,24);
 S.lastRiskAudit.aiCouncil={status:result.status,successfulModels:result.successfulModels,generatedAt:result.generatedAt,models:(result.models||[]).map(m=>({provider:m.provider,model:m.model,status:m.status,error:m.error||null,assessment:m.parsed?.summary||null,traps:(m.parsed?.traps||[]).slice(0,8),missingEvidence:(m.parsed?.missing_evidence||[]).slice(0,10)}))};
 S.lastRiskAudit.aiReviewFlags=flags;
 const groups={};flags.forEach(f=>{const k=String(f.selection||'Unspecified leg').trim().toLowerCase();(groups[k]||(groups[k]={label:f.selection,providers:new Set(),concerns:[]})).providers.add(f.provider);groups[k].concerns.push(f.concern)});const repeated=Object.values(groups).filter(g=>g.providers.size>=2);
 const out=$('strictAuditOutput');
 if(out){
  out.querySelector('#aiCouncilRiskBridge')?.remove();
  const box=document.createElement('div');box.id='aiCouncilRiskBridge';box.className='notice warning';
  box.innerHTML='<h3>🤖 AI COUNCIL CROSS-CHECK — ADVISORY ONLY</h3><p>'+esc(result.status)+' · '+Number(result.successfulModels||0)+' model(s) responded. These flags do not change deterministic CUT/HOLD decisions.</p>'+(repeated.length?'<h4>Repeated concerns across models</h4><ul>'+repeated.map(g=>'<li><b>'+esc(g.label)+'</b> — flagged by '+esc([...g.providers].join(', '))+'; '+esc([...new Set(g.concerns)].join('; '))+'</li>').join('')+'</ul>':'<p>No exact selection concern was repeated across two or more models. This is not evidence that selections are safe; review single-model flags and disagreements.</p>')+(flags.length?'<h4>All advisory flags</h4><ul>'+flags.map(f=>'<li><b>'+esc(f.selection)+'</b> — '+esc(f.concern)+' <small>['+esc(f.provider)+'; '+esc(f.evidenceLevel)+']</small></li>').join('')+'</ul>':'<p>No structured AI trap flags were parsed. This is not evidence that selections are safe.</p>')+'<p class="muted">Verify every AI claim against source evidence. Unverified flags remain questions, not facts.</p>';
  out.appendChild(box);
 }
}

async function initUfiTelemetry(){
 try{
  const response=await fetch('https://universal-football-intelligence.onrender.com/api/analytics/config',{headers:{Accept:'application/json'},cache:'no-store'});
  if(!response.ok)return;
  const cfg=await response.json();
  if(!cfg.configured||!cfg.projectToken||!cfg.apiHost)return;
  if(!window.posthog){
   const script=document.createElement('script');
   script.src=cfg.apiHost.replace(/\/$/,'')+'/static/array.js';
   script.async=true;
   script.onload=()=>startPosthog(cfg);
   script.onerror=()=>{};
   document.head.appendChild(script);
  }else startPosthog(cfg);
 }catch(_){}
}
function startPosthog(cfg){
 try{
  if(!window.posthog||window.__ufiPosthogStarted)return;
  window.__ufiPosthogStarted=true;
  window.posthog.init(cfg.projectToken,{api_host:cfg.apiHost,autocapture:false,capture_pageview:true,
   disable_session_recording:true,persistence:'memory',person_profiles:'never',
   loaded:function(ph){
    ph.capture('ufi_app_loaded',{app_version:'2026-10-28',telemetry_scope:'anonymous product health'});
    const allowed=new Set([
     'runAiCouncil','runAiCouncilScenario','runScenario','runLiveBrain','runExactScore','runMarketAudit',
     'testAiProviders','loadFixtures','refreshLive','beRun','caRun','ufiRunEndToEndPipeline',
     'craineeTacticalSurpriseRun','craineeUnconventionalRun','craineeBackgroundBreakRun','craineeSubconsciousRun'
    ]);
    document.addEventListener('click',function(event){
     const el=event.target&&event.target.closest?event.target.closest('button,[role="button"]'):null;
     if(el&&allowed.has(el.id))ph.capture('ufi_action',{action:el.id});
    },{passive:true});
   }});
 }catch(_){}
}
renderAllSaved();renderManualSelections();
initUfiTelemetry();
if(location.protocol==='file:')$('netState').textContent='Local file mode';
else $('netState').textContent='Ready';
})();

const features=groups.flatMap((g,gi)=>g[1].map((name,ni)=>({id:'UFI-'+String(gi*20+ni+1).padStart(3,'0'),category:g[0],name})));
function evaluate(row,news,state){
 const f=row.fixtureResolution||{},e=row.marketEstimate||null,odd=Number(row.odd);
 const checks=features.map(x=>({...x,status:'NEEDS_DATA',detail:'Required verified data source is not connected or has not been loaded.'}));
 const set=(id,status,detail)=>{const c=checks.find(x=>x.id===id);if(c){c.status=status;c.detail=detail;}};
 set('UFI-001',row.label?'ASSESSED':'FAIL',row.label?'Selection text is present.':'Selection label is empty.');
 set('UFI-007',f.status==='candidate-found'?'ASSESSED':'NEEDS_DATA',f.status==='candidate-found'?'A fixture candidate was matched; verify uniqueness and orientation.':'No unique fixture match has been established.');
 set('UFI-008',f.orientation==='verify-orientation'?'NEEDS_DATA':'ASSESSED',f.orientation==='verify-orientation'?'Home/away orientation needs manual verification.':'No orientation warning was raised by the matcher.');
 set('UFI-009',f.matchId?'ASSESSED':'NEEDS_DATA',f.matchId?'Fixture identifier exists.':'Fixture identifier missing.');
 set('UFI-010',f.kickoff?'ASSESSED':'NEEDS_DATA',f.kickoff?'Kickoff field exists; timezone and source freshness remain separate checks.':'Kickoff timestamp unavailable.');
 set('UFI-015',Number.isFinite(odd)&&odd>1?'ASSESSED':'FAIL',Number.isFinite(odd)&&odd>1?'Decimal odds are greater than 1.':'Decimal odds are missing or invalid.');
 set('UFI-016',Number.isFinite(odd)&&odd>1?'ASSESSED':'NEEDS_DATA',Number.isFinite(odd)&&odd>1?'Odds treated as decimal odds.':'Cannot normalize invalid odds.');
 set('UFI-021',Number.isFinite(odd)&&odd>1?'ASSESSED':'NEEDS_DATA',Number.isFinite(odd)&&odd>1?'Raw implied probability is 1/decimal odds; it is not a fair probability.':'Valid decimal odds required.');
 set('UFI-022',e&&Number.isFinite(e.probability)&&e.probability>=0&&e.probability<=1?'ASSESSED':(e?'FAIL':'NEEDS_DATA'),e&&Number.isFinite(e.probability)&&e.probability>=0&&e.probability<=1?'Estimate is within [0,1]; calibration is not implied.':e?'Probability estimate is outside valid bounds.':'No probability estimate for this exact market.');
 set('UFI-023',e?.modelSource?'ASSESSED':'NEEDS_DATA',e?.modelSource?'Estimate source: '+e.modelSource+'.':'Probability source not supplied.');
 set('UFI-024','NEEDS_DATA','Calibration evidence is not connected to this selection audit.');
 set('UFI-025',e?'NEEDS_DATA':'NEEDS_DATA','A matching market/league base-rate dataset is required.');
 set('UFI-026','NEEDS_DATA','A saved forecast count alone does not establish sample adequacy.');
 set('UFI-028','NEEDS_DATA','Saved forecasts are not automatically split into valid out-of-sample tests.');
 set('UFI-031','NEEDS_DATA','Calibration drift tracking is not implemented.');
 set('UFI-038',Number.isFinite(odd)&&odd>1?'ASSESSED':'NEEDS_DATA',Number.isFinite(odd)&&odd>1?'Implied probability = 1/odds; bookmaker margin cannot be removed from one price alone.':'Odds needed for implied probability.');
 set('UFI-039','NEEDS_DATA','Independence of accumulator legs is not assumed or proven.');
 set('UFI-040','NEEDS_DATA','Joint hit-rate requires calibrated per-leg probabilities and dependence treatment.');
 set('UFI-041',row.marketType?'ASSESSED':'NEEDS_DATA',row.marketType?'Market type: '+row.marketType+'.':'Market type not confidently classified.');
 set('UFI-101','NEEDS_DATA','Shots line parsing needs a structured player/team shots market and source.');
 set('UFI-181',f.status==='candidate-found'?'ASSESSED':'NEEDS_DATA',f.status==='candidate-found'?'Fixture match exists; confirmed starting lineup is not implied.':'Fixture match missing; lineup cannot be assessed.');
 set('UFI-301','NEEDS_DATA','Live score, match clock, and live event verification are not connected to this pre-match audit.');
 set('UFI-198',news?.status==='ok'?'ASSESSED':'NEEDS_DATA',news?.status==='ok'?'News search returned articles; headlines are clues, not verified injury reports.':'News feed did not return verifiable results.');
 set('UFI-381','ASSESSED','The audit permits HOLD / no-pick; a survivor label is not a guarantee.');
 set('UFI-389','ASSESSED','Unverified data is surfaced as NEEDS_DATA rather than silently passed.');
 const counts=checks.reduce((a,c)=>(a[c.status]=(a[c.status]||0)+1,a),{});
 return {version:'UFI 400-signal registry v1',total:checks.length,counts,coveragePercent:Math.round(100*(counts.ASSESSED||0)/checks.length),failures:checks.filter(c=>c.status==='FAIL'),needsData:checks.filter(c=>c.status==='NEEDS_DATA'),assessed:checks.filter(c=>c.status==='ASSESSED'),checks};
}
window.UFI400Audit={version:'UFI 400-signal registry v1',features,evaluate};
})();

(function(){
'use strict';
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const pct=x=>(100*x).toFixed(2)+'%';
function evaluate(){
 const out=$('beOutput');
 try{
  const minute=Number($('beMinute').value),maxAge=Number($('beMaxAge').value),minEV=Number($('beMinEV').value)/100,maxUncertainty=Number($('beMaxUncertainty').value)/100,minSamples=Number($('beMinSamples').value),minSnapshots=Number($('beMinSnapshots').value),minProb=Number($('beMinProbability').value)/100,minEdge=Number($('beMinEdge').value)/100;
  if(!Number.isFinite(minute)||minute<0||minute>130||!Number.isFinite(maxAge)||maxAge<1||![minEV,maxUncertainty,minSamples,minSnapshots,minProb,minEdge].every(Number.isFinite))throw Error('Check the gate settings; values must be numeric and valid.');
  const candidates=JSON.parse($('beCandidates').value||'[]');
  if(!Array.isArray(candidates)||!candidates.length)throw Error('Enter at least one candidate.');
  const now=Date.now(),elapsedSeconds=Math.min(5400,Math.max(0,Math.floor(Math.min(minute,90)*60))),timeWeight=elapsedSeconds/5400;
  const results=candidates.map((c,i)=>{
   const reasons=[];
   const odds=Number(c.odds),p=Number(c.modelProbability),market=Number(c.marketProbability),unc=Number(c.uncertainty),samples=Number(c.validationMatches),snaps=Number(c.snapshots);
   const sourceMs=Date.parse(c.sourceUpdatedAt||'');
   const valid=Number.isFinite(odds)&&odds>1&&Number.isFinite(p)&&p>0&&p<1&&Number.isFinite(market)&&market>=0&&market<=1&&Number.isFinite(unc)&&unc>=0&&unc<=1&&Number.isFinite(samples)&&samples>=0&&Number.isFinite(snaps)&&snaps>=0&&Number.isFinite(sourceMs);
   if(!valid)reasons.push('Missing/invalid odds, probabilities, uncertainty, validation counts or source timestamp');
   const age=valid?(now-sourceMs)/1000:Infinity;
   if(valid&&age< -5)reasons.push('Provider source timestamp is in the future');
   if(valid&&age>maxAge)reasons.push('Stale source odds ('+Math.round(age)+' seconds old)');
   if(valid&&samples<minSamples)reasons.push('Insufficient independent out-of-sample validation matches');
   if(valid&&snaps<minSnapshots)reasons.push('Too few distinct timestamped market snapshots');
   if(valid&&unc>maxUncertainty)reasons.push('Probability uncertainty exceeds limit');
   if(valid&&p<minProb)reasons.push('Model probability below configured floor');
   const conservativeP=valid?Math.max(0,p-1.645*unc):0;
   const conservativeEV=valid?conservativeP*odds-1:-1;
   const conservativeEdge=valid?conservativeP-market:-1;
   // User-requested 5400-second time-scaled margin score. This is a heuristic,
   // not evidence that probability calibration improves as time elapses.
   const impliedProbability=valid?1/odds:1;
   const marginScore=valid?(p/impliedProbability)*(1+timeWeight):0;
   if(valid&&marginScore<1.05)reasons.push('5400 margin gate failed: time-scaled margin score '+marginScore.toFixed(3)+' is below 1.050');
   if(valid&&conservativeEV<minEV)reasons.push('Conservative net EV '+pct(conservativeEV)+' below required '+pct(minEV));
   if(valid&&conservativeEdge<minEdge)reasons.push('Conservative edge versus supplied market probability '+(conservativeEdge*100).toFixed(2)+'pp below threshold');
   const survive=reasons.length===0;
   return {selection:c.selection||('Candidate '+(i+1)),odds,modelProbability:p,marketProbability:market,uncertainty:unc,conservativeP,conservativeEV,conservativeEdge,marginScore,age,survive,reasons};
  });
  const approved=results.filter(x=>x.survive).length;
  const rows=results.map(r=>'<tr><td>'+esc(r.selection)+'</td><td>'+esc(Number.isFinite(r.odds)?r.odds.toFixed(2):'—')+'</td><td>'+esc(Number.isFinite(r.modelProbability)?pct(r.modelProbability):'—')+'</td><td>'+esc(Number.isFinite(r.conservativeP)?pct(r.conservativeP):'—')+'</td><td>'+esc(Number.isFinite(r.marginScore)?r.marginScore.toFixed(3):'—')+'</td><td>'+esc(Number.isFinite(r.conservativeEV)?pct(r.conservativeEV):'—')+'</td><td>'+esc(Number.isFinite(r.conservativeEdge)?(r.conservativeEdge*100).toFixed(2)+'pp':'—')+'</td><td><b>'+(r.survive?'SURVIVES CURRENT GATE':'DISCARD')+'</b><br>'+esc(r.reasons.join('; ')||'All configured filters passed on this evaluation only')+'</td></tr>').join('');
  out.innerHTML='<b>'+approved+' / '+results.length+' candidates pass the current gate.</b><br>Match clock: '+minute+'′ · 5400-second frame: '+elapsedSeconds+' elapsed seconds of 5400 (90 minutes); time weight '+timeWeight.toFixed(3)+'.<p class="muted">The time-scaled margin score is a heuristic and does not itself increase the model’s true probability. Conservative EV, edge, freshness and validation checks remain mandatory. This evaluates supplied data once; it cannot prove 5400 future observations will pass. Re-run when fresh source data arrives; repeated unchanged inputs are not independent evidence.</p><div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Selection</th><th>Odds</th><th>Model P</th><th>Conservative P</th><th>5400 Margin Score</th><th>Conservative EV</th><th>Edge</th><th>Decision / reason</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+rows+'</tbody></table></div>';
 }catch(e){out.innerHTML='<b>Cannot approve any selection.</b><br>'+esc(e.message);}
}
function example(){
 const ts=new Date().toISOString();
 $('beCandidates').value=JSON.stringify([
  {selection:'Example A — strong hypothetical',odds:2.5,modelProbability:0.5,marketProbability:0.38,uncertainty:0.015,sourceUpdatedAt:ts,validationMatches:600,snapshots:5},
  {selection:'Example B — weak hypothetical',odds:3.1,modelProbability:0.3,marketProbability:0.32,uncertainty:0.06,sourceUpdatedAt:ts,validationMatches:600,snapshots:5},
  {selection:'Example C — stale price',odds:4.5,modelProbability:0.28,marketProbability:0.21,uncertainty:0.02,sourceUpdatedAt:'2020-01-01T00:00:00Z',validationMatches:900,snapshots:6}
 ],null,2);
}
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{
 if(!$('beRun'))return;
 $('beRun').addEventListener('click',evaluate);
 $('beExample').addEventListener('click',example);
 $('beClear').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('beCandidates').value='';$('beOutput').textContent='Cleared. No candidate is approved by default.'});
});
})();

/* UFI calculator/risk integration v1.0 — deterministic maths; unknown evidence stays unknown. */
(()=>{'use strict';

function evaluateSlip(rows,stake){

 return {version:'UFI calculator-risk integration v1.0',generatedAt:new Date().toISOString(),rows:rows.length,pricedSelections:priced.length,unpricedSelections:unpriced,combinedOdds:combined,combinedImpliedProbability:combined===null?null:1/combined,stake:Number(stake)||null,potentialReturn:combined!==null&&Number(stake)>0?Number(stake)*combined:null,potentialProfit:combined!==null&&Number(stake)>0?Number(stake)*(combined-1):null,exactDuplicateLabelCount:duplicateCount,checks,registry:{total:registry.length,results:signalResults,counts:{pass:signalResults.filter(x=>x.status==='PASS').length,review:signalResults.filter(x=>x.status==='REVIEW').length,needsData:signalResults.filter(x=>x.status==='NEEDS_DATA').length}},limitations:['Odds-implied probability is not a calibrated prediction.','Market margin requires every outcome from the same market.','Arbitrage requires comparable prices across books and settlement validation.','Registry checks marked NEEDS_DATA do not count as passed.']};
}
window.UFICalculatorRisk={evaluateSlip};
})();

/* Crainee Background Engine: Match-Can-Break-This-Way Simulator.
 * Additive scenario signal engine. Rupture scores are heuristic indices, not probabilities.
 */
'use strict';
(function(root){
 const PITCH_TOTAL_UNITS=5400;
 const clamp=(n,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,n));
 function number(v,fallback,min,max,name){
  const n=v===undefined||v===null||v===''?fallback:Number(v);
  if(!Number.isFinite(n)||n<min||n>max)throw new TypeError(name+' must be between '+min+' and '+max);
  return n;
 }
 function simulateMatchBreakScenarios(matchData={}){
  const grid=number(matchData.ballPositionUnit,2700,0,PITCH_TOTAL_UNITS,'ballPositionUnit');
  const underdog=matchData.underdog||{};
  const favorite=matchData.favorite||{};
  const environment=matchData.environment||{};
  const referee=matchData.referee||{};
  const headStart=matchData.underdogScoredFirst===true;
  const headStartMultiplier=headStart?1.6:1.0;
  const errorRate=number(favorite.defenderErrorTendency,7,1,10,'favorite.defenderErrorTendency');
  const transition=number(underdog.attackerTransitionPacing,8,1,10,'underdog.attackerTransitionPacing');
  const exploitation=(errorRate*0.1)*(transition*0.1)*headStartMultiplier;
  const weather=environment.isHeavyRainOrWind===true?1.3:1.0;
  const whistle=number(referee.whistleFrequencyChaos,1.2,0.5,2.5,'referee.whistleFrequencyChaos');
  const environmental=weather*whistle;
  const spatial=(grid/PITCH_TOTAL_UNITS)*environmental;
  const rupture=exploitation*spatial*2.5;
  const threshold=number(matchData.breakThreshold,2.0,0.1,20,'breakThreshold');
  const broken=rupture>=threshold;
  const paths=[
   {id:'underdog-head-start',active:headStart,label:'Underdog scores first',description:'The favourite must chase; transition space may open.'},
   {id:'defensive-error-chain',active:errorRate>=7&&transition>=7,label:'Defensive error chain',description:'A defensive mistake meets a fast transition attack.'},
   {id:'weather-friction',active:weather>1,label:'Heavy rain or wind',description:'Conditions may disrupt passing rhythm.'},
   {id:'referee-disruption',active:whistle>=1.5,label:'High whistle disruption',description:'Frequent stoppages may break match rhythm.'},
   {id:'spatial-fracture',active:grid>=3600,label:'Advanced pitch position',description:'Ball position is in the attacking/final-third range of the 5400-unit grid.'}
  ];
  const outcomeCandidates=broken?
   (headStart?[{score:'0-2',label:'Underdog shock win'},{score:'0-3',label:'Underdog blowout'},{score:'1-3',label:'Open upset'}]:[{score:'1-2',label:'Late collapse'},{score:'0-2',label:'Favourite collapse'},{score:'2-3',label:'Chaotic high-scoring upset'}]):
   [{score:'0-0',label:'Low-event draw'},{score:'1-1',label:'Level match'},{score:'1-0 / 0-1',label:'Narrow result'}];
  return {
   engine:'Crainee Background Engine: Match-Can-Break-This-Way Simulator',version:1,
   simulationStatus:broken?'MATCH_STRUCTURE_BREAK_SIGNAL':'SCRIPT_NOT_BROKEN_BY_THIS_HEURISTIC',
   pitchGridCoordinate:grid+' / '+PITCH_TOTAL_UNITS,
   underdogHeadStart:headStart,headStartMultiplier:Number(headStartMultiplier.toFixed(3)),
   errorExploitationScore:Number(exploitation.toFixed(4)),
   environmentalChaosFactor:Number(environmental.toFixed(4)),
   spatialFractureIndex:Number(spatial.toFixed(4)),
   ruptureIndex:Number(rupture.toFixed(4)),breakThreshold:threshold,
   scenarioPaths:paths,exactScoreExploration:outcomeCandidates,
   interpretation:broken?'A structural-break scenario is flagged for exploration; this is not a prediction or a calibrated upset probability.':'This input combination did not cross the heuristic threshold; unexpected scores remain possible.',
   confidence:Number(clamp((rupture/threshold)*100,0,100).toFixed(1)),
   confidenceStatus:'HEURISTIC_SIGNAL_PERCENT_NOT_CALIBRATED_PROBABILITY',
   confidenceIsProbability:false,
   limitations:[
    'The confidence percentage is retained as a heuristic signal-strength indicator derived from ruptureIndex / breakThreshold, capped at 100; it is not the chance of an upset and is not calibrated.',
    'A head start is only active when underdogScoredFirst is explicitly true; missing input is not silently treated as true.',
    'A scoreline cannot be inferred reliably from these inputs alone.',
    'Weather, referee and player values must be supplied; this module does not imply automatic live-data feeds.',
    'The 5400-unit coordinate is a simplified position signal, not full pitch tracking.'
   ]
  };
 }
 const api={simulateMatchBreakScenarios,PITCH_TOTAL_UNITS};
 if(typeof module!=='undefined'&&module.exports)module.exports=api;
 root.CraineeBackgroundBreakEngine=api;
})(typeof globalThis!=='undefined'?globalThis:this);

(function(){
'use strict';
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const money=n=>'₦'+Number(n).toLocaleString('en-NG',{maximumFractionDigits:2});
function squadScore(players){
 if(!Array.isArray(players)||players.length===0)throw Error('Each team needs a non-empty players JSON array.');
 let total=0,assumptions=[];
 players.forEach((p,i)=>{
  if(!p||typeof p!=='object')throw Error('Player row '+(i+1)+' must be an object.');
  const marketValue=p.marketValue==null?1000000:Number(p.marketValue);
  const age=p.age==null?26:Number(p.age);
  const caps=p.caps==null?30:Number(p.caps);
  if(!Number.isFinite(marketValue)||marketValue<0||!Number.isFinite(age)||age<15||age>60||!Number.isFinite(caps)||caps<0)throw Error('Invalid marketValue, age or caps for player row '+(i+1)+'.');
  if(p.marketValue==null||p.age==null||p.caps==null)assumptions.push((p.name||('Player '+(i+1)))+': defaulted '+[p.marketValue==null?'marketValue':'',p.age==null?'age':'',p.caps==null?'caps':''].filter(Boolean).join(', '));
  const ageWeight=Math.exp(-0.015*Math.pow(age-27,2));
  const expWeight=Math.log10(caps+10);
  total+=marketValue*ageWeight*expWeight;
 });
 return {score:total,assumptions};
}
function evaluate(){
 const out=$('caOutput');
 try{
  const currentRaw=$('caCurrentSecond').value,bankrollRaw=$('caBankroll').value;
  if(currentRaw===''||bankrollRaw==='')throw Error('Enter elapsed seconds and bankroll (use 0 if you do not want a stake cap).');
  const current=Number(currentRaw),bankroll=Number(bankrollRaw);
  if(!Number.isFinite(current)||current<0||current>6000)throw Error('Elapsed seconds must be between 0 and 6000, including stoppage time.');
  if(!Number.isFinite(bankroll)||bankroll<0)throw Error('Bankroll must be zero or greater.');
  const home=squadScore(JSON.parse($('caHomeSquad').value||'null')),away=squadScore(JSON.parse($('caAwaySquad').value||'null'));
  const referee=JSON.parse($('caReferee').value||'{}');
  const first=referee.avgWhistleGap1to45==null?120:Number(referee.avgWhistleGap1to45);
  const second=referee.avgWhistleGap45to90==null?85:Number(referee.avgWhistleGap45to90);
  if(!Number.isFinite(first)||first<=0||!Number.isFinite(second)||second<=0)throw Error('Referee whistle gaps must be positive numbers in seconds.');
  const elapsed=Math.min(MATCH_TOTAL_SECONDS,current),isSecondHalf=current>HALF_TIME_SECONDS;
  const activeGap=isSecondHalf?second:first,flow=Math.min(1.2,activeGap/100);
  const assets=home.score+away.score;
  const power=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Math.log10(assets+1)/15));
  const timeWeight=elapsed/MATCH_TOTAL_SECONDS;
  const composite=power*flow*(1+timeWeight);
  const phase=isSecondHalf?'Second Half (45m - 90m)':'First Half (1m - 45m)';
  const common='<p>Elapsed time: '+Math.floor(current/60)+' minutes ('+current+'s; regulation frame '+elapsed+'/5400s). Phase: '+phase+'. Active average whistle gap: '+activeGap+' seconds.</p><p>Squad asset score: home '+home.score.toFixed(0)+'; away '+away.score.toFixed(0)+'. Normalized squad power: '+power.toFixed(3)+'. Referee flow factor: '+flow.toFixed(3)+'. Time weight: '+timeWeight.toFixed(3)+'.</p>';
  if(composite<0.25){
   out.innerHTML='<b>DISCARDED — composite score below 0.250.</b><p>Score: '+composite.toFixed(3)+'. The supplied squad/referee/time heuristic did not meet the experimental threshold.</p>'+common;
   return;
  }
  const riskPct=Math.min(0.05,Math.max(0,0.05*composite));
  const stake=bankroll*riskPct;
  const assumptions=home.assumptions.concat(away.assumptions);
  out.innerHTML='<b>Survived current heuristic gate — not a betting approval.</b><p>Composite score: '+composite.toFixed(3)+' (threshold 0.250).</p>'+common+'<p>Heuristic stake cap: '+(riskPct*100).toFixed(2)+'% of entered bankroll = '+money(stake)+'. Hard ceiling is 5%; if bankroll is zero, cap is ₦0.</p>'+(assumptions.length?'<p><b>Defaulted inputs:</b> '+esc(assumptions.join('; '))+'</p>':'<p>All player market values, ages and caps were supplied.</p>')+'<p class="muted">This score is not a calibrated win probability or measured positive expected value. It does not account for player minutes, injuries, replacement quality, league/market calibration, or actual referee event distributions. A larger squad valuation does not automatically mean a bet has an edge.</p>';
 }catch(e){out.innerHTML='<b>Cannot clear match.</b><br>'+esc(e.message);}
}

 const current=Number(matchData.currentSecond||0);
 if(!Array.isArray(matchData.homeTeam?.players)||!Array.isArray(matchData.awayTeam?.players)||!matchData.referee)throw Error('Provide homeTeam.players, awayTeam.players and referee.');
 function calc(players){if(!Array.isArray(players)||players.length===0)throw Error('Each team requires a non-empty players array');return players.reduce((acc,p)=>{const value=p.marketValue==null?1000000:Number(p.marketValue),age=p.age==null?26:Number(p.age),caps=p.caps==null?30:Number(p.caps);if(!Number.isFinite(value)||value<0||!Number.isFinite(age)||age<15||age>60||!Number.isFinite(caps)||caps<0)throw Error('Invalid player attributes');return acc+value*Math.exp(-0.015*Math.pow(age-27,2))*Math.log10(caps+10)},0)}

/* Crainee Subconscious Neural Databank & Fan Narrative Engine. Heuristic scenarios only. */
'use strict';
(function(root){
 const PITCH_TOTAL_UNITS=5400, STEP_UNITS=180, ZONES=['DEFENSE','MIDFIELD','ATTACK'];
 const clamp=(n,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,n));
 function finite(v,d,min,max,name){const n=v===undefined||v===null||v===''?d:Number(v);if(!Number.isFinite(n)||n<min||n>max)throw new TypeError(name+' must be a finite number between '+min+' and '+max);return n;}
 function line(s,z,side){const x=(s&&s[z])||{};return {height:finite(x.height,180,140,220,side+'.'+z+'.height'),composure:finite(x.composure,7,1,10,side+'.'+z+'.composure'),footSkill:finite(x.footSkill,5,0,10,side+'.'+z+'.footSkill'),styleFit:finite(x.styleFit,5,0,10,side+'.'+z+'.styleFit')};}
 function runPositionalMicroSimulations(data={}){
  if(!data||typeof data!=='object'||Array.isArray(data))throw new TypeError('matchData must be an object');
  const home=data.homeSquad||{},away=data.awaySquad||{},out=[];
  for(let unit=0;unit<=PITCH_TOTAL_UNITS;unit+=STEP_UNITS){
   const zone=unit<=1800?'DEFENSE':unit<=3600?'MIDFIELD':'ATTACK',h=line(home,zone,'homeSquad'),a=line(away,zone,'awaySquad');
   const hp=h.height/20+h.composure+h.footSkill+h.styleFit,ap=a.height/20+a.composure+a.footSkill+a.styleFit,friction=Math.abs(hp-ap);
   out.push({unit,zone,scoreline:clamp(Math.floor(hp/8+(friction%2)),0,5)+':'+clamp(Math.floor(ap/8+(friction%3)),0,5),friction:Number(friction.toFixed(4)),homeLinePower:Number(hp.toFixed(4)),awayLinePower:Number(ap.toFixed(4))});
  }return out;
 }
 function applyRealitySupervisor(candidates,isHomeFavorite=true,options={}){
  if(!Array.isArray(candidates))throw new TypeError('candidates must be an array');
  const maxH=finite(options.maxHomeGoals,5,0,20,'maxHomeGoals'),maxA=finite(options.maxAwayGoals,4,0,20,'maxAwayGoals'),base=isHomeFavorite?{home:2,away:1}:{home:1,away:1},out=[];
  for(const c of candidates){if(!c||typeof c.scoreline!=='string')continue;const m=c.scoreline.match(/^(\d+):(\d+)$/);if(!m)continue;const h=+m[1]+base.home,a=+m[2]+base.away;
   if(h<=maxH&&a<=maxA&&h+a>0)out.push({rawZone:c.zone,unit:c.unit,finalAdjustedScore:h+':'+a,frictionScore:finite(c.friction,0,0,10000,'friction'),baseVector:base,rawScoreline:c.scoreline});}
  const seen=new Set(),diverse=[];for(const c of out){const k=c.finalAdjustedScore+'|'+c.rawZone;if(!seen.has(k)){seen.add(k);diverse.push(c);}}
  return diverse.slice(0,finite(options.limit,20,1,200,'limit'));
 }
 function calculateCrossDomainSignals(signalSequences=[]){
  if(!Array.isArray(signalSequences))throw new TypeError('signalSequences must be an array');
  return signalSequences.map((entry,index)=>{
   const values=Array.isArray(entry)?entry:(entry&&Array.isArray(entry.values)?entry.values:null);
   if(!values||values.length<2||values.some(v=>!Number.isFinite(Number(v))))throw new TypeError('each cross-domain signal needs at least two finite numeric values');
   const nums=values.map(Number),mean=nums.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/nums.length,variance=nums.reduce((s,v)=>s+(v-mean)**2,0)/nums.length;
   const scale=Math.max(Math.abs(mean),Math.sqrt(variance),1e-9),momentum=(nums[nums.length-1]-nums[0])/scale;
   const volatility=Math.sqrt(variance)/scale,meanReversion=Math.abs(nums[nums.length-1]-mean)/scale;
   return {index,label:entry&&entry.label?String(entry.label):'numeric-sequence-'+(index+1),samples:nums.length,momentum:Number(momentum.toFixed(4)),volatility:Number(volatility.toFixed(4)),meanReversion:Number(meanReversion.toFixed(4)),patternSignal:Number(clamp((Math.abs(momentum)+volatility+meanReversion)/3,0,2).toFixed(4)),interpretation:'Generic numeric-pattern features only; source-domain relevance to football must be validated.'};
  });
 }
 function buildFanReactionCandidates(scoreline,conditions,homeGoals,awayGoals){
  const result=homeGoals===awayGoals?'DRAW':homeGoals>awayGoals?'HOME_WIN':'AWAY_WIN';
  const missing=conditions.missingKeyPlayers||[],homeName=conditions.homeTeamName||'Home team',awayName=conditions.awayTeamName||'Away team';
  const reactions=[];
  const add=(team,theme,text,fit)=>reactions.push({team,theme,text,fit:Number(clamp(fit,0,1).toFixed(3))});
  if(result==='DRAW'){add('both','tight-game','Both fan bases discuss a close tactical battle and missed chances.',0.7);add('both','missed-opportunity','Supporters ask why their team failed to turn chances into a win.',0.65);}
  else {const winner=result==='HOME_WIN'?homeName:awayName,loser=result==='HOME_WIN'?awayName:homeName;
   add(winner,'dominance',winner+' fans celebrate a deserved or clinical win.',0.62);
   add(loser,'referee-complaint',loser+' fans question refereeing decisions.',conditions.refereeDisruption>1.2?0.9:0.42);
   add(loser,'defensive-error',loser+' fans blame a defensive lapse or transition failure.',conditions.defensiveErrorSignal>0.5?0.9:0.58);
   add(loser,'finishing',loser+' fans blame missed chances or poor finishing.',0.62);
   if(missing.length)add(loser,'missing-starters',loser+' fans point to unavailable players: '+missing.join(', ')+'.',0.95);
   if(conditions.heavyRain)add(loser,'weather',loser+' fans say weather and pitch conditions disrupted the game.',0.82);
   if(conditions.homeAwayStreak)add(loser,'streak',loser+' fans point to the team streak: '+String(conditions.homeAwayStreak)+'.',0.72);
   if(conditions.coachPressure)add(loser,'coach',loser+' fans question the coach and substitutions.',0.72);
  }
  if(Array.isArray(conditions.fanNarratives))for(const n of conditions.fanNarratives){if(n&&typeof n.text==='string')add(n.team||'both',n.theme||'custom',n.text,finite(n.fit,0.5,0,1,'fanNarrative.fit'));}
  return reactions.map(r=>({...r,scoreline,matchContextFit:Number((r.fit*(conditions.heavyRain?1.08:1)*(conditions.missingKeyPlayers&&conditions.missingKeyPlayers.length&&r.theme==='missing-starters'?1.12:1)).toFixed(4))}));
 }
 function matchFanNarrative(candidates,conditions={}){
  if(!Array.isArray(candidates))throw new TypeError('candidates must be an array');
  const missing=conditions.missingKeyPlayers===undefined?[]:conditions.missingKeyPlayers;if(!Array.isArray(missing)||missing.some(x=>typeof x!=='string'))throw new TypeError('missingKeyPlayers must be an array of names');
  const impacts={missingKeyPlayers:missing.length?1.4:1,weather:conditions.heavyRain===true?1.2:1,referee:finite(conditions.refereeDisruption,1,0.5,2.5,'refereeDisruption')};
  const crossDomain=calculateCrossDomainSignals(conditions.crossDomainSignals||[]);
  return candidates.map(c=>{const m=String(c.finalAdjustedScore).match(/^(\d+):(\d+)$/);if(!m)throw new TypeError('invalid finalAdjustedScore');const h=+m[1],a=+m[2];let narrative;
   if(h<a&&missing.length)narrative='Post-match narrative hypothesis: fans may cite absent key starters ('+missing.join(', ')+').';
   else if(h===a)narrative='Post-match narrative hypothesis: both fan bases may describe a tight tactical deadlock.';
   else if(h<a)narrative='Post-match narrative hypothesis: home-side fans may discuss defensive or finishing failures.';
   else narrative='Post-match narrative hypothesis: winning-side fans may describe a dominant performance.';
   const reactions=buildFanReactionCandidates(c.finalAdjustedScore,conditions,h,a);
   const bestReaction=reactions.slice().sort((x,y)=>y.matchContextFit-x.matchContextFit)[0]||{team:'both',theme:'general',text:narrative,matchContextFit:0.5};
   const contradiction=Number(clamp((Math.abs(h-a)/Math.max(h+a,1))*0.25+(conditions.defensiveErrorSignal?clamp(Number(conditions.defensiveErrorSignal),0,1)*0.25:0)+(conditions.tacticalShiftSignal?clamp(Number(conditions.tacticalShiftSignal),0,1)*0.25:0),0,1).toFixed(4));
   const crossSignal=crossDomain.length?crossDomain.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.patternSignal,0)/crossDomain.length:0;
   const quality=Number((c.frictionScore*impacts.missingKeyPlayers*impacts.weather*impacts.referee + bestReaction.matchContextFit*10 + contradiction*5 + crossSignal).toFixed(2));
   return {scoreline:c.finalAdjustedScore,qualityScore:quality,expectedFanReaction:bestReaction.text,fanReactionCandidates:reactions,selectedFanReaction:bestReaction,zoneOrigin:c.rawZone,unit:c.unit,frictionScore:c.frictionScore,contradictionSignal:contradiction,crossDomainSignals:crossDomain,contextFactors:impacts,interpretation:'Fan reaction fit materially contributes to ranking as requested; it is a heuristic, not evidence of a calibrated probability.'};
  }).sort((a,b)=>b.qualityScore-a.qualityScore);
 }
 function runPipeline(data={},conditions={},isHomeFavorite=true,options={}){const raw=runPositionalMicroSimulations(data),realistic=applyRealitySupervisor(raw,isHomeFavorite,options),ranked=matchFanNarrative(realistic,conditions);
  return {engine:'Crainee Subconscious Neural Databank & Fan Narrative Engine',version:2,pitchUnits:PITCH_TOTAL_UNITS,stepUnits:STEP_UNITS,positionsEvaluated:raw.length,rawPositionalCandidates:raw,realisticCandidates:realistic,rankedCandidates:ranked,selectedScorelines:ranked.slice(0,finite(options.finalLimit,5,1,50,'finalLimit')),confidence:null,confidenceStatus:'NOT_ESTIMATED',caveats:['No actual player tracking, biometric, sentiment corpus or live feed is connected by this module.','The 5400 grid is sampled every 180 units; it is not a microsecond simulation or measured pitch tracking.','Equal line treatment uses the same feature formula for all zones; this is a heuristic, not a trained neural network.','Fan narrative is a descriptive hypothesis, not predictive evidence or a probability.','Validate on chronological held-out matches against simple baselines before claiming predictive improvement.']};
 }
 const api={PITCH_TOTAL_UNITS,STEP_UNITS,runPositionalMicroSimulations,applyRealitySupervisor,calculateCrossDomainSignals,buildFanReactionCandidates,matchFanNarrative,runPipeline};if(typeof module!=='undefined'&&module.exports)module.exports=api;root.CraineeSubconsciousNarrativeEngine=api;
})(typeof globalThis!=='undefined'?globalThis:this);
