/* Crainee Tactical Surprise Engine — additive, evidence-aware heuristic.
 * Scores tactical mismatch signals; it does NOT output calibrated probabilities.
 */
'use strict';
const PITCH_TOTAL_UNITS = 5400;
const MATCH_TOTAL_SECONDS = 5400;
const clamp = (n, lo, hi) => Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, n));
const num = (v, fallback, lo, hi) => {
  const n = v === undefined || v === null || v === '' ? fallback : Number(v);
  if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < lo || n > hi) throw new TypeError(`Expected number in [${lo}, ${hi}]`);
  return n;
};
function evaluateSpatialTacticalSurprise(matchData = {}) {
  const ball = num(matchData.ballPositionUnit, 2700, 0, PITCH_TOTAL_UNITS);
  const home = matchData.homeTeam || {}, away = matchData.awayTeam || {};
  const attack = matchData.attackingTeam || home, defense = matchData.defendingTeam || away;
  let zone, shear;
  if (ball <= 1800) {
    zone = 'Defensive Zone (0–1800)';
    shear = num(attack.forwardComposure, 8, 1, 10) / num(defense.pressIntensity, 7, 1, 10);
  } else if (ball <= 3600) {
    zone = 'Midfield Zone (1800–3600)';
    shear = Math.abs(num(home.midfieldRating, 6, 1, 10) - num(away.midfieldRating, 6, 1, 10)) + 1;
  } else {
    zone = 'Attacking / Final Third (3600–5400)';
    shear = num(attack.creativity, 8, 1, 10) / num(defense.lowBlockRating, 5, 1, 10);
  }
  const stretch = 1 + Math.abs(ball - 2700) / 2700;
  const score = shear * stretch;
  const passed = score >= 1.4;
  return {status: passed ? 'SPATIAL_EDGE_FLAGGED' : 'DISCARDED_SPATIALLY', pitchZone: zone,
    ballGridUnit: `${ball} / ${PITCH_TOTAL_UNITS}`, zoneShearFactor: round(shear),
    spatialStretchingMultiplier: round(stretch), spatialSurpriseScore: round(score),
    reason: passed ? 'Spatial mismatch signal flagged for review; not a probability.' : 'Spatial score below heuristic threshold.',
    limitations: ['A one-dimensional 0–5400 coordinate is not full player tracking.', 'Ratings are supplied or defaulted; this score is not calibrated.']};
}
function evaluateTacticalSurpriseEdge(matchData = {}) {
  const second = num(matchData.currentSecond, 0, 0, MATCH_TOTAL_SECONDS);
  const opponent = matchData.opponent || {}, attack = matchData.attackingTeam || {};
  const line = num(opponent.defensiveLineHeight, 5, 1, 10);
  const speed = num(attack.transitionSpeed, 5, 1, 10);
  const spaceBehind = line * speed / 100;
  const winger = num(attack.wingerDribbleRating, 7, 1, 10);
  const fullback = num(opponent.fullbackDefenseRating, 5, 1, 10);
  const fullbackMismatch = clamp(winger / fullback, 0.5, 2);
  const time = second / MATCH_TOTAL_SECONDS;
  const fatigue = Math.exp(0.7 * time); // deliberately restrained, bounded by match time
  const freshness = num(attack.substitutesFreshness, 1, 0.5, 2);
  const opponentFatigue = num(opponent.defenderFatigue, 1, 0.5, 2);
  const shock = freshness * opponentFatigue * fatigue;
  const score = spaceBehind * fullbackMismatch * shock * (1 + time);
  const passed = score >= 0.45;
  return {status: passed ? 'TACTICAL_EDGE_FLAGGED' : 'DISCARDED_TACTICALLY',
    currentMatchTime: `${Math.floor(second / 60)}m / 90m (${second}s)`,
    spaceBehindFactor: round(spaceBehind), fullbackMismatchFactor: round(fullbackMismatch),
    fatigueMultiplier: round(fatigue), substitutionFatigueFactor: round(shock),
    tacticalSurpriseScore: round(score),
    exploitType: line >= 7 && speed >= 7 ? 'High-line space exploitation' :
      winger > fullback ? 'Winger/fullback mismatch' : second >= 3600 ? 'Late-game fatigue window' : 'No dominant single pattern',
    reason: passed ? 'Tactical mismatch signal flagged for review; no confidence percentage inferred.' : 'Tactical score below heuristic threshold.',
    limitations: ['Requires reliable tactical/player inputs; no automatic tracking feed is implied.', 'Score is not an upset probability.']};
}
function evaluateChaosAndStyleMatchup(matchData = {}) {
  const ball = num(matchData.ballPositionUnit, 2700, 0, PITCH_TOTAL_UNITS);
  const teamA = matchData.teamA || {name:'Team A', players:[]}, teamB = matchData.teamB || {name:'Team B', players:[]};
  const allowed = new Set(['Chaos_Dribbler','Counter_Press_Monster']);
  function friction(players, opponents) {
    if (!Array.isArray(players) || !Array.isArray(opponents)) throw new TypeError('team players must be arrays');
    let total = 0;
    for (const p of players) {
      if (!p || typeof p.style !== 'string') continue;
      if (p.style === 'Chaos_Dribbler' && opponents.some(o => o && o.style === 'Slow_Defender')) total += 2.5;
      if (p.style === 'Counter_Press_Monster' && opponents.some(o => o && o.style === 'Deep_Playmaker')) total += 2;
    }
    return total;
  }
  const a = friction(teamA.players || [], teamB.players || []);
  const b = friction(teamB.players || [], teamA.players || []);
  const volatility = Math.abs(ball - 2700) / 2700;
  const raw = a - b;
  const solved = Math.sign(raw) * Math.pow(Math.abs(raw), 1.5) * (1 + volatility);
  const passed = Math.abs(solved) >= 3;
  return {status: passed ? 'STYLE_MISMATCH_FLAGGED' : 'NORMAL_LINEAR_MATCH',
    teamAStyleFriction: round(a), teamBStyleFriction: round(b), gridVolatility: round(volatility),
    chaosIndex: round(solved), potentialAdvantageSide: passed ? (solved > 0 ? 'teamA' : 'teamB') : null,
    potentialAdvantageTeam: passed ? (solved > 0 ? teamA.name : teamB.name) : null,
    reason: passed ? 'Style-friction anomaly flagged for tactical review; not a winner prediction.' : 'No style-friction anomaly crossed the heuristic threshold.',
    limitations: ['Player styles must be evidenced, not guessed.', 'Nonlinear transform and threshold are heuristic and need historical validation.']};
}
function evaluateTacticalSurprise(matchData = {}) {
  const spatial = evaluateSpatialTacticalSurprise(matchData);
  const tactical = evaluateTacticalSurpriseEdge(matchData);
  const styles = evaluateChaosAndStyleMatchup(matchData);
  const flagged = [spatial, tactical, styles].filter(x => /FLAGGED|LOCKED/.test(x.status)).length;
  return {engine:'Crainee Tactical Surprise Engine', flaggedSignals:flagged, spatial, tactical, styles,
    summary: flagged ? `${flagged} of 3 heuristic signal groups flagged; inspect the underlying evidence.` : 'No strong heuristic mismatch signal; this does not mean an upset is impossible.',
    forecastWarning:'These are heuristic mismatch scores, NOT calibrated probabilities or proof of value. Validate on chronological unseen matches before using for forecasts.'};
}
function round(n) { return Number(n.toFixed(3)); }
if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) module.exports = {evaluateSpatialTacticalSurprise,evaluateTacticalSurpriseEdge,evaluateChaosAndStyleMatchup,evaluateTacticalSurprise,PITCH_TOTAL_UNITS,MATCH_TOTAL_SECONDS};
if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
  window.CraineeTacticalSurprise = {evaluateSpatialTacticalSurprise,evaluateTacticalSurpriseEdge,evaluateChaosAndStyleMatchup,evaluateTacticalSurprise};
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    if (!document.querySelector('#view-tactical-surprise') || !document.querySelector('[data-view="tactical-surprise"]')) {
      const nav = document.querySelector('#navigation'), main = document.querySelector('.main');
      if (!nav || !main) return;
      const button = document.createElement('button'); button.className='nav-item'; button.dataset.view='tactical-surprise'; button.innerHTML='<span>⌖</span> Tactical Surprise'; nav.appendChild(button);
      const section=document.createElement('section'); section.className='view'; section.id='view-tactical-surprise';
      section.innerHTML='<div class="page-heading"><div><div class="eyebrow">ADDITIVE EXPERIMENTAL MODULE</div><h1>Crainee Tactical Surprise Engine</h1><p>Pitch grid, high-line transitions, winger/fullback mismatch, fatigue and player-style friction. Existing engines remain unchanged.</p></div></div><div class="panel"><div class="form-grid three"><label>Ball position (0–5400)<input id="ts-ball" type="number" min="0" max="5400" value="4500"></label><label>Match second (0–5400)<input id="ts-time" type="number" min="0" max="5400" value="4200"></label><label>High defensive line (1–10)<input id="ts-line" type="number" min="1" max="10" value="8"></label><label>Transition speed (1–10)<input id="ts-speed" type="number" min="1" max="10" value="9"></label><label>Winger dribbling (1–10)<input id="ts-winger" type="number" min="1" max="10" value="8"></label><label>Fullback defence (1–10)<input id="ts-fullback" type="number" min="1" max="10" value="5"></label><label>Attack creativity (1–10)<input id="ts-creativity" type="number" min="1" max="10" value="8"></label><label>Defensive low-block rating (1–10)<input id="ts-block" type="number" min="1" max="10" value="5"></label><label>Sub freshness (0.5–2)<input id="ts-fresh" type="number" min="0.5" max="2" step="0.1" value="1.2"></label><label>Defender fatigue (0.5–2)<input id="ts-fatigue" type="number" min="0.5" max="2" step="0.1" value="1.3"></label></div><p class="muted">Player styles are optional JSON arrays; enter only styles supported by evidence.</p><label>Team A players JSON<textarea id="ts-a" rows="3">[{"style":"Chaos_Dribbler"}]</textarea></label><label>Team B players JSON<textarea id="ts-b" rows="3">[{"style":"Slow_Defender"},{"style":"Deep_Playmaker"}]</textarea></label><div class="form-actions"><button class="button primary" id="ts-run">Evaluate tactical signals</button><button class="button ghost" id="ts-example">Load example</button></div><div id="ts-result" class="notice" aria-live="polite">Enter match evidence and evaluate. Defaults are illustrative only.</div></div>';
      main.appendChild(section);
      nav.addEventListener('click', e=>{const b=e.target.closest('[data-view="tactical-surprise"]');if(!b)return;document.querySelectorAll('.view').forEach(v=>v.classList.toggle('active',v.id==='view-tactical-surprise'));nav.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(n=>n.classList.toggle('active',n===b));});
      const get=id=>Number(document.getElementById(id).value);
      const run=()=>{try{const result=evaluateTacticalSurprise({ballPositionUnit:get('ts-ball'),currentSecond:get('ts-time'),attackingTeam:{transitionSpeed:get('ts-speed'),wingerDribbleRating:get('ts-winger'),creativity:get('ts-creativity'),substitutesFreshness:get('ts-fresh')},opponent:{defensiveLineHeight:get('ts-line'),fullbackDefenseRating:get('ts-fullback'),lowBlockRating:get('ts-block'),defenderFatigue:get('ts-fatigue')},defendingTeam:{lowBlockRating:get('ts-block')},homeTeam:{midfieldRating:6},awayTeam:{midfieldRating:6},teamA:{name:'Team A',players:JSON.parse(document.getElementById('ts-a').value)},teamB:{name:'Team B',players:JSON.parse(document.getElementById('ts-b').value)}});document.getElementById('ts-result').textContent=JSON.stringify(result,null,2);}catch(e){document.getElementById('ts-result').textContent='Input error: '+e.message;}};
      document.getElementById('ts-run').addEventListener('click',run);
      document.getElementById('ts-example').addEventListener('click',()=>{document.getElementById('ts-ball').value=4500;document.getElementById('ts-time').value=4200;document.getElementById('ts-line').value=8;document.getElementById('ts-speed').value=9;document.getElementById('ts-winger').value=8;document.getElementById('ts-fullback').value=5;document.getElementById('ts-a').value='[{"style":"Chaos_Dribbler"}]';document.getElementById('ts-b').value='[{"style":"Slow_Defender"},{"style":"Deep_Playmaker"}]';run();});
    }
  });
}

/* Crainee Unconventional Outcome Engine
 * Additive scenario explorer. Candidate exploration is deliberately independent of odds.
 * Scenario scores are diversity/coverage scores, NOT calibrated outcome probabilities.
 */
'use strict';
(function(root){
  const clamp=(n,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,n));
  function int(v, fallback, min, max, name){
    const n=v===undefined||v===null||v===''?fallback:Number(v);
    if(!Number.isInteger(n)||n<min||n>max) throw new TypeError(name+' must be an integer from '+min+' to '+max);
    return n;
  }
  function hashSeed(seed){
    let h=2166136261; for(const c of String(seed)){h^=c.charCodeAt(0);h=Math.imul(h,16777619);}
    return h>>>0;
  }
  function rng(seed){
    let state=hashSeed(seed)||0x6d2b79f5;
    return ()=>{state+=0x6D2B79F5;let t=state;t=Math.imul(t^(t>>>15),t|1);t^=t+Math.imul(t^(t>>>7),t|61);return ((t^(t>>>14))>>>0)/4294967296;};
  }
  const scoreKey=s=>s.home+'-'+s.away;
  function uniqueScores(items){
    const seen=new Set(); return items.filter(x=>{const k=scoreKey(x);if(seen.has(k))return false;seen.add(k);return true;});
  }
  function normalizeScore(s){
    if(!s||typeof s!=='object') throw new TypeError('score must be an object');
    return {home:int(s.home,0,0,15,'home goals'),away:int(s.away,0,0,15,'away goals')};
  }
  function generateUnconventionalOutcomes(input={}){
    const seed=input.seed===undefined?'crainee-'+Date.now():String(input.seed);
    if(seed.length>128) throw new TypeError('seed must be 128 characters or fewer');
    const count=int(input.candidateCount,12,4,40,'candidateCount');
    const main=normalizeScore(input.mainModelScore||{home:2,away:1});
    const random=rng(seed);
    const homeName=String(input.homeTeam||'Home').slice(0,80), awayName=String(input.awayTeam||'Away').slice(0,80);
    const maxGoals=int(input.maxGoals,5,2,10,'maxGoals');
    const scenarios=[
      {id:'early-underdog-goal',label:'Underdog scores early',description:'An early goal forces the favourite to chase and opens transition space.',homeBias:-0.45,awayBias:0.7,variance:1.0},
      {id:'red-card-disruption',label:'Red-card tactical break',description:'A sending-off disrupts the original shape; either side can exploit the extra space.',homeBias:0,awayBias:0,variance:1.65},
      {id:'favourite-dominates-blanks',label:'Favourite dominates but blanks',description:'Territory and chances do not become goals; a draw or narrow loss remains possible.',homeBias:-0.65,awayBias:-0.1,variance:0.75},
      {id:'underdog-clinical',label:'Underdog converts few chances',description:'The underdog scores efficiently from limited opportunities.',homeBias:-0.15,awayBias:0.85,variance:1.15},
      {id:'chaotic-open-game',label:'Open, chaotic match',description:'An early swing or broken structure produces a high-variance scoreline.',homeBias:0.1,awayBias:0.1,variance:1.8},
      {id:'late-equaliser',label:'Late equaliser',description:'A late push changes a narrow lead into a draw.',homeBias:0.15,awayBias:0.25,variance:0.9}
    ];
    const pool=[];
    // Anchor a wide score grid so unlikely outcomes aren't excluded by the favourite model.
    for(let h=0;h<=maxGoals;h++) for(let a=0;a<=maxGoals;a++){
      if(h===main.home&&a===main.away) continue;
      const distance=Math.abs(h-main.home)+Math.abs(a-main.away);
      const rarity=(h===0?0.55:0)+(a===0?0.55:0)+(h>=4||a>=4?0.65:0)+(h<a?0.35:0);
      pool.push({home:h,away:a,explorationScore:Number((distance+rarity+random()*0.8).toFixed(4)),distanceFromMain:distance,scenarioId:null,scenarioLabel:'Wide score-space exploration',scenarioDescription:'Generated outside the main model’s exact-score pick; not a probability estimate.'});
    }
    const scenarioScores=[];
    scenarios.forEach(sc=>{
      // Randomly perturb a broad goal baseline; seed fixes all draws and ordering.
      const baseH=clamp(Math.round(main.home+sc.homeBias+(random()-0.5)*sc.variance*4),0,maxGoals);
      const baseA=clamp(Math.round(main.away+sc.awayBias+(random()-0.5)*sc.variance*4),0,maxGoals);
      for(let j=0;j<3;j++){
        const h=clamp(baseH+Math.round((random()-0.5)*sc.variance*3),0,maxGoals);
        const a=clamp(baseA+Math.round((random()-0.5)*sc.variance*3),0,maxGoals);
        if(h===main.home&&a===main.away) continue;
        scenarioScores.push({home:h,away:a,explorationScore:Number((Math.abs(h-main.home)+Math.abs(a-main.away)+sc.variance+random()).toFixed(4)),distanceFromMain:Math.abs(h-main.home)+Math.abs(a-main.away),scenarioId:sc.id,scenarioLabel:sc.label,scenarioDescription:sc.description});
      }
    });
    const merged=uniqueScores(scenarioScores.concat(pool).sort((a,b)=>b.explorationScore-a.explorationScore));
    const unconventional=merged.slice(0,count).map((s,i)=>({...s,rank:i+1,score:scoreKey(s),homeTeam:homeName,awayTeam:awayName,classification:'EXPLORED_POSSIBILITY',probability:null,probabilityStatus:'NOT_ESTIMATED'}));
    return {engine:'Crainee Unconventional Outcome Engine',version:1,seed,seedHash:hashSeed(seed),reproducible:true,mainModelScore:{...main,score:scoreKey(main)},homeTeam:homeName,awayTeam:awayName,conventionalShortlist:input.conventionalShortlist?input.conventionalShortlist.map(normalizeScore).map(s=>({...s,score:scoreKey(s),classification:'CONVENTIONAL_REFERENCE',probabilityStatus:'NOT_RECOMPUTED'})):[],unconventionalShortlist:unconventional,scenarioPaths:scenarios.map(s=>({id:s.id,label:s.label,description:s.description})),method:'Odds-independent score-space exploration with seeded scenario perturbations and diversity ranking.',limitations:['Exploration score is not a probability or evidence of correctness.','Randomness creates coverage, not predictive skill; validate on untouched fixtures before claiming improvement.','Red cards and early goals are hypothetical paths, not assertions about what will happen.']};
  }
  const api={generateUnconventionalOutcomes};
  if(typeof module!=='undefined'&&module.exports) module.exports=api;
  root.CraineeUnconventionalOutcomeEngine=api;
})(typeof globalThis!=='undefined'?globalThis:this);

/* UFI Exact-Score Intelligence — additive research module.
   Transparent Poisson scenario grid, forecast snapshots and evidence-first post-mortems.
   This is not a calibrated live model or betting recommendation. */
(() => {
'use strict';
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const KEY = 'ufi_exact_score_intelligence_v1';
const clamp = (x,a,b) => Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
const num = id => Number($(id)?.value);
const esc = s => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const pct = x => (100*x).toFixed(2)+'%';
const stamp = () => new Date().toISOString();
let ledger = [];
try { const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY)||'[]'); if(Array.isArray(saved)) ledger=saved; } catch (_) {}
function save(){try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(ledger));}catch(e){status('Storage unavailable. Export your records before leaving this page.',true)}}
function status(msg,bad=false){const el=$('esiStatus');if(el){el.textContent=msg;el.className='notice '+(bad?'warning':'good')}}
function factorial(n){let v=1;for(let i=2;i<=n;i++)v*=i;return v}
function pois(k,lambda){return Math.exp(-lambda)*Math.pow(lambda,k)/factorial(k)}
function grid(h,a,cap=7){
 h=clamp(h,0.05,6);a=clamp(a,0.05,6);
 const rows=[];let mass=0;
 for(let i=0;i<=cap;i++)for(let j=0;j<=cap;j++){const p=pois(i,h)*pois(j,a);rows.push({home:i,away:j,p});mass+=p}
 rows.sort((x,y)=>y.p-x.p);
 return {rows,mass,tail:Math.max(0,1-mass),h,a};
}
function renderGrid(){
 const home=($('esiHome').value||'Home').trim(), away=($('esiAway').value||'Away').trim();
 const h=num('esiLambdaHome'), a=num('esiLambdaAway'), odds=num('esiOdds'), k=clamp(Math.floor(num('esiTopK')||5),1,12);
 if(!Number.isFinite(h)||!Number.isFinite(a)||h<=0||a<=0)return status('Enter positive expected-goal rates for both teams.',true);
 const g=grid(h,a), top=g.rows.slice(0,k), shortlist=top.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.p,0);
 const H=g.rows.filter(x=>x.home>x.away).reduce((s,x)=>s+x.p,0),D=g.rows.filter(x=>x.home===x.away).reduce((s,x)=>s+x.p,0),A=g.rows.filter(x=>x.home<x.away).reduce((s,x)=>s+x.p,0);
 const exact=top[0];
 $('esiSummary').innerHTML='<div class="outcome-grid"><div><span>Home win</span><b>'+pct(H)+'</b></div><div><span>Draw</span><b>'+pct(D)+'</b></div><div><span>Away win</span><b>'+pct(A)+'</b></div><div><span>Top '+k+' scores combined</span><b>'+pct(shortlist)+'</b></div></div><p><b>Highest individual scoreline in this baseline:</b> '+exact.home+'–'+exact.away+' at '+pct(exact.p)+'. This is not a strong prediction by itself.</p><p class="muted">Score-grid mass shown: '+pct(g.mass)+' · Tail beyond 0–'+7+' goals: '+pct(g.tail)+'. Independent Poisson baseline; no lineup, live-event, tactical or market calibration is implied.</p>';
 $('esiGrid').innerHTML='<table><thead><tr><th>Rank</th><th>Scoreline</th><th>Probability</th><th>Fair odds (1/p)</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+top.map((r,i)=>'<tr><td>'+(i+1)+'</td><td><b>'+r.home+'–'+r.away+'</b></td><td>'+pct(r.p)+'</td><td>'+(r.p>0?(1/r.p).toFixed(2):'—')+'</td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table>';
 if(Number.isFinite(odds)&&odds>1){const raw=1/odds;$('esiOddsCheck').innerHTML='<p>Odds '+odds.toFixed(2)+' imply a raw break-even probability of <b>'+pct(raw)+'</b> before bookmaker margin. Compare this only with a validated probability for the exact same score and market; this simple tool cannot establish value.</p>'}else $('esiOddsCheck').textContent='Enter decimal odds above 1 to see the raw break-even probability.';
 status('Scenario recalculated. Baseline only; do not interpret as a validated edge.');
}
function recordForecast(){
 const h=num('esiLambdaHome'),a=num('esiLambdaAway');
 if(!(h>0&&a>0))return status('Calculate a scenario with positive goal rates first.',true);
 const g=grid(h,a), top=g.rows.slice(0,5);
 ledger.unshift({id:'ESI-'+Date.now(),type:'forecast',createdAt:stamp(),match:($('esiHome').value||'Home')+' vs '+($('esiAway').value||'Away'),kickoff: $('esiKickoff').value||null,homeLambda:h,awayLambda:a,top5:top.map(x=>({score:x.home+'-'+x.away,probability:Number(x.p.toFixed(8))})),odds:num('esiOdds')>1?num('esiOdds'):null,model:'independent Poisson baseline; cap 7; not calibrated',notes:$('esiForecastNotes').value||'',source:$('esiForecastSource').value||''});
 save();renderLedger();status('Timestamped forecast snapshot saved locally. It will not be overwritten by post-match results.');
}
function recordPostmortem(){
 const match=$('esiPostMatch').value.trim();
 if(!match)return status('Enter the fixture name for the post-match audit.',true);
 const item={id:'PM-'+Date.now(),type:'postmortem',createdAt:stamp(),match,finalScore:$('esiFinal').value.trim(),selectedScore:$('esiSelected').value.trim(),minuteScore:$('esiLiveState').value.trim(),timeline:$('esiTimeline').value.trim(),shotsXg:$('esiShotsXg').value.trim(),lineupsCards:$('esiLineupsCards').value.trim(),reason:$('esiReason').value,source:$('esiPostSource').value.trim(),lesson:$('esiLesson').value.trim()};
 ledger.unshift(item);save();renderLedger();status('Post-match audit saved. The cause remains a hypothesis unless the source evidence supports it.');
}
function renderLedger(){
 const host=$('esiLedger');if(!host)return;
 if(!ledger.length){host.innerHTML='<p class="muted">No forecast snapshots or post-match audits saved yet.</p>';return}
 host.innerHTML='<table><thead><tr><th>Type</th><th>Match</th><th>Saved</th><th>Record</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+ledger.slice(0,100).map((x,i)=>'<tr><td>'+esc(x.type)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.match)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.createdAt)+'</td><td>'+(x.type==='forecast'?esc((x.top5||[]).map(z=>z.score+' '+pct(z.probability)).join(' · ')):'Selected '+esc(x.selectedScore||'unknown')+'; final '+esc(x.finalScore||'unknown')+'; reason '+esc(x.reason||'unknown'))+'</td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table><p class="muted">Showing up to 100 most recent records. Saved only in this browser until exported.</p>';
}
function download(name,data,type){const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([data],{type}));const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download=name;a.click();URL.revokeObjectURL(url)}

$('esiSaveForecast')?.addEventListener('click',recordForecast);
$('esiSavePostmortem')?.addEventListener('click',recordPostmortem);

function feedStatus(msg,bad=false){const el=$('esiFeedStatus');if(el){el.textContent=msg;el.className='notice '+(bad?'warning':'good')}}
function safeBase(){
 const base=feedBase();
 try { const u=new URL(base); if(u.protocol!=='https:' && u.hostname!=='localhost' && u.hostname!=='127.0.0.1') throw new Error(); return u.origin=== 'null' ? '' : base; }
 catch(_){feedStatus('Enter a valid HTTPS backend URL (localhost is allowed for local testing).',true);return ''}
}
async function fetchFeed(path){
 const base=safeBase(); if(!base) throw new Error('Backend URL is missing or invalid.');
 const response=await fetch(base+path,{headers:{Accept:'application/json'},cache:'no-store'});
 if(!response.ok) throw new Error('Backend returned HTTP '+response.status);
 const body=await response.json(); if(body && body.ok===false) throw new Error(body.error||'Provider error');
 return body;
}

 finally{if(button){button.disabled=false;button.textContent='Generate historical score prediction';}}
}

function unwrapRows(payload){
 const root=payload?.data;
 if(Array.isArray(root)) return root;
 if(Array.isArray(root?.data)) return root.data;
 if(Array.isArray(root?.data?.data)) return root.data.data;
 return [];
}
function getTeamNames(f){
 if(f?.homeTeam||f?.awayTeam)return {home:f.homeTeam||'Home',away:f.awayTeam||'Away'};
 const participants=f?.participants||[];
 const homeParticipant=participants.find(p=>String(p?.meta?.location||p?.location||'').toLowerCase()==='home');
 const awayParticipant=participants.find(p=>String(p?.meta?.location||p?.location||'').toLowerCase()==='away');
 const nameOf=p=>p?.name||p?.participant?.name||'';
 const home=nameOf(homeParticipant)||f?.home_team||f?.homeTeam;
 const away=nameOf(awayParticipant)||f?.away_team||f?.awayTeam;
 if(home||away)return {home:home||'Home',away:away||'Away'};
 const names=participants.map(nameOf).filter(Boolean);
 return {home:names[0]||f?.name||'Home',away:names[1]||'Away'};
}
function renderFeed(payload,label){
 const rows=unwrapRows(payload);
 const host=$('esiFeedOutput'); if(!host)return;
 if(!rows.length){host.innerHTML='<p class="muted">No fixture rows returned. Check provider coverage, date and subscription. Raw response metadata: '+esc(JSON.stringify({source:payload?.source,fetchedAt:payload?.fetchedAt,cacheHit:payload?.cacheHit}).slice(0,500))+'</p>'+(payload?.fallbackUsed?'<p class="notice warning">Fallback checked: '+esc(payload?.fallbackReason||'primary source returned no records')+'. '+esc(payload?.note||'No rows are available from the fallback source right now.')+'</p>':'');return}
 host.innerHTML='<p class="muted">Source: '+esc(payload?.source||'provider')+' · fetched '+esc(payload?.fetchedAt?new Date(payload.fetchedAt*1000).toISOString():'time unavailable')+' · '+esc(label)+' · '+rows.length+' row(s). Click a row to copy its team names into the scenario.</p>'+(payload?.fallbackUsed?'<p class="notice warning">Fallback feed: '+esc(payload?.fallbackReason||'primary source returned no records')+'. Missing scores, events, lineups and statistics remain unknown.</p>':'')+'<table><thead><tr><th>Fixture</th><th>Status</th><th>Score</th><th>Fixture ID</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+rows.slice(0,100).map((f,rowIndex)=>{const n=getTeamNames(f);const id=f?.id??f?.fixture_id??f?.fixtureId??'';const st=f?.status;const status=f?.state?.name||f?.state?.short_name||(st&&typeof st==='object'?(st.ended?'Finished':st.started?'Live':'Scheduled'):st)||'Unknown';let scores='? - ?';if(Array.isArray(f?.scores)){const current=f.scores.filter(s=>/^(CURRENT|FT|FULL_TIME|FINAL)$/i.test(String(s?.description||s?.type?.name||'')));const use=current.length?current:f.scores;const sideGoal=(side)=>{const row=use.find(s=>String(s?.score?.participant||s?.participant||'').toLowerCase()===side);return row?.score?.goals??row?.goals};const h=sideGoal('home'),a=sideGoal('away');if(h!=null&&a!=null)scores=h+' - '+a;else{const vals=use.map(s=>s?.score?.goals??s?.score??'').filter(x=>x!=='');if(vals.length)scores=vals.join(' - ')}}else if(f?.score&&typeof f.score==='object'){const h=f.score.home??f.score.homeScore??f.score.home_score;const a=f.score.away??f.score.awayScore??f.score.away_score;if(h!=null&&a!=null)scores=h+' - '+a}else if(f?.score!=null)scores=String(f.score);else if(f?.home_score!=null||f?.away_score!=null)scores=(f.home_score??'?')+' - '+(f.away_score??'?');return '<tr><td><button class="button small esiFixturePick" data-home="'+esc(n.home)+'" data-away="'+esc(n.away)+'" data-id="'+esc(id)+'" data-row="'+rowIndex+'">'+esc(n.home+' vs '+n.away)+'</button></td><td>'+esc(status)+'</td><td>'+esc(scores)+'</td><td>'+esc(id)+'</td></tr>'}).join('')+'</tbody></table><details><summary>Inspect source response</summary><pre>'+esc(JSON.stringify(payload,null,2).slice(0,20000))+'</pre></details>';
 host.querySelectorAll('.esiFixturePick').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{const fixture=rows[Number(b.dataset.row)];$('esiHome').value=b.dataset.home;$('esiAway').value=b.dataset.away;if($('esiProviderFixtureId'))$('esiProviderFixtureId').value=b.dataset.id||'';if(typeof window!=='undefined'&&typeof CustomEvent!=='undefined')window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('ufi:fixture-selected',{detail:{fixture,source:payload?.source||'provider',fallbackUsed:!!payload?.fallbackUsed}}));feedStatus('Fixture selected. Team names and sourced score/state fields are shared with connected engines when available; xG, player profiles and tactical inputs remain unfilled unless the source provides them.');}));
}
async function loadLive(){
 try{const data=await fetchFeed('/api/live');renderFeed(data,'live matches');if(typeof window!=='undefined'&&typeof CustomEvent!=='undefined')window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('ufi:live-feed',{detail:data}));feedStatus('Live feed refreshed. Scores/events are sourced data; exact-score live probabilities remain unavailable until the conditional model is validated.');}
 catch(e){feedStatus('Live feed unavailable: '+e.message,true)}
}
async function loadDateFixtures(){
 try{const d=$('esiFeedDate')?.value||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);const data=await fetchFeed('/api/fixtures?date='+encodeURIComponent(d));renderFeed(data,'fixtures on '+d);feedStatus('Fixture list loaded. Missing provider fields remain unknown.');}
 catch(e){feedStatus('Fixture list unavailable: '+e.message,true)}
}
async function loadFixtureDetails(){
 const id=String($('esiProviderFixtureId')?.value||'').trim();if(!/^\d+$/.test(id))return feedStatus('This selected fixture ID is not a numeric Sportmonks ID. The normalized fallback source does not expose Sportmonks fixture details; choose a numeric Sportmonks fixture ID to use this detail lookup.',true);
 try{const data=await fetchFeed('/api/fixture?id='+encodeURIComponent(id));const host=$('esiFeedOutput');if(host)host.innerHTML='<p class="muted">Detailed provider response · '+esc(data?.source||'provider')+' · '+esc(data?.fetchedAt?new Date(data.fetchedAt*1000).toISOString():'timestamp unavailable')+'</p><pre>'+esc(JSON.stringify(data,null,2).slice(0,30000))+'</pre>';feedStatus('Fixture detail loaded. Check source timestamps and missing fields; no model probability is claimed from raw events alone.');}
 catch(e){feedStatus('Fixture details unavailable: '+e.message,true)}
}
$('esiConnectFeed')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{const base=safeBase();if(!base)return;try{localStorage.setItem(API_KEY,base)}catch(_){}if(feedTimer)clearInterval(feedTimer);loadLive();feedTimer=setInterval(loadLive,10000);});
$('esiLoadFixtures')?.addEventListener('click',loadDateFixtures);
$('esiLoadFixtureDetails')?.addEventListener('click',loadFixtureDetails);
$('esiStopFeed')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(feedTimer)clearInterval(feedTimer);feedTimer=null;feedStatus('Automatic refresh stopped.')});
try{const savedBase=localStorage.getItem(API_KEY);const candidate=String(savedBase||'').trim();const isPlaceholder=!candidate||/your-ufi-backend\.example/i.test(candidate);const isHttps=/^https:\/\//i.test(candidate);const resolvedBase=isPlaceholder||!isHttps?DEFAULT_API_BASE:candidate.replace(/\/+$/,'');if($('esiApiBase'))$('esiApiBase').value=resolvedBase;if(resolvedBase!==candidate)localStorage.setItem(API_KEY,resolvedBase);}catch(_){if($('esiApiBase'))$('esiApiBase').value=DEFAULT_API_BASE;}
if($('esiFeedDate'))$('esiFeedDate').value=new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);

// Verify the configured HTTPS backend on page load without consuming odds-provider quota.
if($('esiApiBase')){
 fetchFeed('/api/providers/status').then(data=>{
  const providers=data?.providers||{};
  const configured=Object.entries(providers).filter(([name,p])=>name!=='openMeteo'&&p?.configured).map(([name])=>name);
  const missing=Object.entries(providers).filter(([name,p])=>name!=='openMeteo'&&!p?.configured).map(([name])=>name);
  feedStatus('UFI HTTPS backend connected. '+(configured.length?'Configured providers: '+configured.join(', ')+'. ':'')+(missing.length?'Provider keys not configured: '+missing.join(', ')+'. ':'')+'Open-Meteo is available without a key. Health check does not verify provider connectivity or model accuracy.');
 }).catch(e=>feedStatus('UFI backend connection check failed: '+e.message,true));
}

})();

<section class="view" id="view-scenarios">

It can challenge a prediction; it cannot manufacture data or override the deterministic risk gate.</p><div class="button-row"><button id="runAiCouncilScenario" class="button primary">Run AI council on current prediction</button></div><div id="aiCouncilScenarioOutput" class="notice ai-council">Configure provider keys in Slip & odds lab, then run the council here to challenge the current scenario.</div></div><div class="panel"><h2>Scenario assumptions checklist</h2><div class="checklist"><label><input type="checkbox" id="checkLineup"> Starting lineup confirmed</label><label><input type="checkbox" id="checkVenue"> Home/away context checked</label><label><input type="checkbox" id="checkRecent"> Recent match sample checked</label><label><input type="checkbox" id="checkOpponent"> Opponent strength considered</label><label><input type="checkbox" id="checkMarket"> Market price compared independently</label><label><input type="checkbox" id="checkUncertainty"> Uncertainty and missing data noted</label></div><label>Scenario caveats<textarea id="scenarioCaveats" placeholder="What could make these assumptions wrong?"></textarea></label></div>
</section>
<section class="view" id="view-bookieedge">
<div class="page-header"><div><div class="eyebrow">MARKET MICROSTRUCTURE · 90-MINUTE SURVIVAL GATE</div><h1>5400 Bookie-Edge Gate</h1><p class="muted">Reject weak, stale, overpriced or poorly supported selections. This is a conservative screening gate, not proof a bookmaker is trapping you or that a surviving selection will win.</p></div></div>
<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><div class="eyebrow">CORE RULE</div><h2>5400 checkpoints ≠ 5400 independent confirmations</h2></div><span class="tag">EXPERIMENTAL FILTER</span></div>
<p>5400 seconds represents 90 minutes. The gate uses match time to set the expected number of elapsed checkpoints (60 per minute) and continuously reevaluates fresh inputs when the app receives them. Repeating the same unchanged odds 5400 times does not create evidence. A candidate must pass every hard gate below; otherwise it is discarded or marked insufficient data.</p>
<div class="form-grid four">
<label>Match minute<input id="beMinute" type="number" min="0" max="130" value="0"></label>
<label>Maximum odds age (seconds)<input id="beMaxAge" type="number" min="1" value="15"></label>
<label>Minimum estimated net EV (%)<input id="beMinEV" type="number" value="3" step="0.5"></label>
<label>Maximum probability uncertainty (percentage points)<input id="beMaxUncertainty" type="number" min="0" max="100" value="8"></label>
<label>Minimum independent validation matches<input id="beMinSamples" type="number" min="0" value="200"></label>
<label>Required market-data snapshots<input id="beMinSnapshots" type="number" min="1" value="3"></label>
<label>Minimum model probability (%)<input id="beMinProbability" type="number" min="0" max="100" value="5"></label>
<label>Minimum conservative edge vs market (percentage points)<input id="beMinEdge" type="number" value="1" step="0.5"></label>
</div></div>
<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><div class="eyebrow">CANDIDATES</div><h2>Candidate evidence JSON</h2></div><button class="button" id="beExample">Load example</button></div>
<p class="muted">Use decimal odds and probabilities from 0 to 1. Include a real ISO timestamp, independent out-of-sample sample count, snapshot count and uncertainty estimate. A source timestamp is required; browser capture time is not a substitute for source freshness.</p>
<label>Candidate array<textarea id="beCandidates" rows="12" spellcheck="false" placeholder='[{"selection":"Home win","odds":2.4,"modelProbability":0.46,"marketProbability":0.41,"uncertainty":0.04,"sourceUpdatedAt":"2026-10-25T19:00:00Z","validationMatches":500,"snapshots":5}]'></textarea></label>
<div class="button-row"><button class="button primary" id="beRun">Evaluate / discard candidates</button><button class="button" id="beClear">Clear</button></div><div id="beOutput" class="notice">Waiting for candidate data. No selection is approved by default.</div></div>
<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><div class="eyebrow">SQUAD · REFEREE · MATCH PHASE</div><h2>Crainee Advanced Match Risk & Chaos Gate</h2></div><span class="tag">EXPERIMENTAL HEURISTIC</span></div>
<p class="muted">Scores squad market-value/age/caps inputs, average referee whistle gaps and elapsed match time. This is a contextual risk heuristic, not a calibrated match-win probability. Missing player values use the stated defaults and are reported as assumptions.</p>
<div class="form-grid two"><label>Current elapsed seconds<input id="caCurrentSecond" type="number" min="0" max="6000" value="0"></label><label>Available bankroll (₦)<input id="caBankroll" type="number" min="0" step="100" value="0"></label></div>
<div class="form-grid two"><label>Home squad players JSON<textarea id="caHomeSquad" rows="8" spellcheck="false" placeholder='[{"name":"Player A","marketValue":25000000,"age":27,"caps":40}]'></textarea></label><label>Away squad players JSON<textarea id="caAwaySquad" rows="8" spellcheck="false" placeholder='[{"name":"Player B","marketValue":18000000,"age":25,"caps":25}]'></textarea></label></div>
<div class="form-grid two"><label>Referee whistle-gap JSON<textarea id="caReferee" rows="3" spellcheck="false" placeholder='{"avgWhistleGap1to45":120,"avgWhistleGap45to90":85}'></textarea></label><label>Gate settings<p class="muted">Fixed experimental survival threshold: 0.25. Absolute stake cap: 5% of entered bankroll.</p><button class="button" id="caExample">Load hypothetical example</button></label></div>
<div class="button-row"><button class="button primary" id="caRun">Evaluate match risk</button><button class="button" id="caClear">Clear inputs</button></div><div id="caOutput" class="notice">Waiting for squad and referee data. No match is cleared by default.</div>
<p class="muted">The stake output is only a mathematical cap derived from this heuristic, not a recommendation to bet. Do not enter borrowed money or treat this score as proof of an edge.</p></div>
<div class="panel"><div class="eyebrow">HOW TO READ THE GATE</div><ul><li><b>Net EV:</b> estimated model probability × decimal odds − 1, reduced by an uncertainty haircut.</li><li><b>Conservative edge:</b> lower-confidence model probability minus de-vigged market probability.</li><li><b>5400 survival:</b> checkpoints track elapsed match time; a candidate survives only if every hard data/edge rule passes on each fresh evaluation. Past checks cannot guarantee future stability.</li><li><b>Discard:</b> stale source timestamp, missing evidence, too few independent validation matches, excessive uncertainty, inadequate conservative edge or negative/insufficient net EV.</li></ul><p class="muted">Never interpret unavailable markets or sudden odds movement as proof of manipulation. The filter does not place bets and does not promise profit.</p></div>
</section>

/* Navigation safety net: keep page switching available even if another app module fails. */
document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(function(button){
  button.addEventListener('click',function(){
    var target='view-'+button.dataset.view;
    document.querySelectorAll('.view').forEach(function(view){view.classList.toggle('active',view.id===target);});
    document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(function(item){item.classList.toggle('active',item===button);});
    window.scrollTo(0,0);
  });
});

/* UFI Live Correct-Score + Player Chaos Engine
 * Experimental research module. No live provider is implied by manual inputs.
 * Probabilities are model outputs, not guarantees; calibrate with chronological holdouts.
 */
(() => {
'use strict';
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const clamp=(x,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
const n=id=>Number($(id).value);
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const pct=x=>(100*x).toFixed(2)+'%';
function factorial(k){let f=1;for(let i=2;i<=k;i++)f*=i;return f}
function pois(k,l){if(l===0)return k===0?1:0;return Math.exp(-l+k*Math.log(l)-Math.log(factorial(k)))}
function tau(h,a,lambda,mu,rho){
 if(h===0&&a===0)return 1-lambda*mu*rho;
 if(h===0&&a===1)return 1+lambda*rho;
 if(h===1&&a===0)return 1+mu*rho;
 if(h===1&&a===1)return 1-rho;
 return 1;
}
function parsePlayers(id){
 try { const x=JSON.parse($(id).value||'[]'); if(!Array.isArray(x))throw Error('must be a JSON array'); return x; }
 catch(e){throw Error(id+' must contain a valid JSON array ('+e.message+').')}
}
function profileChaos(players, pressing){
 return players.map(p=>{
  const foot=String(p.dominantFoot||'unknown').toLowerCase();
  const side=String(p.positionSide||'').toLowerCase();
  const forced=pressing && ((foot==='right'&&side==='left')||(foot==='left'&&side==='right'));
  const footPenalty=forced?0.18:0;
  const observed=clamp(Number(p.rIndex)||0,0,1);
  const r=clamp(observed+footPenalty,0,1);
  return {...p,rIndex:r,footPenalty,virtualRed:r>=0.75};
 });
}
function calc(){
 const home=($('lcsHome').value||'Home').trim(), away=($('lcsAway').value||'Away').trim();
 const live=$('lcsMode').value==='live', minute=clamp(n('lcsMinute')||0,0,120);
 const sh=Math.max(0,Math.floor(n('lcsCurrentHome')||0)), sa=Math.max(0,Math.floor(n('lcsCurrentAway')||0));
 let lh=clamp(n('lcsXgHome')||0,0,6), la=clamp(n('lcsXgAway')||0,0,6);
 const rho=clamp(n('lcsRho')||0,-0.15,0.15), pressing=$('lcsPressing').checked;
 const odds=Number($('lcsOver15Odds').value), combinedBaseline=(Number($('lcsXgHome').value)||0)+(Number($('lcsXgAway').value)||0);
 const filterPassed=Number.isFinite(odds)&&odds>=1.08&&odds<=1.35&&combinedBaseline>=2.60;
 const leagueProfile=$('lcsLeague').value;
 const ph=profileChaos(parsePlayers('lcsPlayersHome'),pressing), pa=profileChaos(parsePlayers('lcsPlayersAway'),pressing);
 const redH=ph.filter(p=>p.virtualRed).length, redA=pa.filter(p=>p.virtualRed).length;
 const tcsH=ph.reduce((s,p)=>s+p.rIndex,0)+(Number($('lcsVolHome').value)||0);
 const tcsA=pa.reduce((s,p)=>s+p.rIndex,0)+(Number($('lcsVolAway').value)||0);
 // Player anomaly adjustments are explicitly heuristic and bounded.
 lh=clamp(lh-0.35*redH,0,6); la=clamp(la-0.35*redA,0,6);
 // Defensive concession adjustment transfers a bounded fraction of expected goals to opponent.
 lh=clamp(lh+0.25*redA,0,6); la=clamp(la+0.25*redH,0,6);
 let remH=lh, remA=la;
 if(live){
  const remain=clamp((1-minute/90),0,1);
  const decay=Math.pow(remain,1.15);
  remH=lh*decay; remA=la*decay;
 }
 const discarded=redH>=2||redA>=2||tcsH>=Number($('lcsTcsLimit').value)||tcsA>=Number($('lcsTcsLimit').value);
 const cardH=Number($('lcsRedHome').value)||0, cardA=Number($('lcsRedAway').value)||0;
 // Explicit real red cards: penalize carded team's attack, increase opponent's attack; bounded.
 remH=clamp(remH-0.40*cardH+0.65*cardA,0,6);
 remA=clamp(remA-0.40*cardA+0.65*cardH,0,6);
 const volatility=minute<20 && live && (sh+sa)>0 ? 1.25:1;
 remH=clamp(remH*volatility,0,6); remA=clamp(remA*volatility,0,6);
 const rows=[]; let mass=0;
 for(let i=0;i<=sh+10;i++)for(let j=0;j<=sa+10;j++){
  const gh=i-sh, ga=j-sa; if(gh<0||ga<0)continue;
  let p=pois(gh,remH)*pois(ga,remA);
  if(!live) p*=Math.max(0,tau(i,j,lh,la,rho));
  rows.push({h:i,a:j,p}); mass+=p;
 }
 const adjustedMass=rows.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.p,0);
 if(adjustedMass>0) rows.forEach(r=>r.p/=adjustedMass);
 rows.sort((x,y)=>y.p-x.p);
 const top=rows.slice(0,4);
 const profilesComplete=ph.length===11&&pa.length===11;
 const green=!discarded&&filterPassed&&profilesComplete&&tcsH<Number($('lcsTcsLimit').value)*0.65&&tcsA<Number($('lcsTcsLimit').value)*0.65&&redH===0&&redA===0;
 const status=discarded?'MATCH DISCARDED: High Randomness Anomaly Detected (TCS Exceeded Limits).':green?'GREEN LIGHT: Low Randomness / Model Filters Passed (experimental, not validated confidence).':'CAUTION: Elevated randomness or model risk. Do not apply maximum model weight.';
 $('lcsOutput').innerHTML='<div class="notice '+(discarded?'warning':green?'good':'warning')+'"><b>'+esc(status)+'</b></div>'+
 '<div class="notice '+(filterPassed?'good':'warning')+'"><b>Candidate filter: '+(filterPassed?'YES':'NO / INCOMPLETE')+'</b> — Over 1.5 odds '+(Number.isFinite(odds)?odds.toFixed(2):'not supplied')+'; combined baseline xG '+combinedBaseline.toFixed(2)+' (target ≥ 2.60). Target lower-tier league selected: '+(leagueProfile==='target'?'yes':leagueProfile==='major'?'no':'unknown')+'. Odds and league classification are user-entered and not verified by a feed.</div>'+
 '<div class="outcome-grid"><div><span>Home remaining xG*</span><b>'+remH.toFixed(2)+'</b></div><div><span>Away remaining xG*</span><b>'+remA.toFixed(2)+'</b></div><div><span>Home TCS</span><b>'+tcsH.toFixed(2)+'</b></div><div><span>Away TCS</span><b>'+tcsA.toFixed(2)+'</b></div></div>'+
 (discarded?'<p class="muted">Prediction suppressed by circuit breaker. The scorelines below are intentionally withheld.</p>':
 '<h3>Top 4 final-score scenarios</h3><div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Rank</th><th>Final score</th><th>Model probability</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+top.map((r,i)=>'<tr><td>'+(i+1)+'</td><td><b>'+r.h+'–'+r.a+'</b></td><td>'+pct(r.p)+'</td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table></div>'+
 '<p class="muted">*Remaining xG is a heuristic derived from entered baseline rates and time decay, not provider-verified live xG. Top four probabilities do not sum to 100%. Displayed score-grid mass: '+pct(mass)+'.</p>')+
 '<h3>Player anomaly audit</h3><div class="form-grid two"><div><b>'+esc(home)+'</b><p>Virtual red flags: '+redH+' · TCS: '+tcsH.toFixed(2)+'</p>'+playerTable(ph)+'</div><div><b>'+esc(away)+'</b><p>Virtual red flags: '+redA+' · TCS: '+tcsA.toFixed(2)+'</p>'+playerTable(pa)+'</div></div>'+
 '<p class="muted">'+(!profilesComplete?'Green-light approval withheld: provide all 11 starting-player profiles for both teams; missing profiles are unknown. ':'')+'Inverse-foot rule adds 0.18 to R-Index for right-footed players in a left-side role or left-footed players in a right-side role when high pressing is toggled. This is a hypothesis, not an empirically established universal effect. “Virtual red” is a simulation penalty only; it is not a real sending-off.</p>';
}
function playerTable(ps){if(!ps.length)return '<p class="muted">No player profiles entered; player-level risk is unavailable, not zero.</p>';return '<div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Player</th><th>R-Index</th><th>Foot penalty</th><th>Flag</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+ps.map(p=>'<tr><td>'+esc(p.name||'Unnamed')+'</td><td>'+p.rIndex.toFixed(2)+'</td><td>'+(p.footPenalty*100).toFixed(0)+'%</td><td>'+(p.virtualRed?'VIRTUAL RED':'—')+'</td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table></div>'}
$('lcsRun').addEventListener('click',()=>{try{calc()}catch(e){$('lcsOutput').innerHTML='<div class="notice warning">'+esc(e.message)+'</div>'}});
function scoreFromFixture(f){
 const s=f?.score;
 if(s&&typeof s==='object'){
  const h=s.home??s.homeScore??s.home_score,a=s.away??s.awayScore??s.away_score;
  if(Number.isInteger(Number(h))&&Number.isInteger(Number(a))&&h!==null&&a!==null)return {h:Number(h),a:Number(a)};
 }
 if(f?.home_score!=null&&f?.away_score!=null&&Number.isFinite(Number(f.home_score))&&Number.isFinite(Number(f.away_score)))return {h:Number(f.home_score),a:Number(f.away_score)};
 if(Array.isArray(f?.scores)){
  const current=f.scores.filter(x=>/^(CURRENT|FT|FULL_TIME|FINAL)$/i.test(String(x?.description||x?.type?.name||'')));
  const rows=current.length?current:f.scores;
  const home=rows.find(x=>String(x?.score?.participant??x?.participant??'').toLowerCase()==='home');
  const away=rows.find(x=>String(x?.score?.participant??x?.participant??'').toLowerCase()==='away');
  const h=home?.score?.goals??home?.goals,a=away?.score?.goals??away?.goals;
  if(h!=null&&a!=null&&Number.isFinite(Number(h))&&Number.isFinite(Number(a)))return {h:Number(h),a:Number(a)};
 }
 return null;
}
window.addEventListener('ufi:fixture-selected',event=>{
 const detail=event.detail||{},f=detail.fixture||{},status=f.status&&typeof f.status==='object'?f.status:{};

 const live=status.started===true||/live|in.?play/.test(stateText);
 if(hasLiveState)$('lcsMode').value=live?'live':'prematch';

 const note=$('lcsFeedStatus');if(note)note.textContent='Selected fixture from '+(detail.source||'provider')+(detail.fallbackUsed?' (fallback source)':'')+'. Team names'+(score?' and sourced score':'')+' updated where available. Expected-goal rates, player profiles and tactical fields remain manual unless verified source data is connected.';
});

});
})();

/* Live market-shock monitor + chronological exact-score backtest.
 * Uses explicit source timestamps; never treats odds movement as proof of a goal.
 * Browser module is additive and does not replace the existing engines.
 */
(() => {
'use strict';
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const clamp=(x,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
const finite=(x)=>Number.isFinite(Number(x));
function poisson(k,lambda){if(k<0)return 0;let p=Math.exp(-lambda);for(let i=1;i<=k;i++)p*=lambda/i;return p}
function implied(row){
 const keys=['home','draw','away'];const raw=keys.map(k=>1/Number(row[k]));
 if(raw.some((v,i)=>!Number.isFinite(v)||Number(row[keys[i]])<=1))throw Error('Every 1X2 decimal price must be > 1.00.');
 const sum=raw.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);return raw.map(v=>v/sum);
}
function parseSourceTime(value){
 if(!value)return NaN;
 let s=String(value).trim().replace(' ','T');
 if(!(s.endsWith('Z')||s.endsWith('z')||/[+-][0-9]{2}:?[0-9]{2}$/.test(s)))s+='Z';
 return Date.parse(s);
}
function parseOdds(){
 const rows=JSON.parse($('lmsOddsSnapshots').value||'[]');
 if(!Array.isArray(rows)||rows.length<2)throw Error('Enter at least two timestamped odds snapshots as a JSON array.');
 const valid=rows.map(r=>{
  if(!r.timestamp)throw Error('Every odds snapshot needs an ISO timestamp.');
  const ts=Date.parse(r.timestamp);if(!Number.isFinite(ts))throw Error('Invalid odds timestamp: '+r.timestamp);
  const sourceTs=r.sourceUpdatedAt?parseSourceTime(r.sourceUpdatedAt):ts;
  if(!Number.isFinite(sourceTs))throw Error('Invalid provider source-update timestamp: '+r.sourceUpdatedAt);
  return {...r,ts,sourceTs,p:implied(r)}
 }).sort((a,b)=>a.ts-b.ts);
 if(valid[0].ts===valid[1].ts)throw Error('The two latest snapshots need distinct capture timestamps.');
 return valid;
}
function runMarket(){
 const out=$('lmsOutput');
 try{
  const snaps=parseOdds(),a=snaps[snaps.length-2],b=snaps[snaps.length-1],now=Date.now();
  const rawAge=(now-b.sourceTs)/1000,age=Math.max(0,rawAge),gap=(b.ts-a.ts)/1000;
  const minute=clamp(Number($('lmsMinute').value)||0,0,120),h=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number($('lmsScoreH').value)||0)),away=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number($('lmsScoreA').value)||0));
  const lh=clamp(Number($('lmsRemH').value)||0,0,4),la=clamp(Number($('lmsRemA').value)||0,0,4);
  const changes=b.p.map((p,i)=>p-a.p[i]),maxAbs=Math.max(...changes.map(Math.abs));
  const maxRel=Math.max(...['home','draw','away'].map(k=>Math.abs(Number(a[k])-Number(b[k]))/Number(a[k])));
  const shockLimit=(Number($('lmsShockLimit').value)||10)/100;
  const stale=rawAge < -5 || age>120 || gap>900 || gap<1;
  const shock=maxAbs>=shockLimit||maxRel>=0.25;
  const reasons=[];
  if(rawAge < -5)reasons.push('FUTURE-DATED snapshot — source clock or timestamp is invalid.'); else if(stale)reasons.push('STALE / badly spaced odds snapshots — do not use them to update a live forecast.');
  if(shock)reasons.push('MARKET SHOCK — material price movement; suspend scoreline approval and re-check events, VAR, cards, injuries and feed timestamps.');
  if(!stale&&!shock)reasons.push('No configured shock threshold crossed. This is not proof that the market is correct.');
  // Small bounded adjustment only for modest, fresh moves. A large move causes a HOLD, not chasing.
  let adjH=0,adjA=0,decision='MONITOR';
  if(stale){decision='DISCARD / HOLD';}
  else if(shock){decision='PAUSE / DISCARD CURRENT FORECAST';}
  else {
   adjH=clamp((changes[0]-changes[2])*0.35,-0.20,0.20);
   adjA=clamp((changes[2]-changes[0])*0.35,-0.20,0.20);
  }
  const finalH=[],finalA=[];
  for(let i=0;i<=5;i++)for(let j=0;j<=5;j++)finalH.push({h:h+i,a:away+j,p:poisson(i,lh*(1+adjH))*poisson(j,la*(1+adjA))});
  const sum=finalH.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.p,0)||1;finalH.forEach(x=>x.p/=sum);finalH.sort((x,y)=>y.p-x.p);
  const details=['home','draw','away'].map((k,i)=>({k,from:a[k],to:b[k],probFrom:a.p[i],probTo:b.p[i],delta:changes[i]}));
  out.innerHTML='<div class="notice '+(stale||shock?'warning':'')+'"><b>'+esc(decision)+'</b><br>Snapshot interval: '+gap.toFixed(1)+' s · Latest source age: '+age.toFixed(1)+' s · Current state: '+h+'–'+away+' at '+minute+'′.<br>'+reasons.map(esc).join('<br>')+'</div>'+
  '<div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Market</th><th>Odds before</th><th>Odds now</th><th>De-vig probability shift</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+
  details.map(d=>'<tr><td>'+d.k.toUpperCase()+'</td><td>'+Number(d.from).toFixed(2)+'</td><td>'+Number(d.to).toFixed(2)+'</td><td>'+((d.delta*100)>=0?'+':'')+(d.delta*100).toFixed(2)+' pp</td></tr>').join('')+
  '</tbody></table></div>'+
  '<p><b>Conditional score branches (remaining-goal xG supplied by user):</b> '+finalH.slice(0,5).map(x=>x.h+'–'+x.a+' ('+(x.p*100).toFixed(1)+'%)').join(' · ')+'</p>'+
  '<p class="muted">Adjustment applied: home '+(adjH*100).toFixed(1)+'%, away '+(adjA*100).toFixed(1)+'%. Large or stale moves are never force-fit into a prediction. Market prices are not verified match events; refresh the event feed and confirm score/minute first.</p>';
 }catch(e){out.innerHTML='<div class="notice warning">'+esc(e.message)+'</div>'}
}
function csvParse(text){
 const lines=text.replace(String.fromCharCode(65279),'').split(String.fromCharCode(10)).map(x=>x.replaceAll(String.fromCharCode(13),'')).filter(x=>x.trim());
 if(lines.length<2)throw Error('CSV needs a header and at least two match rows.');
 const split=line=>{const out=[];let cur='',q=false;for(let i=0;i<line.length;i++){const c=line[i];if(c==='"'&&line[i+1]==='"'&&q){cur+='"';i++}else if(c==='"')q=!q;else if(c===','&&!q){out.push(cur);cur=''}else cur+=c}out.push(cur);return out};
 const headers=split(lines[0]).map(x=>x.trim().toLowerCase());const rows=lines.slice(1).map(line=>{const vals=split(line);const o={};headers.forEach((h,i)=>o[h]=(vals[i]||'').trim());return o});
 const required=['date','home_team','away_team','home_goals','away_goals'];for(const k of required)if(!headers.includes(k))throw Error('Missing CSV column: '+k);
 const dated=rows.map(r=>({...r,hg:Number(r.home_goals),ag:Number(r.away_goals)}));
 const validIsoDate=value=>/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)&&!Number.isNaN(Date.parse(value+'T00:00:00Z'))&&new Date(value+'T00:00:00Z').toISOString().slice(0,10)===value;
 const badDate=dated.find(r=>r.date&&!validIsoDate(r.date));
 if(badDate)throw Error('Invalid date "'+badDate.date+'". Use YYYY-MM-DD for every row so the chronological walk-forward test cannot leak future results into earlier forecasts.');
 return dated.filter(r=>r.date&&r.home_team&&r.away_team&&Number.isInteger(r.hg)&&Number.isInteger(r.ag)&&r.hg>=0&&r.ag>=0).sort((a,b)=>a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
}
function poissonDist(lam,cap=9){const p=[Math.exp(-lam)];for(let k=1;k<=cap;k++)p.push(p[k-1]*lam/k);return p}
function gridProb(hg,ag,lh,la){const hp=poissonDist(lh),ap=poissonDist(la);return Math.max(1e-12,(hp[hg]||0)*(ap[ag]||0))}
function runBacktest(){
 const out=$('lmsBacktestOutput'),file=$('lmsBacktestFile').files[0];
 if(!file){out.innerHTML='<div class="notice warning">Choose a historical CSV first.</div>';return}
 const reader=new FileReader();reader.onload=()=>{
 try{
  const rows=csvParse(String(reader.result||''));if(rows.length<30)throw Error('Only '+rows.length+' valid rows. For meaningful evaluation, load at least 30 chronologically ordered matches; 100+ is preferable.');
  const minHistory=Math.max(3,Number($('lmsMinHistory').value)||5), window=Math.max(5,Number($('lmsWindow').value)||10);
  const teamHist=new Map(),leagueHist=new Map(),results=[];let skipped=0;
  const mean=a=>a.length?a.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0)/a.length:1.25;
  const hist=(map,k)=>map.get(k)||[];
  // Date-batched walk-forward: all forecasts on a date are produced before that date's results are added.
  for(let start=0;start<rows.length;){
   let end=start+1;while(end<rows.length&&rows[end].date===rows[start].date)end++;
   const batch=rows.slice(start,end);
   for(const r of batch){
    const league=(r.league||r.league_code||'ALL').trim()||'ALL',home=r.home_team,away=r.away_team;
    const hh=hist(teamHist,league+'|'+home),ah=hist(teamHist,league+'|'+away),lhist=hist(leagueHist,league);
    if(hh.length<minHistory||ah.length<minHistory||lhist.length<minHistory){skipped++;continue}
    const last=(arr,key)=>arr.slice(-window).map(x=>x[key]);
    const leagueHome=mean(lhist.map(x=>x.hg)),leagueAway=mean(lhist.map(x=>x.ag));
    const attackH=mean(last(hh,'scored'))/Math.max(.2,leagueHome);
    const defenceA=mean(last(ah,'conceded'))/Math.max(.2,leagueHome);
    const attackA=mean(last(ah,'scored'))/Math.max(.2,leagueAway);
    const defenceH=mean(last(hh,'conceded'))/Math.max(.2,leagueAway);
    const modelH=clamp(leagueHome*attackH*defenceA,.08,4.5),modelA=clamp(leagueAway*attackA*defenceH,.08,4.5);
    const baseH=clamp(leagueHome,.08,4.5),baseA=clamp(leagueAway,.08,4.5);
    const modelP=gridProb(r.hg,r.ag,modelH,modelA),baseP=gridProb(r.hg,r.ag,baseH,baseA);
    const top=(h,a)=>{let best={h:0,a:0,p:0};for(let i=0;i<=7;i++)for(let j=0;j<=7;j++){const p=gridProb(i,j,h,a);if(p>best.p)best={h:i,a:j,p}}return best};
    const mp=top(modelH,modelA),bp=top(baseH,baseA);
    results.push({date:r.date,home,away,actual:r.hg+'-'+r.ag,model:mp.h+'-'+mp.a,modelProb:mp.p,base:bp.h+'-'+bp.a,baseProb:bp.p,modelNll:-Math.log(modelP),baseNll:-Math.log(baseP),modelHit:mp.h===r.hg&&mp.a===r.ag,baseHit:bp.h===r.hg&&bp.a===r.ag});
   }
   // Update only after every fixture on this date has been forecast.
   for(const r of batch){
    const league=(r.league||r.league_code||'ALL').trim()||'ALL';
    for(const [team,scored,conceded] of [[r.home_team,r.hg,r.ag],[r.away_team,r.ag,r.hg]]){
     const key=league+'|'+team,arr=hist(teamHist,key);arr.push({scored,conceded});if(arr.length>window*3)arr.shift();teamHist.set(key,arr);
    }
    const la=hist(leagueHist,league);la.push({hg:r.hg,ag:r.ag});if(la.length>500)la.shift();leagueHist.set(league,la);
   }
   start=end;
  }
  if(!results.length)throw Error('No out-of-sample rows. Reduce minimum history or supply more matches per league.');
  const n=results.length,modelNll=results.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.modelNll,0)/n,baseNll=results.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.baseNll,0)/n;
  const modelHit=results.filter(r=>r.modelHit).length/n,baseHit=results.filter(r=>r.baseHit).length/n;
  const win=modelNll<baseNll;
  out.innerHTML='<div class="metrics"><article class="metric"><span>Out-of-sample matches</span><strong>'+n+'</strong><small>'+skipped+' rows held back for history warm-up</small></article><article class="metric"><span>Model exact-score top-1</span><strong>'+(modelHit*100).toFixed(2)+'%</strong><small>Hit rate is noisy</small></article><article class="metric"><span>Model exact-score log loss</span><strong>'+modelNll.toFixed(3)+'</strong><small>Lower is better</small></article><article class="metric"><span>Baseline log loss</span><strong>'+baseNll.toFixed(3)+'</strong><small>League-average Poisson</small></article></div>'+
  '<div class="notice '+(win?'':'warning')+'"><b>'+(win?'Rolling team-rate model beat':'Rolling team-rate model did not beat')+' the league-average baseline on this sample.</b> This is a walk-forward diagnostic, not proof of a betting edge. Test-set dates were not randomly shuffled. Results may still be affected by source coverage and CSV quality.</div>'+
  '<div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>Match</th><th>Actual</th><th>Model top score</th><th>Baseline top score</th><th>Model NLL</th><th>Baseline NLL</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+
  results.slice(-100).map(r=>'<tr><td>'+esc(r.date)+'</td><td>'+esc(r.home)+' – '+esc(r.away)+'</td><td>'+r.actual+'</td><td>'+r.model+(r.modelHit?' ✓':'')+'</td><td>'+r.base+(r.baseHit?' ✓':'')+'</td><td>'+r.modelNll.toFixed(3)+'</td><td>'+r.baseNll.toFixed(3)+'</td></tr>').join('')+
  '</tbody></table></div><button id="lmsDownloadBacktest" class="button">Download backtest CSV</button>';
  $('lmsDownloadBacktest').addEventListener('click',()=>{const header='date,home,away,actual,model_top_score,baseline_top_score,model_exact_score_logloss,baseline_exact_score_logloss,model_hit,baseline_hit\n';const body=results.map(r=>[r.date,r.home,r.away,r.actual,r.model,r.base,r.modelNll.toFixed(6),r.baseNll.toFixed(6),r.modelHit,r.baseHit].map(v=>'"'+String(v).replace(/"/g,'""')+'"').join(',')).join('\n');const blob=new Blob([header+body],{type:'text/csv'}),url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='ufi-walkforward-exact-score-backtest.csv';a.click();URL.revokeObjectURL(url)});
 }catch(e){out.innerHTML='<div class="notice warning">'+esc(e.message)+'</div>'}
 };reader.readAsText(file);
}
async function runWeather(){
 const out=$('lmsWeatherOutput');
 try{
  const base=($('lmsWeatherBase').value||($('esiApiBase')&&$('esiApiBase').value)||'').trim().replace(/[/]+$/,'');
  if(!$('lmsWeatherLat').value.trim()||!$('lmsWeatherLon').value.trim())throw Error('Enter verified stadium coordinates; blank coordinates are not valid.');
  const lat=Number($('lmsWeatherLat').value),lon=Number($('lmsWeatherLon').value),day=$('lmsWeatherDate').value,hour=Number($('lmsWeatherHour').value);
  if(!Number.isFinite(lat)||lat < -90||lat>90||!Number.isFinite(lon)||lon < -180||lon>180)throw Error('Enter valid stadium latitude and longitude.');
  if(!day||!Number.isInteger(hour)||hour<0||hour>23)throw Error('Enter match date and UTC hour (0–23).');
  out.textContent='Loading weather…';
  let url,backend=Boolean(base);
  const params={latitude:String(lat),longitude:String(lon),hourly:'temperature_2m,relative_humidity_2m,precipitation,wind_speed_10m',timezone:'UTC'};
  if(base){
   if(!base.toLowerCase().startsWith('https://'))throw Error('Weather backend must use HTTPS.');
   url=base+'/api/weather?'+new URLSearchParams({lat:String(lat),lon:String(lon),date:day,hour:String(hour)});
  }else{
   const today=new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
   if(day<today){url='https://archive-api.open-meteo.com/v1/archive?'+new URLSearchParams({...params,start_date:day,end_date:day})}
   else{url='https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast?'+new URLSearchParams({...params,forecast_days:'16'})}
  }
  const response=await fetch(url,{headers:{Accept:'application/json'}});
  const data=await response.json();
  if(!response.ok||data.error)throw Error(data.reason||data.error||('Weather endpoint HTTP '+response.status));
  let w=data.weather||{},matched=data.matchedHourUtc||'';
  if(!backend){
   const times=data.hourly&&data.hourly.time||[],indices=times.map((t,i)=>({t,i})).filter(x=>x.t.startsWith(day+'T'));
   if(!indices.length)throw Error('Open-Meteo has no hourly data for that date; forecast requests cover up to 16 days ahead.');
   const chosen=indices.reduce((best,x)=>Math.abs(Number(x.t.slice(11,13))-hour)<Math.abs(Number(best.t.slice(11,13))-hour)?x:best);
   const val=key=>data.hourly[key]&&data.hourly[key][chosen.i]!==undefined?data.hourly[key][chosen.i]:null;
   w={temperatureC:val('temperature_2m'),relativeHumidityPct:val('relative_humidity_2m'),precipitationMm:val('precipitation'),windSpeedKmh:val('wind_speed_10m')};
   matched=chosen.t;
  }
  const fetched=Number(data.fetchedAt||Date.now()/1000);
  out.innerHTML='<b>Weather source:</b> '+esc(backend?(data.source||'Open-Meteo via UFI backend'):'Open-Meteo direct')+' · matched '+esc(matched)+' UTC<br>Temperature: '+esc(w.temperatureC??'unknown')+' °C · Humidity: '+esc(w.relativeHumidityPct??'unknown')+'% · Precipitation: '+esc(w.precipitationMm??'unknown')+' mm · Wind: '+esc(w.windSpeedKmh??'unknown')+' km/h<br><span class="muted">Fetched '+new Date(fetched*1000).toISOString()+'. Grid-cell estimate, not a stadium sensor. Verify coordinates and kickoff time.</span>';
 }catch(e){out.innerHTML='<div class="notice warning">'+esc(e.message)+'</div>'}
}
let autoOddsTimer=null,autoOddsSnapshots=[];
async function pollProviderOdds(){
 const status=$('lmsAutoStatus');
 try{
  const base=$('lmsAutoBase').value.trim().replace(/[/]+$/,'');
  const fixture=($('lmsAutoFixture').value.trim()||(($('esiProviderFixtureId')&&$('esiProviderFixtureId').value)||'').trim()),bookmaker=$('lmsAutoBookmaker').value.trim(),market=$('lmsAutoMarket').value.trim();
  if(fixture&&!$('lmsAutoFixture').value.trim())$('lmsAutoFixture').value=fixture;
  if(!base||!base.toLowerCase().startsWith('https://'))throw Error('Enter the deployed UFI HTTPS backend URL.');
  if(!/^[0-9]+$/.test(fixture)||!/^[0-9]+$/.test(bookmaker)||!/^[0-9]+$/.test(market))throw Error('Fixture, bookmaker and market IDs must be numeric.');
  const url=base+'/api/inplay-odds?'+new URLSearchParams({id:fixture,bookmaker,market});
  const response=await fetch(url,{headers:{Accept:'application/json'}});
  const payload=await response.json();
  if(!response.ok||payload.error)throw Error(payload.error||('In-play odds HTTP '+response.status));
  const root=payload.data||{},rows=Array.isArray(root.data)?root.data:(Array.isArray(payload.data)?payload.data:[]);
  const label=r=>String(r.label||r.name||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const pick=names=>rows.find(r=>names.includes(label(r)));
  const home=pick(['home','1']),draw=pick(['draw','x']),away=pick(['away','2']);
  if(!home||!draw||!away)throw Error('Provider returned no complete Home/Draw/Away trio for this bookmaker/market. Verify market ID, bookmaker ID and plan access.');
  const sourceUpdatedAt=[home,draw,away].map(r=>r.latest_bookmaker_update||r.updated_at||'').filter(Boolean).sort().slice(-1)[0]||'';
  const snap={timestamp:new Date(Number(payload.fetchedAt||Date.now()/1000)*1000).toISOString(),sourceUpdatedAt,home:Number(home.value),draw:Number(draw.value),away:Number(away.value),source:'Sportmonks',fixtureId:fixture,bookmakerId:bookmaker,marketId:market};
  if([snap.home,snap.draw,snap.away].some(v=>!Number.isFinite(v)||v<=1))throw Error('Provider returned invalid decimal odds.');
  autoOddsSnapshots.push(snap);autoOddsSnapshots=autoOddsSnapshots.slice(-20);
  $('lmsOddsSnapshots').value=JSON.stringify(autoOddsSnapshots,null,2);
  status.innerHTML='Polling every 5 seconds · '+esc(payload.source||'Sportmonks')+' · latest fetch '+esc(snap.timestamp)+' · source update '+esc(sourceUpdatedAt||'not supplied')+' · '+autoOddsSnapshots.length+' snapshots captured.';
  if(autoOddsSnapshots.length>=2)runMarket();
 }catch(e){status.innerHTML='<span class="warning-text">'+esc(e.message)+'</span>';if(/token|HTTP 4|HTTP 5|plan|entitlement/i.test(e.message)){if(autoOddsTimer){clearInterval(autoOddsTimer);autoOddsTimer=null}}}
}
function startAutoOdds(){
 if(autoOddsTimer)clearInterval(autoOddsTimer);
 autoOddsSnapshots=[];
 $('lmsOddsSnapshots').value='[]';
 pollProviderOdds();
 autoOddsTimer=setInterval(pollProviderOdds,5000);
 $('lmsAutoStatus').textContent='Starting provider odds polling…';
}
function stopAutoOdds(){
 if(autoOddsTimer)clearInterval(autoOddsTimer);
 autoOddsTimer=null;
 $('lmsAutoStatus').textContent='Automatic odds polling stopped.';
}
function syncLiveScore(payload){
 try{
  const fixtureId=String(($('lmsAutoFixture').value||($('esiProviderFixtureId')&&$('esiProviderFixtureId').value)||'')).trim();
  if(!fixtureId)return;
  const root=payload&&payload.data,rows=Array.isArray(root)?root:(Array.isArray(root&&root.data)?root.data:(Array.isArray(root&&root.data&&root.data.data)?root.data.data:[]));
  const f=rows.find(x=>String(x.id??x.fixture_id??x.fixtureId??'')===fixtureId);if(!f)return;
  let h=null,a=null;
  const direct=f.score;
  if(direct&&typeof direct==='object'){
   const dh=direct.home??direct.homeScore??direct.home_score,da=direct.away??direct.awayScore??direct.away_score;
   if(dh!=null&&da!=null&&Number.isInteger(Number(dh))&&Number.isInteger(Number(da))){h=Number(dh);a=Number(da);}
  }
  if((h===null||a===null)&&f.home_score!=null&&f.away_score!=null&&Number.isInteger(Number(f.home_score))&&Number.isInteger(Number(f.away_score))){h=Number(f.home_score);a=Number(f.away_score);}
  if(h===null||a===null){
   const scores=Array.isArray(f.scores)?f.scores:[],current=scores.filter(s=>String(s.description||'').toUpperCase()==='CURRENT');
   for(const s of current){
    const side=String(s.score&&s.score.participant||s.participant&&s.participant.meta&&s.participant.meta.location||s.location||'').toLowerCase();
    const goals=Number(s.score&&s.score.goals!==undefined?s.score.goals:s.goals);
    if(!Number.isInteger(goals)||goals<0)continue;
    if(side==='home')h=goals;if(side==='away')a=goals;
   }
  }
  if(h===null||a===null)return;
  $('lmsScoreH').value=String(h);$('lmsScoreA').value=String(a);
  const minute=Number((f.state&&f.state.minute) ?? (f.status&&f.status.clock) ?? f.minute ?? f.elapsed);
  if(Number.isFinite(minute)&&minute>=0)$('lmsMinute').value=String(minute);
  const status=$('lmsAutoStatus');if(status)status.innerHTML+=' · score synced from live feed: '+h+'–'+a+(Number.isFinite(minute)?' at '+minute+'′':'');
 }catch(_){/* Keep unknown score unknown if provider schema is incomplete. */}
}
if(typeof window!=='undefined'&&window.addEventListener)window.addEventListener('ufi:live-feed',event=>syncLiveScore(event.detail));
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{
 $('lmsRun').addEventListener('click',runMarket);
 $('lmsBacktestRun').addEventListener('click',runBacktest);
 $('lmsWeatherRun').addEventListener('click',runWeather);
 $('lmsAutoStart').addEventListener('click',startAutoOdds);
 $('lmsAutoStop').addEventListener('click',stopAutoOdds);
});
})();

/* Additive UI bridge for the source-fixture -> historical-model pipeline. */
(function () {
  "use strict";
  const byId = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
  const button = byId("ufiRunEndToEndPipeline");
  if (!button) return;

  const baseInput = byId("esiApiBase");
  if (baseInput && !baseInput.value) {
    baseInput.value = "https://universal-football-intelligence.onrender.com";
  }

  button.addEventListener("click", async function () {
    const status = byId("ufiPipelineStatus");
    const output = byId("ufiPipelineOutput");
    const fixtureId = (byId("esiProviderFixtureId")?.value || "").trim();
    const league = (byId("ufiPipelineLeague")?.value || "").trim();
    const seasons = (byId("ufiPipelineSeasons")?.value || "3").trim();
    const base = (baseInput?.value || "").trim().replace(/\/+$/, "");

    if (!base || !/^https:\/\//i.test(base)) {
      status.textContent = "Enter the HTTPS URL of the deployed UFI backend.";
      return;
    }
    if (!/^\d+$/.test(fixtureId)) {
      status.textContent = "Enter a numeric provider fixture ID from the loaded fixture feed.";
      return;
    }
    if (!league) {
      status.textContent = "Choose the historical league code that matches this fixture competition. The app will not guess a mapping.";
      return;
    }
    if (!/^[1-8]$/.test(seasons)) {
      status.textContent = "Season window must be an integer from 1 to 8.";
      return;
    }

    button.disabled = true;
    button.textContent = "Running connected pipeline…";
    status.textContent = "Fetching the source fixture and passing its teams into the historical score model. Market approval remains separate.";
    output.textContent = "Working…";
    try {
      const params = new URLSearchParams({ fixture_id: fixtureId, league, seasons });
      const response = await fetch(base + "/api/pipeline/predict?" + params.toString(), {
        method: "GET",
        headers: { "Accept": "application/json" },
        cache: "no-store"
      });
      const payload = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok || payload.ok === false) {
        throw new Error(payload.error || ("Backend returned HTTP " + response.status));
      }
      const fixture = payload.fixture_source || {};
      const model = payload.historical_model || {};
      const models = model.models || (model.model && model.model.models) || {};
      const topScores = {};
      Object.keys(models).forEach(function (name) {
        topScores[name] = (models[name].top_scores || []).slice(0, 5).map(function (row) {
          return {
            score: String(row.home) + "-" + String(row.away),
            probability: row.p,
            fair_odds: row.fair_odds
          };
        });
      });
      const view = {
        pipeline_status: payload.pipeline_status,
        fixture: fixture,
        stages: payload.stages,
        context_availability: payload.match_context,
        historical_model_status: model.status || model.model?.status || "NEEDS_DATA",
        top_scorelines: topScores,
        market_gate: payload.market_gate || "WAIT",
        gate_reasons: payload.gate_reasons || [],
        warning: payload.warning || "Experimental model output; not a guaranteed result or betting signal."
      };
      output.textContent = JSON.stringify(view, null, 2);
      status.textContent = payload.pipeline_status === "model_ready_market_wait"
        ? "Fixture ingestion and historical model ran. Market gate remains WAIT; tactical/player joins and exact-score market validation are not cleared."
        : "Fixture loaded, but the historical model needs more usable data. No scoreline was fabricated; market gate remains WAIT.";
      if (window.posthog && typeof window.posthog.capture === "function") {
        window.posthog.capture("ufi_pipeline_run", {
          pipeline_status: payload.pipeline_status,
          model_status: view.historical_model_status,
          market_gate: view.market_gate
        });
      }
    } catch (error) {
      status.textContent = "Pipeline did not complete: " + String(error && error.message || error);
      output.textContent = "No integrated prediction was produced. Check backend reachability, fixture ID, league code and historical coverage.";
    } finally {
      button.disabled = false;
      button.textContent = "Run connected fixture → model pipeline";
    }
  });
})();
