#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Fetch public-domain OpenFootball JSON results and normalize them to CSV.

No API key or third-party Python packages required.
Source data: https://github.com/openfootball/football.json
The script downloads only public-domain fixture/result files and saves them locally;
it does not upload datasets to GitHub.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

BASE = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/openfootball/football.json/master"
LEAGUES = {
    "en.1": "England Premier League",
    "en.2": "England Championship",
    "en.3": "England League One",
    "en.4": "England League Two",
    "de.1": "Germany Bundesliga",
    "de.2": "Germany 2. Bundesliga",
    "es.1": "Spain La Liga",
    "es.2": "Spain Segunda Division",
    "it.1": "Italy Serie A",
    "it.2": "Italy Serie B",
    "fr.1": "France Ligue 1",
    "fr.2": "France Ligue 2",
    "nl.1": "Netherlands Eredivisie",
    "pt.1": "Portugal Primeira Liga",
    "sco.1": "Scotland Premiership",
}
FIELDS = [
    "season", "league_code", "league_name", "date", "round",
    "home_team", "away_team", "home_goals", "away_goals", "status",
    "source_url",
]


def season_names(start_year: int, end_year: int):
    for year in range(start_year, end_year + 1):
        yield f"{year}-{str(year + 1)[-2:]}"


def get_json(url: str):
    request = urllib.request.Request(
        url,
        headers={"User-Agent": "UFI-openfootball-importer/1.0 (public-domain data research)"},
    )
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=25) as response:
            return json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
        if exc.code == 404:
            return None
        raise


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--start-year", type=int, default=2010,
                        help="first season start year (default: 2010)")
    parser.add_argument("--end-year", type=int, default=2026,
                        help="last season start year (default: 2026)")
    parser.add_argument("--leagues", default=",".join(LEAGUES),
                        help="comma-separated league codes; default: all listed leagues")
    parser.add_argument("--out", default="data/private/openfootball",
                        help="local output directory (gitignored by this repository)")
    parser.add_argument("--delay", type=float, default=0.15,
                        help="polite pause between requests in seconds")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    selected = [code.strip() for code in args.leagues.split(",") if code.strip()]
    unknown = sorted(set(selected) - set(LEAGUES))
    if unknown:
        parser.error(f"unknown league code(s): {', '.join(unknown)}")
    if args.end_year < args.start_year:
        parser.error("--end-year must be >= --start-year")

    output = Path(args.out)
    raw_dir = output / "raw"
    raw_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    rows = []
    files_found = 0
    requests_made = 0

    for season in season_names(args.start_year, args.end_year):
        for code in selected:
            url = f"{BASE}/{season}/{code}.json"
            requests_made += 1
            try:
                payload = get_json(url)
            except Exception as exc:
                print(f"[WARN] {url}: {exc}")
                continue
            if not payload:
                continue
            files_found += 1
            (raw_dir / f"{season}_{code}.json").write_text(
                json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
            )
            for match in payload.get("matches", []):
                score = match.get("score") or {}
                ft = None

                # OpenFootball files have used more than one score shape:
                # {"ft": [home, away]} and a direct [home, away] list.
                if isinstance(score, dict):
                    ft = score.get("ft") or score.get("fulltime") or score.get("full_time")
                elif isinstance(score, list):
                    if len(score) >= 2 and all(
                        isinstance(value, (int, float)) or
                        (isinstance(value, str) and value.strip().isdigit())
                        for value in score[:2]
                    ):
                        ft = score[:2]
                    else:
                        # Tolerate nested/labelled score structures where possible.
                        for item in score:
                            if isinstance(item, dict):
                                candidate = (
                                    item.get("ft") or item.get("fulltime") or
                                    item.get("full_time") or item.get("score")
                                )
                                if isinstance(candidate, list) and len(candidate) >= 2:
                                    ft = candidate[:2]
                                    break

                has_ft = (
                    isinstance(ft, (list, tuple)) and len(ft) >= 2 and
                    all(value is not None and str(value).strip() != "" for value in ft[:2])
                )
                rows.append({
                    "season": season,
                    "league_code": code,
                    "league_name": LEAGUES[code],
                    "date": match.get("date", ""),
                    "round": match.get("round", ""),
                    "home_team": match.get("team1", ""),
                    "away_team": match.get("team2", ""),
                    "home_goals": ft[0] if has_ft else "",
                    "away_goals": ft[1] if has_ft else "",
                    "status": "finished" if has_ft else "scheduled_or_unscored",
                    "source_url": url,
                })
            print(f"[OK] {season} {code}: {len(payload.get('matches', []))} matches")
            time.sleep(max(0.0, args.delay))

    csv_path = output / "matches.csv"
    with csv_path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=FIELDS)
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)

    manifest = {
        "source": "OpenFootball football.json",
        "source_url": "https://github.com/openfootball/football.json",
        "source_license": "Public domain (verify upstream LICENSE.md before redistribution)",
        "leagues_requested": selected,
        "start_year": args.start_year,
        "end_year": args.end_year,
        "files_found": files_found,
        "requests_made": requests_made,
        "match_rows": len(rows),
        "csv": str(csv_path),
        "note": "Results/fixtures only; odds, player traits and tracking are not included.",
    }
    (output / "manifest.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(manifest, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
    )
    print(f"\nDone: {files_found} season/league files, {len(rows)} match rows")
    print(f"CSV: {csv_path}")
    print("Raw JSON and CSV remain local; nothing was uploaded to GitHub.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Download permitted TheStatsAPI match data to a LOCAL, git-ignored folder.

No API key is stored in this file. Set THESTATSAPI_API_KEY in your shell.
Start with one competition/season to understand request usage before broad pulls.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import re
import sys
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.error import HTTPError, URLError
from urllib.parse import urlencode
from urllib.request import Request, urlopen

BASE_URL = "https://api.thestatsapi.com/api"
DEFAULT_OUT = Path("data/private/thestatsapi")


def request_json(path: str, params: dict | None = None) -> dict:
    key = os.environ.get("THESTATSAPI_API_KEY")
    if not key:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Missing THESTATSAPI_API_KEY. Create a key in your TheStatsAPI account "
            "and set it as an environment variable; never commit it to GitHub."
        )
    query = "?" + urlencode(params) if params else ""
    req = Request(
        BASE_URL + path + query,
        headers={
            "Authorization": f"Bearer {key}",
            "Accept": "application/json",
            "User-Agent": "UFI-local-research-client/1.0",
        },
    )
    try:
        with urlopen(req, timeout=45) as response:
            return json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))
    except HTTPError as exc:
        detail = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")[:1000]
        raise RuntimeError(f"API HTTP {exc.code} for {path}: {detail}") from exc
    except URLError as exc:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Network error for {path}: {exc}") from exc


def fetch_pages(path: str, params: dict | None = None, pause: float = 0.25) -> list:
    collected = []
    page = 1
    while True:
        query = dict(params or {})
        query.update({"page": page, "per_page": 100})
        body = request_json(path, query)
        rows = body.get("data", [])
        if not isinstance(rows, list):
            raise RuntimeError(f"Unexpected response shape for {path}: expected data list")
        collected.extend(rows)
        meta = body.get("meta") or {}
        total_pages = int(meta.get("total_pages", page))
        if page >= total_pages:
            break
        page += 1
        if pause:
            time.sleep(pause)
    return collected


def safe_name(value: str) -> str:
    value = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9._-]+", "_", value).strip("._")
    return value[:100] or "unknown"


def save_json(out_dir: Path, name: str, payload: dict) -> Path:
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path = out_dir / f"{safe_name(name)}.json"
    path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    return path


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--competition-id", help="Fetch a single competition, e.g. comp_3039")
    parser.add_argument("--season-id", help="Optional season ID; requires --competition-id")
    parser.add_argument("--search", help="Search competition names, e.g. Premier League")
    parser.add_argument("--all-competitions", action="store_true",
                        help="Fetch multiple competitions; use --max-competitions to control scope")
    parser.add_argument("--max-competitions", type=int, default=5,
                        help="Maximum competitions for --all-competitions (default: 5; 0 means no client-side cap)")
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_OUT,
                        help=f"Local output directory (default: {DEFAULT_OUT})")
    parser.add_argument("--pause", type=float, default=0.25,
                        help="Seconds between paginated requests (default: 0.25)")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if args.season_id and not args.competition_id:
        parser.error("--season-id requires --competition-id")
    if args.competition_id and args.all_competitions:
        parser.error("Choose --competition-id or --all-competitions, not both")

    try:
        if args.competition_id:
            competitions = [request_json(f"/football/competitions/{args.competition_id}").get("data", {})]
        else:
            competitions = fetch_pages(
                "/football/competitions",
                {"search": args.search} if args.search else {},
                pause=args.pause,
            )
            if not args.all_competitions:
                if args.search:
                    competitions = competitions[:1]
                else:
                    parser.error("Use --competition-id, --search, or explicitly pass --all-competitions")
            elif args.max_competitions > 0:
                competitions = competitions[:args.max_competitions]

        manifest = {
            "source": "TheStatsAPI",
            "source_url": "https://www.thestatsapi.com/",
            "retrieved_at_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
            "scope": {
                "competition_id": args.competition_id,
                "season_id": args.season_id,
                "search": args.search,
                "all_competitions": args.all_competitions,
                "competition_count": len(competitions),
            },
            "records": [],
            "note": "Raw API snapshots; availability varies by competition and season. Keep local unless your data licence permits redistribution.",
        }

        for comp in competitions:
            comp_id = comp.get("id")
            if not comp_id:
                continue
            comp_name = comp.get("name", comp_id)
            if args.season_id:
                seasons = [{"id": args.season_id}]
            else:
                season_body = request_json(f"/football/competitions/{comp_id}/seasons")
                seasons = season_body.get("data", [])
            save_json(args.out, f"competition_{comp_id}", {
                "source": "TheStatsAPI",
                "retrieved_at_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                "data": comp,
            })
            print(f"[{comp_name}] {len(seasons)} season(s)")
            for season in seasons:
                season_id = season.get("id")
                if not season_id:
                    continue
                matches = fetch_pages(
                    "/football/matches",
                    {"competition_id": comp_id, "season_id": season_id, "status": "finished"},
                    pause=args.pause,
                )
                filename = f"matches_{comp_id}_{season_id}"
                save_json(args.out, filename, {
                    "source": "TheStatsAPI",
                    "source_url": "https://www.thestatsapi.com/",
                    "retrieved_at_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                    "competition": {"id": comp_id, "name": comp_name},
                    "season": season,
                    "data": matches,
                })
                manifest["records"].append({
                    "competition_id": comp_id,
                    "competition_name": comp_name,
                    "season_id": season_id,
                    "match_count": len(matches),
                    "file": f"{safe_name(filename)}.json",
                })
                print(f"  {season_id}: saved {len(matches)} finished matches")
                if args.pause:
                    time.sleep(args.pause)

        manifest_path = save_json(args.out, "manifest", manifest)
        print(f"Done. Manifest: {manifest_path}")
        print("Files are local and git-ignored; do not upload raw data unless your licence permits it.")
        return 0
    except (RuntimeError, KeyError, ValueError) as exc:
        print(f"ERROR: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Chronological UFI research model: Elo probabilities plus a Poisson score model.

All predictions for a calendar date are made before any results from that date
are added. This is research infrastructure, not a betting recommendation.
"""
import argparse, csv, json, math
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from collections import defaultdict, deque
from pathlib import Path

LABELS = ("H", "D", "A")
def probs_from_ratings(home_rating, away_rating, draw_base=0.26):
    # Bradley-Terry-style home/away strength, with an explicit draw component.
    diff = max(-800.0, min(800.0, (home_rating + 55.0 - away_rating) * math.log(10) / 400))
    decisive = 1.0 / (1.0 + math.exp(-diff))
    home = (1.0 - draw_base) * decisive
    away = (1.0 - draw_base) * (1.0 - decisive)
    return (home, draw_base, away)

def poisson_probs(lam, cap=10):
    lam = max(0.05, min(6.0, float(lam)))
    out = [math.exp(-lam)]
    for k in range(1, cap + 1):
        out.append(out[-1] * lam / k)
    total = sum(out)
    return [x / total for x in out]

def poisson_1x2(home_lam, away_lam):
    hp, ap = poisson_probs(home_lam), poisson_probs(away_lam)
    h = d = a = 0.0
    for i, pi in enumerate(hp):
        for j, pj in enumerate(ap):
            if i > j: h += pi * pj
            elif i == j: d += pi * pj
            else: a += pi * pj
    total = h + d + a
    return (h/total, d/total, a/total)

def score(p, actual):
    brier = sum((p[i] - (1 if LABELS[i] == actual else 0)) ** 2 for i in range(3))
    logloss = -math.log(max(1e-12, p[LABELS.index(actual)]))
    return brier, logloss

def metrics(rows, prefix):
    if not rows: return {"matches": 0, "accuracy": None, "brier": None, "log_loss": None}
    n = len(rows)
    return {
        "matches": n,
        "accuracy": round(sum(r[prefix+"_correct"] for r in rows)/n, 6),
        "brier": round(sum(r[prefix+"_brier"] for r in rows)/n, 6),
        "log_loss": round(sum(r[prefix+"_logloss"] for r in rows)/n, 6)
    }

def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    ap.add_argument("--input", default="output/openfootball/matches.csv")
    ap.add_argument("--out", default="output/openfootball/advanced")
    ap.add_argument("--min-history", type=int, default=5)
    ap.add_argument("--window", type=int, default=12)
    ap.add_argument("--elo-k", type=float, default=20.0)
    ap.add_argument("--odds-csv", default="", help="Optional permitted, timestamped odds CSV; never scraped by this script")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    if args.min_history < 1 or args.window < 1 or args.elo_k <= 0: ap.error("history/window must be positive and Elo K must be > 0")
    out = Path(args.out); out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(args.input, encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f: source = list(csv.DictReader(f))
    source.sort(key=lambda r: (r.get("date",""), r.get("league_code",""), r.get("home_team",""), r.get("away_team","")))
    by_date = defaultdict(list)
    skipped = 0
    for r in source:
        try:
            date = (r.get("date") or "").strip()
            league = (r.get("league_code") or "").strip()
            home = (r.get("home_team") or "").strip()
            away = (r.get("away_team") or "").strip()
            hg, ag = float(r.get("home_goals","")), float(r.get("away_goals",""))
            if not date or not league or not home or not away or not math.isfinite(hg) or not math.isfinite(ag) or hg < 0 or ag < 0 or not hg.is_integer() or not ag.is_integer():
                raise ValueError()
            r["_date"], r["_league"], r["_home"], r["_away"], r["_hg"], r["_ag"] = date, league, home, away, int(hg), int(ag)
            by_date[date].append(r)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            skipped += 1
    ratings = defaultdict(lambda: 1500.0)
    played = defaultdict(int)
    hgf, hga, agf, aga = defaultdict(lambda: deque(maxlen=args.window)), defaultdict(lambda: deque(maxlen=args.window)), defaultdict(lambda: deque(maxlen=args.window)), defaultdict(lambda: deque(maxlen=args.window))
    league_h, league_a = defaultdict(lambda: deque(maxlen=100)), defaultdict(lambda: deque(maxlen=100))
    outcome_counts = defaultdict(lambda: [0,0,0])
    predictions, ledger = [], []
    # optional user-supplied odds must be dated and have decimal odds available pre-match.
    odds = {}
    odds_status = "not supplied; ROI and bookmaker comparison intentionally not computed"
    if args.odds_csv:
        with open(args.odds_csv, encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f:
            for row in csv.DictReader(f):
                key = (row.get("date","").strip(), row.get("league_code","").strip(), row.get("home_team","").strip(), row.get("away_team","").strip())
                try:
                    vals = [float(row[k]) for k in ("home_odds","draw_odds","away_odds")]
                    captured = datetime.fromisoformat(row["captured_at_utc"].replace("Z", "+00:00"))
                    kickoff = datetime.fromisoformat(row["kickoff_at_utc"].replace("Z", "+00:00"))
                    if captured.tzinfo is None or kickoff.tzinfo is None or captured >= kickoff:
                        continue
                    if all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 1 for v in vals) and all(key):
                        odds[key] = {"values": vals, "captured_at_utc": captured.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat(), "kickoff_at_utc": kickoff.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat()}
                except (KeyError, ValueError, TypeError): continue
        odds_status = f"loaded {len(odds)} valid odds rows with timezone-aware capture timestamps earlier than kickoff"
    per_league = defaultdict(list)
    for date in sorted(by_date):
        updates = []
        for r in by_date[date]:
            league, home, away, hg, ag = r["_league"], r["_home"], r["_away"], r["_hg"], r["_ag"]
            hk, ak = (league, home), (league, away)
            actual = "H" if hg > ag else ("D" if hg == ag else "A")
            ready = played[hk] >= args.min_history and played[ak] >= args.min_history and len(league_h[league]) >= args.min_history
            if ready:
                # Elo forecast.
                elo_p = probs_from_ratings(ratings[hk], ratings[ak])
                # Shrunk rolling venue-specific goals forecast; league means anchor small samples.
                bh = sum(league_h[league])/len(league_h[league])
                ba = sum(league_a[league])/len(league_a[league])
                h_attack = (sum(hgf[hk])/len(hgf[hk]) if hgf[hk] else bh) / max(.1,bh)
                h_def = (sum(hga[hk])/len(hga[hk]) if hga[hk] else ba) / max(.1,ba)
                a_attack = (sum(agf[ak])/len(agf[ak]) if agf[ak] else ba) / max(.1,ba)
                a_def = (sum(aga[ak])/len(aga[ak]) if aga[ak] else bh) / max(.1,bh)
                hl = max(.15,min(4.5,bh*h_attack*a_def))
                al = max(.15,min(4.5,ba*a_attack*h_def))
                goal_p = poisson_1x2(hl, al)
                # Equal-weight ensemble is fixed before testing; no test-set tuning.
                ensemble = tuple((elo_p[i]+goal_p[i])/2 for i in range(3))
                counts = outcome_counts[league]; denom = sum(counts)+3
                baseline = tuple((counts[i]+1)/denom for i in range(3))
                pred = {"date":date,"season":r.get("season",""),"league_code":league,"home_team":home,"away_team":away,
                    "home_goals":hg,"away_goals":ag,"actual_1x2":actual,
                    "elo_p_home":round(elo_p[0],6),"elo_p_draw":round(elo_p[1],6),"elo_p_away":round(elo_p[2],6),
                    "poisson_p_home":round(goal_p[0],6),"poisson_p_draw":round(goal_p[1],6),"poisson_p_away":round(goal_p[2],6),
                    "ensemble_p_home":round(ensemble[0],6),"ensemble_p_draw":round(ensemble[1],6),"ensemble_p_away":round(ensemble[2],6),
                    "baseline_p_home":round(baseline[0],6),"baseline_p_draw":round(baseline[1],6),"baseline_p_away":round(baseline[2],6),
                    "predicted_1x2":LABELS[max(range(3),key=lambda i:ensemble[i])],"method":"fixed 50/50 Elo + rolling-goals Poisson; prior dates only"}
                for name, p in (("ensemble",ensemble),("baseline",baseline),("elo",elo_p),("poisson",goal_p)):
                    b,ll=score(p,actual); pred[name+"_brier"]=round(b,6); pred[name+"_logloss"]=round(ll,6); pred[name+"_correct"]=int(LABELS[max(range(3),key=lambda i:p[i])]==actual)
                predictions.append(pred); per_league[league].append(pred)
                # Paper ledger only where matching, valid decimal odds were explicitly supplied.
                key=(date,league,home,away)
                if key in odds:
                    odds_row=odds[key]; o=odds_row["values"]; implied=[1/x for x in o]; total=sum(implied); fair=[x/total for x in implied]
                    pick=max(range(3),key=lambda i:ensemble[i])
                    ledger.append({"date":date,"league_code":league,"home_team":home,"away_team":away,"actual_1x2":actual,
                        "home_odds":o[0],"draw_odds":o[1],"away_odds":o[2],"odds_captured_at_utc":odds_row["captured_at_utc"],"kickoff_at_utc":odds_row["kickoff_at_utc"],"model_probability":round(ensemble[pick],6),
                        "market_implied_probability_raw":round(implied[pick],6),"market_probability_no_vig":round(fair[pick],6),
                        "selection":LABELS[pick],"flat_stake_units":1,"paper_profit_units":round(o[pick]-1 if LABELS[pick]==actual else -1,4),
                        "warning":"retrospective paper test only; verify odds timestamp predates kickoff and account for limits/voids"})
            updates.append((league,hk,ak,hg,ag,actual))
        for league,hk,ak,hg,ag,actual in updates:
            # Elo updates occur only after the entire date has been predicted.
            expected=1/(1+10**((ratings[ak]-(ratings[hk]+55))/400))
            actual_home=1.0 if actual=="H" else (0.5 if actual=="D" else 0.0)
            change=args.elo_k*(actual_home-expected)
            ratings[hk]+=change; ratings[ak]-=change
            hgf[hk].append(hg); hga[hk].append(ag); agf[ak].append(ag); aga[ak].append(hg)
            league_h[league].append(hg); league_a[league].append(ag); outcome_counts[league][LABELS.index(actual)]+=1
            played[hk]+=1; played[ak]+=1
    fields=list(predictions[0]) if predictions else ["date","league_code","home_team","away_team","actual_1x2"]
    with open(out/"advanced_predictions.csv","w",encoding="utf-8-sig",newline="") as f:
        w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=fields); w.writeheader(); w.writerows(predictions)
    summary={"data_source":"OpenFootball fixtures/results","source_rows":len(source),"valid_scored_rows":sum(len(v) for v in by_date.values()),"backtested_matches":len(predictions),"skipped_invalid_rows":skipped,
        "same_date_leakage_control":"All fixtures on a calendar date predicted before any result that date updates Elo or form.",
        "models":{"fixed_elo_plus_poisson_ensemble":metrics(predictions,"ensemble"),"elo_only":metrics(predictions,"elo"),"poisson_only":metrics(predictions,"poisson"),"league_frequency_baseline":metrics(predictions,"baseline")},
        "per_league":{k:{"ensemble":metrics(v,"ensemble"),"elo":metrics(v,"elo"),"poisson":metrics(v,"poisson"),"baseline":metrics(v,"baseline")} for k,v in sorted(per_league.items())},
        "odds_status":odds_status,"paper_bet_count":len(ledger),"paper_profit_units":round(sum(x["paper_profit_units"] for x in ledger),4) if ledger else None,
        "paper_roi":round(sum(x["paper_profit_units"] for x in ledger)/len(ledger),6) if ledger else None,
        "limitations":["No player features are inferred from results-only data.","Odds ROI is omitted unless an odds CSV is explicitly supplied.","A retrospective paper ledger is not proof of live profitability; odds must be timestamped before kickoff.","Same-date fixtures are withheld together because kickoff times are absent.","Ensemble weights are fixed, not tuned on test outcomes."],
        "predictions_csv":str(out/"advanced_predictions.csv"),"paper_ledger_csv":str(out/"paper_betting_ledger.csv")}
    with open(out/"metrics.json","w",encoding="utf-8") as f: json.dump(summary,f,indent=2)
    with open(out/"paper_betting_ledger.csv","w",encoding="utf-8-sig",newline="") as f:
        cols=list(ledger[0]) if ledger else ["date","league_code","home_team","away_team","selection","home_odds","draw_odds","away_odds","paper_profit_units","warning"]
        w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=cols); w.writeheader(); w.writerows(ledger)
    print(json.dumps(summary,indent=2))
    if not predictions: raise SystemExit("No eligible matches; no advanced model metrics produced.")

if __name__ == "__main__": main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Run UFI's coordinated Phase 1–3 research pipeline with explicit evidence gates."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, csv, json, subprocess, sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PLAYER_FIELDS = {"player_name","source_name","source_url","preferred_foot","weak_foot_evidence","role","observed_style_tags","efootball_style_tags","confidence_0_to_1","record_type"}
VALID_FEET = {"left","right","both","unknown"}

def audit_player_registry(path: Path) -> dict:
    if not path.is_file():
        return {"file":str(path),"exists":False,"records":0,"status":"missing","errors":[],"warnings":["No registry CSV supplied."],"footedness_known_records":0,"weak_foot_evidence_records":0,"style_tagged_records":0,"efootball_tagged_records":0,"used_as_prediction_feature":False}
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8-sig",newline="") as handle:
        reader=csv.DictReader(handle); headers=set(reader.fieldnames or [])
        rows=[r for r in reader if (r.get("player_name") or "").strip()]
    missing=sorted(PLAYER_FIELDS-headers); errors=[]; warnings=[]
    feet=weak=styles=game_styles=0
    for line,row in enumerate(rows,start=2):
        foot=(row.get("preferred_foot") or "unknown").strip().lower()
        if foot not in VALID_FEET: errors.append(f"Row {line}: invalid preferred_foot '{foot}'.")
        if foot in {"left","right","both"}: feet+=1
        if (row.get("weak_foot_evidence") or "").strip(): weak+=1
        if (row.get("observed_style_tags") or "").strip(): styles+=1
        if (row.get("efootball_style_tags") or "").strip(): game_styles+=1
        if not (row.get("source_name") or "").strip() or not (row.get("source_url") or "").strip():
            warnings.append(f"Row {line}: source provenance missing; do not treat claims as verified.")
        try:
            confidence=float(row.get("confidence_0_to_1") or "")
            if not 0 <= confidence <= 1: raise ValueError()
        except ValueError: warnings.append(f"Row {line}: confidence_0_to_1 missing or outside 0–1.")
    if missing: errors.append("Missing required headers: "+", ".join(missing))
    if not rows: warnings.append("Registry has no player records; player-aware prediction is disabled.")
    status="validated_with_current_checks" if rows and not errors and not warnings else ("needs_correction" if errors else "needs_evidence_or_more_records")
    return {"file":str(path),"exists":True,"records":len(rows),"status":status,"errors":errors,"warnings":warnings,"footedness_known_records":feet,"weak_foot_evidence_records":weak,"style_tagged_records":styles,"efootball_tagged_records":game_styles,"required_headers_present":not missing,"used_as_prediction_feature":False,"reason_not_used":"OpenFootball results do not identify lineups or player minutes; player claims are not joined to fixtures without verified player-match participation data."}

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument("--matches",default="output/openfootball/matches.csv")
    p.add_argument("--out",default="output/openfootball/integrated")
    p.add_argument("--player-registry",default="data/private/player_registry.csv")
    p.add_argument("--odds-csv",default="data/private/pre_match_odds.csv")
    p.add_argument("--skip-advanced",action="store_true")
    a=p.parse_args(); out=Path(a.out); out.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    advanced_out=out/"advanced"; odds_path=Path(a.odds_csv); player_path=Path(a.player_registry)
    if not player_path.is_file(): player_path=ROOT/"data/templates/player_registry_template.csv"
    if not a.skip_advanced:
        cmd=[sys.executable,str(ROOT/"tools/ufi_advanced_backtest.py"),"--input",str(Path(a.matches)),"--out",str(advanced_out)]
        if odds_path.is_file() and odds_path.stat().st_size>0: cmd += ["--odds-csv",str(odds_path)]
        subprocess.run(cmd,check=True)
    metrics_path=advanced_out/"metrics.json"
    if not metrics_path.is_file(): raise SystemExit(f"Advanced metrics not found: {metrics_path}")
    m=json.loads(metrics_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")); players=audit_player_registry(player_path)
    odds_supplied=odds_path.is_file() and odds_path.stat().st_size>0
    market_audit={"status":"not_run_no_odds_supplied"}
    if odds_supplied:
        audit_out=out/"market_audit"
        audit_cmd=[sys.executable,str(ROOT/"tools/ufi_probability_market_audit.py"),
                   "--predictions",str(advanced_out/"advanced_predictions.csv"),
                   "--odds-csv",str(odds_path),"--out",str(audit_out)]
        audit_run=subprocess.run(audit_cmd,check=False,capture_output=True,text=True)
        audit_json=audit_out/"probability_market_audit.json"
        if audit_json.is_file():
            market_audit=json.loads(audit_json.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            market_audit["status"]="completed" if market_audit.get("matched_scored_fixtures",0)>0 else "no_matched_fixtures"
        else:
            market_audit={"status":"failed_to_write_report","return_code":audit_run.returncode,
                          "error":(audit_run.stderr or "")[-1000:]}
    models=m.get("models",{})
    phase1={"status":"evaluated","backtested_matches":m.get("backtested_matches",0),"models":models,"same_date_leakage_control":m.get("same_date_leakage_control","")}
    phase2_status="player evidence available for future integration" if players["records"] else "blocked_pending_sourced_player_records"
    phase2={"status":phase2_status,"registry_audit":players,"prediction_integration":"disabled until player-to-match participation, minutes, and pre-kickoff availability are verified"}
    phase3={"status":"paper_test_completed_with_supplied_odds" if m.get("paper_bet_count",0) else ("odds_supplied_but_no_matches_joined" if odds_supplied else "ready_but_no_odds_supplied"),"odds_file_supplied":odds_supplied,"odds_status":m.get("odds_status"),"paper_bet_count":m.get("paper_bet_count",0),"paper_profit_units":m.get("paper_profit_units"),"paper_roi":m.get("paper_roi"),"probability_market_audit":market_audit,"warning":"Retrospective paper results are not evidence of future profitability. Quotes must be captured before kickoff and source terms must permit this use."}
    report={"project":"Universal Football Intelligence","pipeline":"integrated Phase 1 + Phase 2 evidence audit + Phase 3 paper-validation gate","phase_1_match_model":phase1,"phase_2_player_intelligence":phase2,"phase_3_odds_validation":phase3,"probability_market_audit":market_audit,"limitations":["Results-only OpenFootball data cannot provide player footedness, play style, lineups, substitutions or tracking.","Player registry fields are audited but not used as forecast features until player-match participation and timing are verified.","ROI is not computed unless timestamped, authorized odds are supplied.","Accuracy alone does not establish profitability; compare Brier score, log loss, market baselines and multiple chronological periods."]}
    (out/"ufi_integrated_report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2),encoding="utf-8")
    ens=models.get("fixed_elo_plus_poisson_ensemble",{}); elo=models.get("elo_only",{}); base=models.get("league_frequency_baseline",{})
    lines=["# UFI integrated research report","","All three phases are coordinated in one pipeline. Status distinguishes tested components from data-gated components.","","## Phase 1 — match prediction",f"- Eligible historical matches: {phase1['backtested_matches']:,}",f"- Elo + Poisson ensemble accuracy: {ens.get('accuracy')}",f"- Elo-only accuracy: {elo.get('accuracy')}",f"- Historical league baseline accuracy: {base.get('accuracy')}",f"- Leakage control: {phase1['same_date_leakage_control']}","","## Phase 2 — player intelligence",f"- Status: {phase2_status}",f"- Registry records: {players['records']}",f"- Records with known foot: {players['footedness_known_records']}",f"- Records with weak-foot evidence: {players['weak_foot_evidence_records']}",f"- Records with observed style tags: {players['style_tagged_records']}","- Player attributes are not fed into predictions without verified player-match participation data.","","## Phase 3 — paper validation",f"- Status: {phase3['status']}",f"- Odds status: {phase3['odds_status']}",f"- Paper bets: {phase3['paper_bet_count']}",f"- Paper profit (units): {phase3['paper_profit_units']}",f"- Paper ROI: {phase3['paper_roi']}",f"- Probability-versus-market audit: {market_audit.get('status')}",f"- Mean Brier difference (model minus market): {market_audit.get('mean_brier_difference_model_minus_market')}",f"- Mean log-loss difference (model minus market): {market_audit.get('mean_log_loss_difference_model_minus_market')}","","## Important limitations",*[f"- {x}" for x in report["limitations"]],"","Machine-readable detail: ufi_integrated_report.json; model predictions and ledger are in the advanced/ subfolder."]
    (out/"ufi_integrated_report.md").write_text("\n".join(lines)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({"report_json":str(out/"ufi_integrated_report.json"),"report_markdown":str(out/"ufi_integrated_report.md"),"phase_1":phase1["status"],"phase_2":phase2_status,"phase_3":phase3["status"],"player_records":players["records"],"paper_bets":phase3["paper_bet_count"]},indent=2))

if __name__=="__main__": main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Small secret-safe HTTP proxy for Sportmonks Football API v3.

Run behind a private/controlled backend (Replit/Render/VPS), never expose the
SPORTMONKS_API_TOKEN in browser JavaScript or commit it to the repository.
This is a data transport adapter, not a prediction model.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from datetime import date

API_BASE = "https://api.sportmonks.com/v3/football"
TOKEN = os.environ.get("SPORTMONKS_API_TOKEN", "").strip()
PORT = int(os.environ.get("PORT", "8080"))
ALLOWED_ORIGIN = os.environ.get("UFI_ALLOWED_ORIGIN", "https://edoborrmagdalene9-dev.github.io").strip()
CACHE: dict[str, tuple[float, object]] = {}
CACHE_TTL = {"live": 5, "fixtures": 300, "fixture": 5, "weather": 1800}
REQUESTS: dict[str, list[float]] = {}
RATE_WINDOW = 60
RATE_LIMIT = 60


class ProviderError(Exception):
    pass


def provider_get(path: str, params: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> object:
    if not TOKEN:
        raise ProviderError("SPORTMONKS_API_TOKEN is not configured on the server.")
    query = dict(params or {})
    query["api_token"] = TOKEN
    url = API_BASE + path + "?" + urllib.parse.urlencode(query)
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"Accept": "application/json", "User-Agent": "UFI-research/1.0"})
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=12) as response:
            if response.status != 200:
                raise ProviderError(f"Provider returned HTTP {response.status}")
            return json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
        # Never echo request URL: it contains the private token.
        raise ProviderError(f"Football data provider returned HTTP {exc.code}. Check plan, endpoint and token.") from exc
    except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise ProviderError(f"Football data provider unavailable: {type(exc).__name__}") from exc


def cached(key: str, ttl: int, fn):
    now = time.time()
    if key in CACHE and now - CACHE[key][0] < ttl:
        return CACHE[key][1], True
    value = fn()
    CACHE[key] = (now, value)
    return value, False


def origin_allowed(origin: str | None) -> bool:
    # Non-browser clients may omit Origin. Browser origins must match exactly.
    return not origin or origin == ALLOWED_ORIGIN


def rate_limited(client_ip: str, now: float | None = None) -> bool:
    """Apply a per-client sliding-window limit; return True when over limit."""
    current = time.time() if now is None else now
    recent = [stamp for stamp in REQUESTS.get(client_ip, []) if current - stamp < RATE_WINDOW]
    if len(recent) >= RATE_LIMIT:
        REQUESTS[client_ip] = recent
        return True
    recent.append(current)
    REQUESTS[client_ip] = recent
    return False


def open_meteo_weather(lat: float, lon: float, day: str, hour: int) -> object:
    """Fetch one hourly weather observation/forecast from Open-Meteo (no API key)."""
    today = date.today().isoformat()
    archive = day < today
    base = "https://archive-api.open-meteo.com/v1/archive" if archive else "https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast"
    params = {
        "latitude": str(lat), "longitude": str(lon), "start_date": day, "end_date": day,
        "hourly": "temperature_2m,relative_humidity_2m,precipitation,wind_speed_10m",
        "timezone": "UTC"
    }
    # Forecast uses forecast_days/date selection; archive uses start/end date.
    if not archive:
        params.pop("start_date", None)
        params.pop("end_date", None)
        params["forecast_days"] = "7"
    url = base + "?" + urllib.parse.urlencode(params)
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"Accept": "application/json", "User-Agent": "UFI-research/1.0"})
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=12) as response:
            payload = json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))
    except (urllib.error.URLError, urllib.error.HTTPError, TimeoutError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise ProviderError("Open-Meteo weather source unavailable: " + type(exc).__name__) from exc
    hourly = payload.get("hourly") or {}
    times = hourly.get("time") or []
    if not times:
        raise ProviderError("Weather source returned no hourly observations for that date/location.")
    index = min(range(len(times)), key=lambda i: abs((times[i].split("T")[0] != day) * 100000 + (int(times[i][11:13]) - hour) ** 2))
    if not times[index].startswith(day + "T"):
        raise ProviderError("Requested date is outside the provider's available weather window.")
    def value(key):
        values = hourly.get(key) or []
        return values[index] if index < len(values) else None
    return {
        "source": "Open-Meteo", "sourceUrl": base, "fetchedAt": time.time(),
        "requested": {"latitude": lat, "longitude": lon, "date": day, "hourUtc": hour},
        "matchedHourUtc": times[index], "archive": archive,
        "weather": {"temperatureC": value("temperature_2m"), "relativeHumidityPct": value("relative_humidity_2m"),
                    "precipitationMm": value("precipitation"), "windSpeedKmh": value("wind_speed_10m")},
        "note": "Grid-cell weather estimate, not stadium sensor data; location and kickoff-hour mapping must be checked."
    }


def get_payload(route: str, query: dict[str, list[str]]) -> tuple[object, bool]:
    if route == "/api/health":
        return {"ok": True, "provider": "Sportmonks Football API v3", "tokenConfigured": bool(TOKEN),
                "note": "Provider connectivity and coverage must be verified; health does not imply model accuracy."}, False
    if route == "/api/weather":
        try:
            lat = float((query.get("lat") or [""])[0])
            lon = float((query.get("lon") or [""])[0])
            raw_date = (query.get("date") or [date.today().isoformat()])[0]
            hour = int((query.get("hour") or ["12"])[0])
            date.fromisoformat(raw_date)
            if not (-90 <= lat <= 90 and -180 <= lon <= 180 and 0 <= hour <= 23):
                raise ValueError()
        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
            raise ProviderError("weather requires valid lat, lon, date=YYYY-MM-DD and hour=0..23") from exc
        key = f"weather:{lat:.3f}:{lon:.3f}:{raw_date}:{hour}"
        data, hit = cached(key, CACHE_TTL["weather"], lambda: open_meteo_weather(lat, lon, raw_date, hour))
        data["cacheHit"] = hit
        return data, hit
    if route == "/api/inplay-odds":
        fixture_id = (query.get("id") or [""])[0].strip()
        bookmaker_id = (query.get("bookmaker") or [""])[0].strip()
        market_id = (query.get("market") or ["1"])[0].strip()
        if not fixture_id.isdigit() or not bookmaker_id.isdigit() or not market_id.isdigit():
            raise ProviderError("inplay-odds requires numeric id=fixture, bookmaker=provider bookmaker ID and market=market ID")
        filters = f"bookmakers:{bookmaker_id};markets:{market_id}"
        key = f"inplay-odds:{fixture_id}:{bookmaker_id}:{market_id}"
        data, hit = cached(key, CACHE_TTL["live"], lambda: provider_get(
            "/odds/inplay/fixtures/" + fixture_id,
            {"include": "market;bookmaker;fixture", "filters": filters}))
        return {"source": "Sportmonks in-play odds", "fetchedAt": time.time(), "fixtureId": fixture_id,
                "bookmakerId": bookmaker_id, "marketId": market_id, "cacheHit": hit, "data": data}, hit
    if route == "/api/fixtures":
        raw_date = (query.get("date") or [date.today().isoformat()])[0]
        try:
            date.fromisoformat(raw_date)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise ProviderError("date must use YYYY-MM-DD") from exc
        includes = "participants;scores;state;events.type;lineups.details.type;statistics.type;referees;venue"
        key = "fixtures:" + raw_date
        data, hit = cached(key, CACHE_TTL["fixtures"], lambda: provider_get(
            "/fixtures/date/" + raw_date, {"include": includes, "per_page": "100"}))
        return {"source": "Sportmonks", "fetchedAt": time.time(), "date": raw_date, "cacheHit": hit, "data": data}, hit
    if route == "/api/live":
        includes = "participants;scores;state;events.type;lineups.details.type;statistics.type;referees;venue"
        key = "live"
        data, hit = cached(key, CACHE_TTL["live"], lambda: provider_get("/livescores/inplay", {"include": includes}))
        return {"source": "Sportmonks", "fetchedAt": time.time(), "cacheHit": hit, "data": data}, hit
    if route == "/api/fixture":
        fixture_id = (query.get("id") or [""])[0].strip()
        if not fixture_id.isdigit():
            raise ProviderError("id must be a numeric provider fixture ID")
        includes = "participants;scores;state;events.type;lineups.details.type;statistics.type;referees;venue"
        key = "fixture:" + fixture_id
        data, hit = cached(key, CACHE_TTL["fixture"], lambda: provider_get(
            "/fixtures/" + fixture_id, {"include": includes}))
        return {"source": "Sportmonks", "fetchedAt": time.time(), "cacheHit": hit, "data": data}, hit
    raise ProviderError("Unknown endpoint. Use /api/health, /api/fixtures?date=YYYY-MM-DD, /api/live, /api/fixture?id=ID, /api/inplay-odds?id=ID&bookmaker=ID&market=1 or /api/weather?lat=..&lon=..&date=YYYY-MM-DD&hour=..")


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def _headers(self, status: int = 200):
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
        origin = self.headers.get("Origin")
        if origin and origin == ALLOWED_ORIGIN:
            self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", origin)
            self.send_header("Vary", "Origin")
        self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, OPTIONS")
        self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Content-Type")
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        self.end_headers()

    def _json(self, status: int, payload: object):
        self._headers(status)
        if status != 204:
            self.wfile.write(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8"))

    def do_OPTIONS(self):
        origin = self.headers.get("Origin")
        if not origin_allowed(origin):
            self._json(403, {"ok": False, "error": "Origin not allowed"})
            return
        self._json(204, {})

    def do_GET(self):
        origin = self.headers.get("Origin")
        if not origin_allowed(origin):
            self._json(403, {"ok": False, "error": "Origin not allowed"})
            return
        if rate_limited(self.client_address[0]):
            self._json(429, {"ok": False, "error": "Rate limit exceeded; retry shortly."})
            return
        parsed = urllib.parse.urlparse(self.path)
        try:
            payload, _ = get_payload(parsed.path, urllib.parse.parse_qs(parsed.query))
            self._json(200, payload)
        except ProviderError as exc:
            status = 400 if "must use" in str(exc) or "must be" in str(exc) or "Unknown endpoint" in str(exc) else 502
            self._json(status, {"ok": False, "error": str(exc)})

    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
        # Avoid logging query strings or secrets.
        print(f"{self.address_string()} {self.command} {urllib.parse.urlparse(self.path).path}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if not TOKEN:
        print("WARNING: SPORTMONKS_API_TOKEN is unset; /api/health works but data endpoints will not.")
    print(f"UFI live-data proxy listening on 0.0.0.0:{PORT}")
    ThreadingHTTPServer(("0.0.0.0", PORT), Handler).serve_forever()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Research-only audit of model probabilities against timestamp-valid 1X2 odds.

This tool measures probability differences and forecast quality. It does not rank
or recommend bets and does not estimate profit. Odds must be user-supplied with
timezone-aware capture timestamps strictly before kickoff.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, csv, json, math
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

OUTCOMES = ("H", "D", "A")
MODEL_PREFIX = "ensemble_p_"


def parse_timestamp(value: str) -> datetime:
    """Parse ISO-8601 timestamp and reject timestamps without an explicit zone."""
    dt = datetime.fromisoformat(value.strip().replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    if dt.tzinfo is None or dt.utcoffset() is None:
        raise ValueError("timestamp must include a timezone")
    return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc)


def normalize_market(odds: list[float]) -> tuple[list[float], float]:
    """Return margin-normalized implied probabilities and raw overround."""
    if len(odds) != 3 or any(not math.isfinite(x) or x <= 1 for x in odds):
        raise ValueError("expected three finite decimal odds values greater than 1")
    raw = [1.0 / x for x in odds]
    total = sum(raw)
    if total <= 0:
        raise ValueError("invalid implied probability total")
    return [x / total for x in raw], total - 1.0


def brier(probs: list[float], actual: str) -> float:
    if actual not in OUTCOMES or len(probs) != 3:
        raise ValueError("invalid outcome or probability vector")
    if any(not math.isfinite(p) or p < 0 or p > 1 for p in probs):
        raise ValueError("probabilities must be finite and in [0, 1]")
    if abs(sum(probs) - 1.0) > 1e-4:
        raise ValueError("probabilities must sum to 1")
    return sum((probs[i] - (1.0 if OUTCOMES[i] == actual else 0.0)) ** 2 for i in range(3))


def log_loss(probs: list[float], actual: str) -> float:
    if actual not in OUTCOMES:
        raise ValueError("invalid outcome")
    if len(probs) != 3 or any(not math.isfinite(p) or p < 0 or p > 1 for p in probs):
        raise ValueError("invalid probability vector")
    if abs(sum(probs) - 1.0) > 1e-4:
        raise ValueError("probabilities must sum to 1")
    return -math.log(max(1e-15, probs[OUTCOMES.index(actual)]))


def load_odds(path: Path) -> tuple[dict, dict]:
    valid, ambiguous = {}, set()
    rejected = {"missing_or_invalid_timestamp": 0, "bad_odds": 0, "duplicate_key": 0}
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f:
        for row in csv.DictReader(f):
            key = tuple((row.get(k) or "").strip() for k in
                        ("date", "league_code", "home_team", "away_team"))
            try:
                if not all(key):
                    raise ValueError("missing fixture key")
                captured = parse_timestamp(row["captured_at_utc"])
                kickoff = parse_timestamp(row["kickoff_at_utc"])
                if captured >= kickoff:
                    rejected["missing_or_invalid_timestamp"] += 1
                    continue
            except (KeyError, ValueError, TypeError):
                rejected["missing_or_invalid_timestamp"] += 1
                continue
            try:
                odds = [float(row[k]) for k in ("home_odds", "draw_odds", "away_odds")]
                market, overround = normalize_market(odds)
            except (KeyError, ValueError, TypeError):
                rejected["bad_odds"] += 1
                continue
            if key in ambiguous:
                rejected["duplicate_key"] += 1
                continue
            if key in valid:
                rejected["duplicate_key"] += 1
                # Ambiguous duplicate prices are excluded rather than silently choosing one.
                valid.pop(key, None)
                ambiguous.add(key)
                continue
            valid[key] = {"odds": odds, "market": market, "overround": overround,
                          "captured_at_utc": captured.isoformat(), "kickoff_at_utc": kickoff.isoformat()}
    return valid, rejected


def run_audit(predictions_path: Path, odds_path: Path, out_dir: Path) -> dict:
    odds, rejected = load_odds(odds_path)
    rows, unmatched, invalid_probs = [], 0, 0
    with predictions_path.open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f:
        predictions = list(csv.DictReader(f))
    for row in predictions:
        key = tuple((row.get(k) or "").strip() for k in
                    ("date", "league_code", "home_team", "away_team"))
        if key not in odds:
            unmatched += 1
            continue
        actual = (row.get("actual_1x2") or "").strip()
        try:
            model_probs = [float(row[MODEL_PREFIX + suffix]) for suffix in ("home", "draw", "away")]
            # Reuse the same probability validity rules as the scoring functions.
            model_brier = brier(model_probs, actual)
            model_ll = log_loss(model_probs, actual)
        except (KeyError, ValueError, TypeError):
            invalid_probs += 1
            continue
        market_row = odds[key]
        market_probs = market_row["market"]
        market_brier = brier(market_probs, actual)
        market_ll = log_loss(market_probs, actual)
        result = {"date": key[0], "league_code": key[1], "home_team": key[2], "away_team": key[3],
                  "actual_1x2": actual, "odds_captured_at_utc": market_row["captured_at_utc"],
                  "kickoff_at_utc": market_row["kickoff_at_utc"],
                  "home_odds": market_row["odds"][0], "draw_odds": market_row["odds"][1],
                  "away_odds": market_row["odds"][2], "market_overround": round(market_row["overround"], 6),
                  "model_brier": round(model_brier, 6), "market_brier": round(market_brier, 6),
                  "model_log_loss": round(model_ll, 6), "market_log_loss": round(market_ll, 6),
                  "brier_difference_model_minus_market": round(model_brier - market_brier, 6),
                  "log_loss_difference_model_minus_market": round(model_ll - market_ll, 6)}
        for i, label in enumerate(("home", "draw", "away")):
            result[f"model_p_{label}"] = round(model_probs[i], 6)
            result[f"market_p_{label}_no_vig"] = round(market_probs[i], 6)
            result[f"probability_gap_{label}"] = round(model_probs[i] - market_probs[i], 6)
        rows.append(result)
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    output_csv = out_dir / "probability_market_audit.csv"
    columns = list(rows[0]) if rows else ["date", "league_code", "home_team", "away_team",
        "actual_1x2", "model_brier", "market_brier", "model_log_loss", "market_log_loss"]
    with output_csv.open("w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=columns)
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)
    n = len(rows)
    summary = {
        "purpose": "retrospective probability-quality audit; not a betting recommendation",
        "matched_scored_fixtures": n,
        "prediction_rows_without_valid_odds_match": unmatched,
        "invalid_prediction_rows": invalid_probs,
        "odds_rejections": rejected,
        "mean_model_brier": round(sum(r["model_brier"] for r in rows) / n, 6) if n else None,
        "mean_market_brier": round(sum(r["market_brier"] for r in rows) / n, 6) if n else None,
        "mean_model_log_loss": round(sum(r["model_log_loss"] for r in rows) / n, 6) if n else None,
        "mean_market_log_loss": round(sum(r["market_log_loss"] for r in rows) / n, 6) if n else None,
        "mean_brier_difference_model_minus_market": round(sum(r["brier_difference_model_minus_market"] for r in rows) / n, 6) if n else None,
        "mean_log_loss_difference_model_minus_market": round(sum(r["log_loss_difference_model_minus_market"] for r in rows) / n, 6) if n else None,
        "interpretation": "Negative model-minus-market differences indicate lower average forecast loss on this matched historical sample only; they do not establish future profitability.",
        "output_csv": str(output_csv),
        "limitations": [
            "Only matched, scored historical fixtures with valid pre-kickoff odds are compared.",
            "Margin-normalized odds are a market benchmark, not objective true probabilities.",
            "Do not infer live performance from retrospective results; use multiple chronological periods and enough fixtures.",
            "This audit reports probability gaps and forecast scores, not recommended selections or profit estimates."
        ]
    }
    (out_dir / "probability_market_audit.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    return summary


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--predictions", default="output/openfootball/integrated/advanced/advanced_predictions.csv")
    parser.add_argument("--odds-csv", default="data/private/pre_match_odds.csv")
    parser.add_argument("--out", default="output/openfootball/integrated/market_audit")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    summary = run_audit(Path(args.predictions), Path(args.odds_csv), Path(args.out))
    print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))
    if summary["matched_scored_fixtures"] == 0:
        raise SystemExit("No matched historical fixtures with valid pre-kickoff odds; audit produced empty outputs.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Executable ditch audit.
 * Uses the copied UFI calculator + Crainee engines, then Dixon-Coles
 * (rho=-0.10) fit to a 1X2. Browser IIFEs in github-app/ stay the spec;
 * this runner does not replace them.
 *
 * Usage: node references/runner/ditch-audit.mjs
 */
import fs from 'node:fs';
import vm from 'node:vm';
import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);
const here = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const app = path.resolve(here, '../github-app');

function loadBrowser(file, exportName) {
  const ctx = {
    window: {},
    document: { addEventListener() {}, getElementById() { return null; }, querySelector() { return null; } },
    console,
    localStorage: { getItem() { return null; }, setItem() {} },
  };
  vm.createContext(ctx);
  vm.runInContext(fs.readFileSync(path.join(app, file), 'utf8'), ctx, { filename: file });
  return ctx.window[exportName];
}

const calc = loadBrowser('calculator-risk-engine.js', 'UFICalculatorRisk');
const chaos = require(path.join(app, 'crainee-chaos-gate.js'));
const tactical = require(path.join(app, 'crainee-tactical-surprise-engine.js'));

function pois(k, l) {
  let p = Math.exp(-l);
  for (let i = 1; i <= k; i++) p *= l / i;
  return p;
}

export function fitDixonColes(oh, od, oa, rho = -0.1) {
  const ih = 1 / oh, id = 1 / od, ia = 1 / oa, s = ih + id + ia;
  const th = ih / s, td = id / s, ta = ia / s;
  let best = null;
  for (let lh = 0.2; lh <= 4.6; lh += 0.05) {
    for (let la = 0.15; la <= 3.2; la += 0.05) {
      const tau = (i, j) => {
        if (i === 0 && j === 0) return Math.max(0, 1 - lh * la * rho);
        if (i === 0 && j === 1) return Math.max(0, 1 + lh * rho);
        if (i === 1 && j === 0) return Math.max(0, 1 + la * rho);
        if (i === 1 && j === 1) return Math.max(0, 1 - rho);
        return 1;
      };
      let H = 0, D = 0, A = 0, mass = 0;
      const cells = [];
      for (let i = 0; i <= 10; i++) for (let j = 0; j <= 10; j++) {
        const p = tau(i, j) * pois(i, lh) * pois(j, la);
        cells.push([i, j, p]);
        mass += p;
      }
      for (const [i, j, p0] of cells) {
        const p = p0 / mass;
        if (i > j) H += p; else if (i === j) D += p; else A += p;
      }
      const err = (H - th) ** 2 + 3.2 * (D - td) ** 2 + (A - ta) ** 2;
      if (!best || err < best.err) best = { lh, la, H, D, A, err };
    }
  }
  return { ...best, market: { home: th, draw: td, away: ta } };
}

const loaded = {
  calculator: calc?.version || null,
  chaos: Object.keys(chaos),
  tactical: Object.keys(tactical),
  githubFiles: fs.readFileSync(path.resolve(here, '../github-app.FILELIST.txt'), 'utf8').trim().split('\n').length,
};
if (process.argv[1] && process.argv[1].endsWith('ditch-audit.mjs')) {
  const sample = fitDixonColes(1.25, 7.5, 9.5);
  const slip = calc.evaluateSlip([{ label: 'sample', odd: 1.7, source: 'model' }], 150);
  console.log(JSON.stringify({ loaded, sample: { lh: sample.lh, la: sample.la, draw: sample.D }, slipCombined: slip.combinedOdds, registry: slip.registry.total }, null, 2));
}
